# Roblox DevForum: Studio & Scripting

All Roblox DevForum tips on this theme, grouped by topic, oldest first. Cross-creator synthesis: see [[README]] in roblox-kb.

## [[scripting-and-performance]]
- 2015-01-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-cframes/11743) Use CFrame:components() to read raw values when doing custom math; it is faster than repeated CFrame operations in hot code. ([[2015-01-03 - How to think about CFrames|How to think about CFrames]])
- 2015-01-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-cframes/11743) Remember the Right, Top, Back vectors are directions (unit length), not face-center positions. ([[2015-01-03 - How to think about CFrames|How to think about CFrames]])
- 2016-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017) Use Terrain:FillBlock(part.CFrame, part.Size, material) in the command bar to fill a part's volume with smooth terrain. ([[2016-04-08 - Smooth Terrain How To's and Tips and Tricks|Smooth Terrain: How To's and Tips and Tricks]])
- 2016-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017) Use Terrain:FillBall(position, radius, material) to create spherical terrain; the docs example uses part.Size.Y/2 as radius. ([[2016-04-08 - Smooth Terrain How To's and Tips and Tricks|Smooth Terrain: How To's and Tips and Tricks]])
- 2016-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017) Use Terrain:FillRegion(region:ExpandToGrid(4), 4, material) to fill a Region3; ExpandToGrid aligns it to the 4-stud voxel grid. ([[2016-04-08 - Smooth Terrain How To's and Tips and Tricks|Smooth Terrain: How To's and Tips and Tricks]])
- 2016-07-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756) Profile before and after optimizing; the author warns some changes may have no effect, since the newer Luau VM already handles some optimizations. ([[2016-07-05 - The Basics Of Basic Optimization|The Basics Of Basic Optimization]])
- 2016-07-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756) Precompute constant values like CFrame.new(0,1,0) outside loops instead of recreating them every iteration. ([[2016-07-05 - The Basics Of Basic Optimization|The Basics Of Basic Optimization]])
- 2016-07-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756) Cache indexed methods and constructors (e.g. Workspace.FindPartOnRay, Vector3.new) in locals to reduce repeated __index calls inside loops. ([[2016-07-05 - The Basics Of Basic Optimization|The Basics Of Basic Optimization]])
- 2016-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-method-hooking-and-keeping-your-game-protected/31442) Place main code in ModuleScripts, require them, then delete the modules; code keeps running while the requiring script exists. Obfuscation adds further protection but is not absolute. ([[2016-12-09 - Understanding method hooking and keeping your game protected|Understanding method hooking and keeping your game]])
- 2017-10-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-scripting-bots-javascript-tutorial-funcaptcha-and-new-host-info/58201) Use a unique password for the bot's Roblox account, separate from your main account. ([[2017-10-25 - Guide to Scripting Bots Javascript Tutorial FunCaptcha and New Host In|Guide to Scripting Bots | Javascript Tutorial | Fu]])
- 2018-02-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082) Call :Destroy() on Instances you no longer need; Destroy recursively breaks their event connections so they can be garbage collected. ([[2018-02-08 - PSA Connections can memory leak Instances!|PSA: Connections can memory leak Instances!]])
- 2018-02-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082) Store connection objects and call :Disconnect() on them when done; a connection that is never disconnected keeps the closure and its upvalues alive. ([[2018-02-08 - PSA Connections can memory leak Instances!|PSA: Connections can memory leak Instances!]])
- 2018-02-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082) Prioritise leak checks on the server, since long-running server processes accumulate leaks while short client sessions rarely hit a problematic level. ([[2018-02-08 - PSA Connections can memory leak Instances!|PSA: Connections can memory leak Instances!]])
- 2018-07-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-quaternions/143295) When blending rotations, pass unnormalized component weights to CFrame.new(0,0,0,X,Y,Z,W); it normalizes them onto the 4D unit sphere for you. ([[2018-07-05 - How to think about Quaternions|How to think about Quaternions]])
- 2018-07-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-quaternions/143295) For custom slerp, choose the twin with positive dot product between start and end quaternions so the path is the shortest rotation. ([[2018-07-05 - How to think about Quaternions|How to think about Quaternions]])
- 2018-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434) Compute aim-down-sights offset as Handle.CFrame:inverse() * Aim.CFrame and set joint C1 by lerping, so the camera centres on the sight rather than the handle. ([[2018-08-06 - The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter|The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter]])
- 2018-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434) Update arm shoulder C1 values each frame in RenderStepped so arms stay attached to the weapon while aiming down sights. ([[2018-08-06 - The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter|The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter]])
- 2018-09-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/modeling-a-projectiles-motion/176677) Use x(t) = 0.5*g*t^2 + v0*t + x0 to compute launch velocity v0 = (target - x0 - 0.5*g*t^2)/t for a chosen flight time t. ([[2018-09-07 - Modeling a projectile's motion|Modeling a projectile's motion]])
- 2018-11-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-furniture-placement-system/205509) Use RunService:IsServer() inside a shared class so one module handles both server and client logic without duplicated code. ([[2018-11-26 - Creating A Furniture Placement System|Creating A Furniture Placement System]])
- 2018-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-prevent-speed-hacking/210733) Run the position check as a server Script in the character's StarterCharacterScripts loop with wait(checktime), storing the last CFrame each tick. ([[2018-12-12 - How to Prevent Speed Hacking|How to Prevent Speed Hacking]])
- 2019-03-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754) Refresh leaderboard fetches on a long interval (tutorial uses 120 seconds) to limit DataStore and thumbnail request load. ([[2019-03-23 - How to make a simple global leaderboard|How to make a simple global leaderboard]])
- 2019-07-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253) Tween the root's CFrame, not its Position; welds only propagate CFrame changes to other parts. ([[2019-07-24 - Introduction to Tweening Models|Introduction to Tweening Models]])
- 2019-07-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253) Use TweenService directly for model tweens; avoid CFrameValue proxies with SetPrimaryPartCFrame, which add overhead and fail without a PrimaryPart. ([[2019-07-24 - Introduction to Tweening Models|Introduction to Tweening Models]])
- 2019-07-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253) Avoid SetPrimaryPartCFrame for repeated moves: floating-point offset errors accumulate and tear models apart over time (deprecated; use Pivot API). ([[2019-07-24 - Introduction to Tweening Models|Introduction to Tweening Models]])
- 2019-08-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-couple-of-advanced-cframe-tricks/337682) Use the quaternion trick for rotation between vectors: add [1+dot, cross] and let the CFrame constructor normalize; cheaper and more robust than acos/fromAxisAngle. ([[2019-08-24 - A Couple of Advanced CFrame Tricks|A Couple of Advanced CFrame Tricks]])
- 2019-08-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-couple-of-advanced-cframe-tricks/337682) Guard the opposite-vector case (dot < -0.99999) separately with a backup axis; the quaternion sum is zero there and cannot be normalized. ([[2019-08-24 - A Couple of Advanced CFrame Tricks|A Couple of Advanced CFrame Tricks]])
- 2019-08-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-couple-of-advanced-cframe-tricks/337682) Measure spin around an axis with swing-twist decomposition on the delta CFrame rather than the part's delta CFrame; this avoids errors from wobbling parts. ([[2019-08-24 - A Couple of Advanced CFrame Tricks|A Couple of Advanced CFrame Tricks]])
- 2019-08-28 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-a-maid-class-on-roblox-to-manage-state/340061) Disconnect signals and destroy objects when state changes or objects are removed; a Maid centralises this cleanup so nothing is forgotten. ([[2019-08-28 - How to use a Maid class on Roblox to manage state|How to use a Maid class on Roblox to manage state]])
- 2019-09-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-requests-simple-powerful-http-in-roblox/355294) Use configurable rate limiting to balance your game's HTTP usage so requests stay within Roblox and third-party API limits. ([[2019-09-22 - Roblox Requests Simple, powerful HTTP in Roblox|Roblox Requests: Simple, powerful HTTP in Roblox]])
- 2019-09-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-requests-simple-powerful-http-in-roblox/355294) Enable local or global response caching to avoid repeating identical HTTP requests. ([[2019-09-22 - Roblox Requests Simple, powerful HTTP in Roblox|Roblox Requests: Simple, powerful HTTP in Roblox]])
- 2019-09-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484) Set RequireHandles to OFF on the Tool, otherwise the custom grip setup will not work in game. ([[2019-09-30 - How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)|How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)]])
- 2019-10-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954) Disconnect event connections and destroy instances when done; a connected function keeps every upvalue it references alive indefinitely. ([[2019-10-24 - Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - What you should know|Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - Wh]])
- 2019-10-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954) Set local variables to nil when finished with them; a referenced table is never collected while any strong reference remains. ([[2019-10-24 - Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - What you should know|Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - Wh]])
- 2019-10-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954) Wrap temporary values in do blocks so they go out of scope and become collectable without waiting for the thread to end. ([[2019-10-24 - Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - What you should know|Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - Wh]])
- 2019-10-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954) Use setmetatable with __mode set to "k", "v", or "kv" for caches so entries can be garbage collected automatically. ([[2019-10-24 - Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - What you should know|Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - Wh]])
- 2019-10-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954) Clear or weak-reference tables you still hold but no longer use; a table keeps its values alive until the table itself is collected. ([[2019-10-24 - Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - What you should know|Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - Wh]])
- 2019-10-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954) Clean up callbacks like Bindable/RemoteFunction OnInvoke handlers, since they hold references to captured variables just like connections. ([[2019-10-24 - Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - What you should know|Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - Wh]])
- 2019-10-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954) Clean up never-ending threads (e.g. coroutine.yield loops) by scoping temporaries in do blocks or clearing them, because such threads never collect their root values. ([[2019-10-24 - Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - What you should know|Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - Wh]])
- 2019-11-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801) Before blaming Humanoid, analyze your own NPC code and ask Scripting Support to review it; the author says it usually can be improved for significant gains. ([[2019-11-17 - How to Optimize Humanoids|How to Optimize Humanoids]])
- 2019-11-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792) For constantly changing UI like inventories or player lists, wrap the CanvasSize update in a function and call it whenever content changes. ([[2019-11-22 - Quick Tutorial How to make scrolling frames properly|Quick Tutorial: How to make scrolling frames prope]])
- 2019-11-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcalls-when-and-how-to-use-them/393687) Pass the method as pcall(DataStore.GetAsync, DataStore, "key") rather than wrapping in an anonymous function; avoids creating a closure each call. ([[2019-11-23 - Pcalls - When and how to use them|Pcalls - When and how to use them]])
- 2019-11-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcalls-when-and-how-to-use-them/393687) Pcall StarterGui:SetCore in a retry loop with wait(1), since SetCores aren't registered until CoreScripts initialise. ([[2019-11-23 - Pcalls - When and how to use them|Pcalls - When and how to use them]])
- 2019-11-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcalls-when-and-how-to-use-them/393687) Do not use ypcall; pcall can yield in modern Luau, so ypcall gives no benefit. ([[2019-11-23 - Pcalls - When and how to use them|Pcalls - When and how to use them]])
- 2019-11-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcalls-when-and-how-to-use-them/393687) Do not yield (wait) inside xpcall; it cannot yield. Use xpcall with debug.traceback as handler to get stack traces on errors. ([[2019-11-23 - Pcalls - When and how to use them|Pcalls - When and how to use them]])
- 2019-11-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618) Learn Luau fundamentals (print, variables, functions, loops, tables) before tackling advanced game systems; the guide says it provides the basics needed to follow advanced tutorials. ([[2019-11-24 - Lua Scripting Starter Guide|Lua Scripting Starter Guide]])
- 2019-12-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dragging-objects-with-the-mouse/403766) Set mouse.TargetFilter to the dragged target so mouse.Hit ignores it, and only move the part while Button1 is held. ([[2019-12-08 - Dragging objects with the mouse|Dragging objects with the mouse]])
- 2019-12-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672) Run the terrain conversion script in the Studio command bar, not in Test Mode, or the terrain may not save. ([[2019-12-11 - Large-Scale Roblox Terrain The ultimate guide|Large-Scale Roblox Terrain: The ultimate guide]])
- 2019-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-circularradial-hp-bar-like-kingdom-hearts/420996) Put the bar math in a function (CurvedBarResize) and reuse it per segment instead of duplicating code for each corner. ([[2019-12-31 - Creating A CircularRadial HP Bar Like Kingdom Hearts|Creating A Circular/Radial HP Bar Like Kingdom Hea]])
- 2020-01-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replicating-instances-to-only-some-players/438374) Replicate instances to only the players who need them (e.g. a player's interior) instead of ReplicatedStorage, to save bandwidth, CPU and memory, especially on mobile. ([[2020-01-20 - Replicating Instances to Only Some Players|Replicating Instances to Only Some Players]])
- 2020-01-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replicating-instances-to-only-some-players/438374) Store instances as table keys to prevent garbage collection while they are held in a pending state, rather than parenting them early. ([[2020-01-20 - Replicating Instances to Only Some Players|Replicating Instances to Only Some Players]])
- 2020-02-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/consume-everything-how-greedy-meshing-works/452717) Extend greedy meshing to 2D by expanding the 1D run across the other axis while every block in that direction can be included, then emit one cuboid per region. ([[2020-02-07 - Consume everything - how greedy meshing works|Consume everything - how greedy meshing works]])
- 2020-02-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/consume-everything-how-greedy-meshing-works/452717) Mark blocks as visited so each block is included in exactly one cuboid; repeat for every remaining block. Extending the same idea to 3D follows the 2D pattern. ([[2020-02-07 - Consume everything - how greedy meshing works|Consume everything - how greedy meshing works]])
- 2020-02-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584) Clamp health with math.clamp between 0 and MaxHealth so values cannot go negative or exceed max after heal/attack. ([[2020-02-14 - Creating a battle system OOP style!|Creating a battle system: OOP style!]])
- 2020-02-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584) Disconnect event connections and destroy the battle area when a battle ends to avoid leaked connections. ([[2020-02-14 - Creating a battle system OOP style!|Creating a battle system: OOP style!]])
- 2020-02-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/about-our-upcoming-global-compliance-system/461447) Use GetPolicyInfoForPlayerAsync to check a player's region policy info so game features can comply with local rules. ([[2020-02-20 - About our upcoming Global Compliance System|About our upcoming Global Compliance System]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318) Multiply procedural movement by deltaTime (e.g. *deltaTime*60) so FPS-unlocked clients (100-300 FPS) don't run animations faster. ([[2020-03-31 - Writing an FPS framework (2020)|Writing an FPS framework (2020)]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318) Use TweenService for aim in/out transitions and lerp between idle and aim CFrame offsets via a NumberValue. ([[2020-03-31 - Writing an FPS framework (2020)|Writing an FPS framework (2020)]])
- 2020-04-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/coroutines-when-and-how-to-use-them/541683) Errors inside a coroutine do not crash the main thread; check coroutine.resume's returned success value to handle failures. ([[2020-04-25 - Coroutines - When and how to use them|Coroutines - When and how to use them]])
- 2020-05-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basic-trigonometry/554329) Roblox math.sin/cos/tan take radians; convert degrees with math.rad(). Inverse functions return radians; convert with math.deg(). ([[2020-05-02 - Basic Trigonometry|Basic Trigonometry]])
- 2020-05-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basic-trigonometry/554329) Use Vector3:Dot(other) divided by magnitudes to get the cosine of the angle between two vectors (|a||b|cos(Θ)). ([[2020-05-02 - Basic Trigonometry|Basic Trigonometry]])
- 2020-05-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basic-trigonometry/554329) Law of Cosines only applies when the known angle sits between the two known sides; for SAS, compute the missing side first, then Law of Sines. ([[2020-05-02 - Basic Trigonometry|Basic Trigonometry]])
- 2020-05-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basic-trigonometry/554329) In SSA/Law of Sines cases, solve for the angle opposite the smaller known side first to avoid picking the wrong angle. ([[2020-05-02 - Basic Trigonometry|Basic Trigonometry]])
- 2020-05-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587) Route external HTTP calls through a queue instead of raw HttpService so rate-limited (429) requests are retried automatically rather than lost. ([[2020-05-03 - HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on the rest!|HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on t]])
- 2020-05-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587) Set maxSimultaneousSendOperations no higher than the service's rate limit, per the author's guidance. ([[2020-05-03 - HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on the rest!|HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on t]])
- 2020-05-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587) Use retryAfter cooldown matching the service's rate-limit window (example: Trello 10 requests/10 seconds, cooldown 10). ([[2020-05-03 - HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on the rest!|HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on t]])
- 2020-05-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587) Use HttpRequestPriority.Prioritary or First sparingly; default first-come-first-serve covers most cases. ([[2020-05-03 - HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on the rest!|HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on t]])
- 2020-05-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/httpqueue-forget-about-rate-limiting-focus-on-the-rest/555587) Append a trailing slash to URLs when using query options; library normalizes this from v1.0.0 onward. ([[2020-05-03 - HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on the rest!|HttpQueue - Forget about rate limiting, focus on t]])
- 2020-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782) Tween a CFrameValue and apply its Changed value to the joint's C0; TweenService can only tween values like CFrameValue, not CFrame properties directly. ([[2020-05-08 - Procedural Animation What it is and how to do it|Procedural Animation: What it is and how to do it]])
- 2020-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782) Save a base C0 (savedBaseCFrame) at start and multiply offsets by it instead of using the current CFrame to avoid odd drift. ([[2020-05-08 - Procedural Animation What it is and how to do it|Procedural Animation: What it is and how to do it]])
- 2020-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782) Reuse one TweenBase by cancelling it, editing goal.Value, and recreating the tween per keyframe rather than creating many tweens. ([[2020-05-08 - Procedural Animation What it is and how to do it|Procedural Animation: What it is and how to do it]])
- 2020-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782) Use C0 of a Motor6D so procedural offsets can run alongside regular animation tracks, enabling augmentation such as character tilt. ([[2020-05-08 - Procedural Animation What it is and how to do it|Procedural Animation: What it is and how to do it]])
- 2020-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782) CFrame.Angles takes radians; wrap degree values in math.rad(). ([[2020-05-08 - Procedural Animation What it is and how to do it|Procedural Animation: What it is and how to do it]])
- 2020-05-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/perspective-object-dragging-like-superliminal/566200) Anchor picked-up parts and zero Velocity/RotVelocity before dragging; an anchored part with velocity still acts as a conveyor. ([[2020-05-09 - Perspective object dragging like Superliminal|Perspective object dragging like Superliminal]])
- 2020-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-part-2-2020/581791) Keep projectiles client-side for responsiveness and replicate them, rather than server-only bullets which freeze and cost more to create. ([[2020-05-18 - Writing an FPS framework, part 2 (2020)|Writing an FPS framework, part 2 (2020)]])
- 2020-06-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401) Avoid plain os.time() for cross-server sync; author reported servers drifting 3-5 minutes apart. Fetch time externally via the Google HTTP Date header. ([[2020-06-18 - How to Make Server-Synced Daily Shops|How to Make Server-Synced Daily Shops]])
- 2020-06-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401) Seed Random.new() with the day number so every server generates the same shop items without communicating. ([[2020-06-18 - How to Make Server-Synced Daily Shops|How to Make Server-Synced Daily Shops]])
- 2020-06-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401) Floor the day value so the seed only changes when a full day boundary passes. ([[2020-06-18 - How to Make Server-Synced Daily Shops|How to Make Server-Synced Daily Shops]])
- 2020-06-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943) Use TweenInfo reverse and repeat flags instead of separate return tweens; this cut the bobbing script from ~156 lines to ~87. - 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321) Sync Humanoid enabled states via attributes set every frame in BindToSimulation, since Humanoid:SetStateEnabled does not replicate. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engineers!|Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engin]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321) Always nil-check the instance before RunService:SetPredictionMode; passing nil currently crashes. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engineers!|Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engin]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) Don't replicate large NPC counts as moving Humanoids; use a custom system where the server sends position data and clients render it, to offload replication cost. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) Use flowfield pathfinding (e.g. FastFlow) for hordes: one calculated field is shared by all NPCs and only recalculated when targets or the environment change. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2026-01-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561) Search codebase (Ctrl+Shift+F) for Motor6D references; C0/C1/Part0/Part1 setters will error on AnimationConstraints, so switch to Attachments. ([[2026-01-26 - Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Character Movement is No|Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Char]])
- 2026-01-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561) Scripts using instance:IsA("Motor6D") will not detect AnimationConstraints; update those checks. ([[2026-01-26 - Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Character Movement is No|Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Char]])
- 2026-01-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/completing-the-dynamic-head-migration/4301387) Call AvatarEditorService:ConformToAvatarRulesAsync() before presenting an avatar so legacy Classic assets are swapped for their Dynamic Head equivalents with the correct HeadShape. ([[2026-01-27 - Completing the Dynamic Head Migration|Completing the Dynamic Head Migration]])
- 2026-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904) Keep the AbilityManagerActor folder intact with both ServerInitialize and ClientInitialize scripts when moving scripts into your experience. ([[2026-02-18 - Studio Beta AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-Based Character Control|[Studio Beta] AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-B]])
- 2026-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904) Update to the latest library version (restart Studio to force update) to get the RemoteEvent Player argument verification fix. ([[2026-02-18 - Studio Beta AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-Based Character Control|[Studio Beta] AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-B]])
- 2026-04-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788) Set graph parameters from Lua with track:SetParameter("Speed", value) instead of manually blending tracks in code. ([[2026-04-02 - Studio Beta Introducing the Animation Graph System|[Studio Beta] Introducing the Animation Graph Syst]])
- 2026-04-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788) Existing LoadAnimation scripts keep working; adopt the graph only where complex blending or sequencing justifies it. ([[2026-04-02 - Studio Beta Introducing the Animation Graph System|[Studio Beta] Introducing the Animation Graph Syst]])
- 2026-04-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575) Use RaycastParams.ExcludeInstances/IncludeInstances instead of FilterDescendantsInstances; new APIs are much faster, especially with large filter lists. ([[2026-04-07 - New spatial query filters|New spatial query filters]])
- 2026-04-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575) Set IncludeInstances = nil for most permissive filter and {} for include-nothing; they are opposites, don't confuse them. ([[2026-04-07 - New spatial query filters|New spatial query filters]])
- 2026-04-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575) If an instance is in both exclude and include lists, exclusion wins; design lists so they don't overlap unintentionally. ([[2026-04-07 - New spatial query filters|New spatial query filters]])
- 2026-04-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575) Repeatedly updating filter lists (e.g., adding parts dynamically) is cheap in the new APIs thanks to tuned reflection code. ([[2026-04-07 - New spatial query filters|New spatial query filters]])
- 2026-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267) Expect performance from equal to 2x faster than Humanoid on server and client depending on in-game circumstances; benchmark your own scene. ([[2026-04-08 - Full Release The Future of Character Movement Character Controller Lib|[Full Release] The Future of Character Movement: C]])
- 2026-04-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056) Call parameters:Pause() inside long generation loops to spread work across frames and avoid dropping frames. ([[2026-04-23 - Studio Beta Introducing Procedural Models Build Parametrized 3D Models|[Studio Beta] Introducing Procedural Models: Build]])
- 2026-05-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/protect-your-games-with-script-capabilities-sandboxing/4634642) Avoid require(id), LoadAsset, LoadString and getfenv/setfenv in Store assets; these are blocked in sandboxed assets and prohibited by Store policy. ([[2026-05-13 - Protect Your Games with Script Capabilities Sandboxing|Protect Your Games with Script Capabilities Sandbo]])
- 2026-06-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/automated-performance-profiling-with-microprofiler-and-assistant/4704417) For advanced profiling, use the MicroProfiler API (LibMP) to programmatically access all MicroProfiler data. ([[2026-06-26 - Automated Performance Profiling with MicroProfiler and Assistant|Automated Performance Profiling with MicroProfiler]])
- 2026-07-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993) Enable Server Authority via Workspace Properties AuthorityMode=Server; it auto-sets NextGenerationReplication, PlayerScriptsUseInputActionSystem, SignalBehavior=Deferred, StreamingEnabled and UseFixedSimulation. ([[2026-07-09 - Full Release Ship Fair And Competitive Games with Server Authority|[Full Release] Ship Fair And Competitive Games wit]])
- 2026-07-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905) Serialise Vector3, CFrame and Color3 as packed buffers (12, 13 and 4 bytes) rather than JSON tables (CFrame about 200 bytes as JSON). ([[2026-07-15 - Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated player data built o|Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated]])
- 2026-07-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/reference-instances-directly-with-attributes/4753441) Replace ObjectValue children with attributes that hold Instance references directly via SetAttribute(name, instance), removing extra instances to manage. ([[2026-07-23 - Reference Instances Directly with Attributes|Reference Instances Directly with Attributes]])
- 2026-08-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143) Scope collections to a small root instance via CreateCollection's rootInstance argument; workspace-wide queries force tracking of many instances and cost more. ([[2026-08-13 - Collections How to group instances with query patterns|Collections: How to group instances with query pat]])
- 2026-08-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143) Include a class specifier (Part#Killbrick, not #Killbrick) so the engine ignores non-matching classes and only monitors relevant instances for name changes. ([[2026-08-13 - Collections How to group instances with query patterns|Collections: How to group instances with query pat]])
- 2026-08-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143) Avoid combinators (>, >>, has, in-radius) where possible; they add significant tracking overhead, with has() and in-radius() called out as expensive. ([[2026-08-13 - Collections How to group instances with query patterns|Collections: How to group instances with query pat]])
- 2026-08-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143) Tag and attribute selectors do not speed up queries and slightly slow them, since extra listeners are needed to detect tag and attribute changes. ([[2026-08-13 - Collections How to group instances with query patterns|Collections: How to group instances with query pat]])
- 2026-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568) Don't call BindToCollisionSummaries from inside a Touched handler; it is a separate API for collision information. ([[2026-10-06 - Collision Summaries API Detailed Collision Data From The Engine|Collision Summaries API: Detailed Collision Data F]])
- 2026-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568) Subscribe only the parts you need; processing overhead scales with subscribed parts, not total scene collisions. ([[2026-10-06 - Collision Summaries API Detailed Collision Data From The Engine|Collision Summaries API: Detailed Collision Data F]])
- 2026-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568) Dot RelativeVelocity with Normal to get head-on impact speed; use the tangential component to detect glancing hits and sliding. ([[2026-10-06 - Collision Summaries API Detailed Collision Data From The Engine|Collision Summaries API: Detailed Collision Data F]])
- 2026-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568) Parts with CanCollide = false produce no summaries; sleeping-vs-sleeping contacts are not recorded. ([[2026-10-06 - Collision Summaries API Detailed Collision Data From The Engine|Collision Summaries API: Detailed Collision Data F]])
- 2026-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568) Disconnect the returned RBXScriptConnection when finished with the subscription. ([[2026-10-06 - Collision Summaries API Detailed Collision Data From The Engine|Collision Summaries API: Detailed Collision Data F]])

## [[building-art-and-assets]]
- 2015-09-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-slicecenter-robloxs-9-slice-gui-property/18313) Design the template so corners stay fixed, side edges stretch in one direction, and the center stretches; example: 48x48 image with 6px borders gives a 36x36 center. ([[2015-09-07 - How to use SliceCenter (Roblox's 9-Slice GUI Property)|How to use SliceCenter (Roblox's 9-Slice GUI Prope]])
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) Learn building plugins and their keyboard shortcuts (Build v4, F3X, qCmd Ult); the author says most builders only got good after using plugins. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) Study other showcases closely for detail rather than skimming for two minutes; use Google images for inspiration. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) Build on a fixed grid (e.g. 0.2 or 0.25 studs) and never switch increments mid-build to avoid Z-fighting and messy flow. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) When adding detail on a 0.2 grid, offset parts by 0.04 studs (0.05 on a 0.25 grid) instead of leaving gaps. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) Combine wedges, corner wedges, cylinders, spheres and blocks instead of only blocks and triangles to make curves, bevels and ramps without overlap. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) Use a triangle terrain tool that does not leave gaps between triangles; Build v4 has one built in. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) Use Resize Align and Gapfill (by stravant) to fix alignment and gaps between parts. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) Add transparency to scorched or ground decals so they blend into the background instead of looking pasted on. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) Revisit old builds that look weak and upgrade them, while keeping the map's building style consistent. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2016-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017) Fix ridges left by the Smooth tool by running Grow or Erode at minimum strength over the affected area; this adds or removes a thin layer and looks cleaner. ([[2016-04-08 - Smooth Terrain How To's and Tips and Tricks|Smooth Terrain: How To's and Tips and Tricks]])
- 2016-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017) Blend two terrain materials by growing the opposite material at low strength on each side of the boundary to create a gradual transition. ([[2016-04-08 - Smooth Terrain How To's and Tips and Tricks|Smooth Terrain: How To's and Tips and Tricks]])
- 2016-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017) Use the Paint tool's plane lock to paint only along one plane, useful for layered material bands such as canyon strata. ([[2016-04-08 - Smooth Terrain How To's and Tips and Tricks|Smooth Terrain: How To's and Tips and Tricks]])
- 2016-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017) Use the Add tool's Snap to Grid and Ignore Water options to align chunks to the voxel grid and avoid overwriting water. ([[2016-04-08 - Smooth Terrain How To's and Tips and Tricks|Smooth Terrain: How To's and Tips and Tricks]])
- 2016-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-method-hooking-and-keeping-your-game-protected/31442) Store models and maps in ServerStorage rather than ReplicatedStorage to reduce asset leaks; clone client-needed assets, delete originals, and hold references to clones. ([[2016-12-09 - Understanding method hooking and keeping your game protected|Understanding method hooking and keeping your game]])
- 2017-03-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729) Humanoid clothing is not auto-imported; export an OBJ from Studio and link meshes to the generated bones manually if needed. ([[2017-03-30 - Blender rig exporteranimation importer|Blender rig exporter/animation importer]])
- 2018-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434) Build the first-person view model as a clone of the character with only arms, upper torso and head kept; set head and torso transparency to 1 and anchor the head so its CFrame can be set directly. ([[2018-08-06 - The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter|The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter]])
- 2018-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434) Add invisible reference parts (Handle, Right, Left, Aim) with consistent front/up faces on weapon models so offsets and hand placement stay predictable across weapons. ([[2018-08-06 - The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter|The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter]])
- 2018-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434) Expect to tune Motor6D C0 per weapon (for example C0 = CFrame.new(1,-1.5,-2)) since weapon size and camera fit vary. ([[2018-08-06 - The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter|The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Start with Roblox's default or old animator, then try Moon Animator or Blender once basic animations like a wave are comfortable. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-a-toolobject-with-a-dummy-in-the-animation-editor/232317) Create a Handle part in the Tool, set Transparency to 1 and CanCollide to false, and place it where the tool should be held from. ([[2019-01-27 - How to animate a toolobject with a Dummy in the Animation Editor|How to animate a tool/object with a Dummy in the A]])
- 2019-01-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-a-toolobject-with-a-dummy-in-the-animation-editor/232317) Make the Handle the primary part; weld all other tool parts to it (not the Handle itself) so the tool doesn't fall apart while animating. ([[2019-01-27 - How to animate a toolobject with a Dummy in the Animation Editor|How to animate a tool/object with a Dummy in the A]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) Don't put textures/decals on every side of a part; only on faces players see. A brick with textures on all 6 sides is roughly 7x more expensive to draw than a plain material brick. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) Use materials instead of textures where possible, but use Neon and Glass sparingly; they are the most costly materials. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) Compare Parts, Unions and MeshParts with the Performance Analyser; Meshes are typically most efficient per unit of detail, Unions medium, welded/squished Parts worst. Union when short on time, but not habitually. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) Terrain has little memory impact, so use a variety of terrain materials freely; water has a slight cost but is fine to use. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) Avoid many parts with varying transparency. Use transparency 0 or 1 and merge several glass parts into one part to reduce render work. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-03-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/2-joint-2-limb-inverse-kinematics/252399) Apply solved angles either as Motor6D/Weld C0 offsets or by positioning limb parts directly from the base plane CFrame. ([[2019-03-15 - 2 Joint 2 Limb Inverse Kinematics|2 Joint 2 Limb Inverse Kinematics]])
- 2019-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785) Export each UI element as a separate image and colour the symbol assets white so they can be tinted in Studio. ([[2019-05-19 - How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfaces|How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfa]])
- 2019-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785) Run exported images through Pixelfix to remove dark edges caused by transparent pixels when images are scaled down. ([[2019-05-19 - How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfaces|How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfa]])
- 2019-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785) Upload reusable UI assets via the Roblox website for use across games, and avoid duplicate uploads to keep the inventory uncluttered. ([[2019-05-19 - How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfaces|How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfa]])
- 2019-06-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-game/287493) Give builds a written story or lore first; storytelling-driven builds (e.g. After The Flash) were cited as captivating players. ([[2019-06-02 - How to make a game|How to make a game]])
- 2019-07-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253) Verify root orientation before rigging: green (top) arrow must point up; red (right) and blue (front) define axes. Use Handles in the command bar to check. ([[2019-07-24 - Introduction to Tweening Models|Introduction to Tweening Models]])
- 2019-07-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253) Weld parts in Studio rather than live servers to avoid slightly displaced parts in running servers. ([[2019-07-24 - Introduction to Tweening Models|Introduction to Tweening Models]])
- 2019-07-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253) Prefer WeldConstraint over Weld: it needs no C0/C1 offsets and welds relative position automatically. Use Motor6D only for animation joints. ([[2019-07-24 - Introduction to Tweening Models|Introduction to Tweening Models]])
- 2019-09-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484) Group all tool parts under a model with a BodyAttach root part near the trigger/grip; place the model under the animating rig dummy. ([[2019-09-30 - How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)|How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)]])
- 2019-09-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484) Use Constraint Editor plugin: select BodyAttach, Ctrl-click parts; New Motor6D for moving parts (magazine, bolt), New Weld for static parts. Every part needs a weld or Motor6D. ([[2019-09-30 - How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)|How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)]])
- 2019-09-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484) Play tool animations on Equipped and stop on Unequipped using a LocalScript under the tool, with Humanoid:LoadAnimation. ([[2019-09-30 - How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)|How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)]])
- 2019-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258) Pick three colours (base, dark, light) first and reuse them across the whole HUD for visual consistency. ([[2019-10-12 - How to create good User Interface for your games|How to create good User Interface for your games]])
- 2019-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258) Design HUD at 1920x1080 in Photoshop, and convert shapes to smart objects so they stay sharp when scaled down then up. ([[2019-10-12 - How to create good User Interface for your games|How to create good User Interface for your games]])
- 2019-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258) Use clipping masks (Ctrl+Shift+N, 'Use Previous Layer to Create Clipping Mask') to paint textures confined inside a shape. ([[2019-10-12 - How to create good User Interface for your games|How to create good User Interface for your games]])
- 2019-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258) Use reference images (e.g. Google Images or Pinterest searches) when painting textures like wood grain. ([[2019-10-12 - How to create good User Interface for your games|How to create good User Interface for your games]])
- 2019-10-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805) Set Lighting Technology to Shadowmap to get dynamic shadows without baking or scripting. ([[2019-10-13 - How to make your lighting more realistic in shadowmap|How to make your lighting more realistic in shadow]])
- 2019-10-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805) Set ShadowSoftness to 0 for crisper shadows. ([[2019-10-13 - How to make your lighting more realistic in shadowmap|How to make your lighting more realistic in shadow]])
- 2019-10-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805) Use a morning or late-afternoon time of day (e.g. 09:00:00) for warmer, more atmospheric light. ([[2019-10-13 - How to make your lighting more realistic in shadowmap|How to make your lighting more realistic in shadow]])
- 2019-10-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805) Raise Lighting Brightness from the default 2 to 3-5 for most builds; use 6-7 for nature builds. - 2019-10-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805) Set ColorShift_Top to a warm yellow-orange (e.g. 255,138,35) for a morning glow. - 2019-10-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805) Use a cool blue ColorShift_Top (e.g. 44,128,255) for a moon-glow night look; adjust brightness to taste. - 2019-10-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-your-lighting-more-realistic-in-shadowmap/367805) Pair lighting with a skybox that matches the time of day; the author says it noticeably improves realism. - 2019-11-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801) Minimize clothing memory: Roblox builds one clothing texture per humanoid, so keep texture file sizes small, limit item count, and reuse items. ([[2019-11-17 - How to Optimize Humanoids|How to Optimize Humanoids]])

- 2019-12-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672) Use FillWedge in the conversion script for better results than the older method, and back up the Studio file first since crashing is possible. - 2019-12-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672) Heightmap import into Roblox is faster and supports higher resolution than mesh conversion; use it unless you need overhangs or caves. - 2023-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333) Disable CanCollide, CanTouch and CanQuery on small, unreachable, or decorative parts (plants, windows, tools) to reduce physics cost. ([[2023-09-09 - All about optimization|All about optimization]])
- 2023-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333) Disable CastShadow on small or hard-to-see parts such as flowers and windows. ([[2023-09-09 - All about optimization|All about optimization]])
- 2023-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333) Use fewer semi-transparent, Glass, and Neon parts since they need more rendering calculations than opaque plastic. ([[2023-09-09 - All about optimization|All about optimization]])

- 2023-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333) Avoid unnecessary gradients, many open frames and glitter effects, which can reduce performance on low-end devices. ([[2023-09-09 - All about optimization|All about optimization]])

- 2025-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855) Use texture atlases only when textures appear together on batchable parts; one high-res texture in an atlas streams the whole atlas at that mip level. - 2025-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855) Prefer individual unique textures over atlases for non-batchable parts so texture streaming can load mips independently. ([[2025-12-12 - Introducing Texture Streaming|Introducing Texture Streaming]])
- 2025-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855) Keep near-uniform texel density across UV-mapped meshes so the mip selection heuristic produces the best results. ([[2025-12-12 - Introducing Texture Streaming|Introducing Texture Streaming]])

- 2017-03-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729) Use the latest plugin and addon versions; incompatible versions cause errors. - 2022-02-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/my-intro-to-raycasting-what-why-and-how/1681977) Use raycasts for NPC line-of-sight checks, asking whether a player is visible before the NPC reacts. - 2022-03-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/update-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1715717) Use the Audio Discovery plugin in Studio to review ownership of all audio assets in your experience before the March 22 deadline. ([[2022-03-16 - Update Changes to Asset Privacy for Audio|[Update] Changes to Asset Privacy for Audio]])
- 2022-04-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/benis-pathfinding-customizable-easy-to-use-auto-animating-r6-r15-support/1759994) Set animateNPC to true to auto-animate the NPC; works with both R6 and R15 rigs. ([[2022-04-18 - Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & Auto-Animating (R6 &|Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & A]])
- 2023-11-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585) Use the open-source modules BridgeNet2, TopbarPlus, ProfileService and similar rather than rebuilding them; keep only one copy in the project. ([[2023-11-02 - Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide|Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide]])
- 2024-01-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lets-teach-an-ai-to-catch-exploiters/2783460) Use ML only where it fits; for a simple obby exploit check, invisible checkpoints are more practical than an ML model. ([[2024-01-07 - Let's teach an AI to catch exploiters!|Let's teach an AI to catch exploiters!]])
- 2024-01-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lets-teach-an-ai-to-catch-exploiters/2783460) Expect real-world data to outperform synthetic noise; the author's noise-augmented model reached ~85% test accuracy and says player-generated data is much better. ([[2024-01-07 - Let's teach an AI to catch exploiters!|Let's teach an AI to catch exploiters!]])
- 2025-05-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-pathfinding-chase/3648450) Drop the SmoothChase module into ServerScriptService to use it; full docs are in the project's GitHub README. ([[2025-05-13 - Smooth Pathfinding Chase|Smooth Pathfinding Chase]])
- 2025-11-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-reflectionservice-programmatic-access-to-engine-api-information/4078522) Use ReflectionService to build API inspection plugins that analyze new engine API releases, or Studio plugins that change instance classes faster. ([[2025-11-18 - Introducing ReflectionService Programmatic Access to Engine API Inform|Introducing ReflectionService: Programmatic Access]])
- 2026-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904) Force-load autocomplete by running require("@rbx/AvatarAbilities") in the command bar after each Studio reload while the beta is active. ([[2026-02-18 - Studio Beta AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-Based Character Control|[Studio Beta] AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-B]])
- 2026-04-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788) Enable via File > Beta Features > Animation Graph in Studio; open the Clip Editor (Animation Graph Editor) from the Avatar tab on a rig with an Animator. ([[2026-04-02 - Studio Beta Introducing the Animation Graph System|[Studio Beta] Introducing the Animation Graph Syst]])
- 2026-04-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-procedural-models-build-parametrized-3d-models-with-code-or-ai/4592056) Use /generate_procedural_model with a text prompt or reference image in Assistant or Studio MCP; reference images improve accuracy. ([[2026-04-23 - Studio Beta Introducing Procedural Models Build Parametrized 3D Models|[Studio Beta] Introducing Procedural Models: Build]])
- 2026-04-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091) Use the Age Check API: Player:PromptAgeCheck() on a deliberate player action, not on spawn; check Player.AgeChecked. ([[2026-04-30 - Introducing the US 18+ DevEx Rate Earn 42% More on Spend from 18+ US P|Introducing the US 18+ DevEx Rate: Earn 42% More o]])

## [[luau-language-and-types]]
- 2014-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-object-oriented-programming/8585) Use colon syntax (obj:Method()) for methods that act on an instance; it passes self automatically, equivalent to obj.Method(obj). ([[2014-09-03 - All about Object Oriented Programming|All about Object Oriented Programming]])
- 2015-01-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-cframes/11743) Use CFrame * Vector3 to convert local offsets to world space and CFrame:inverse() * Vector3 to convert world positions into a CFrame's local space. ([[2015-01-03 - How to think about CFrames|How to think about CFrames]])
- 2016-07-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-basic-optimization/26756) Declare frequently used globals and functions as locals (e.g. local sqrt = math.sqrt) so lookups skip the global table. ([[2016-07-05 - The Basics Of Basic Optimization|The Basics Of Basic Optimization]])
- 2018-07-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-think-about-quaternions/143295) Use quaternion twins (negate all components) to pick SLERP direction; flip only W when going the opposite way for a full 360-degree spin. ([[2018-07-05 - How to think about Quaternions|How to think about Quaternions]])
- 2018-08-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780) Use Promises instead of callbacks for asynchronous work so requests do not block other code. ([[2018-08-31 - Guide to Web Development|Guide to Web Development]])
- 2019-02-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avoiding-wait-and-why/244015) Never use wait() in production code; treat it as a code smell signalling a design problem. ([[2019-02-23 - Avoiding wait() and why|Avoiding wait() and why]])
- 2019-02-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avoiding-wait-and-why/244015) Replace polling loops (repeat wait() until flag) with event-based yielding, e.g. event.Event:Wait(), so code resumes the instant the condition is met. ([[2019-02-23 - Avoiding wait() and why|Avoiding wait() and why]])
- 2019-02-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avoiding-wait-and-why/244015) Guard against already-fired events: check the flag first (if not called then event.Event:Wait()) to avoid yielding unnecessarily. ([[2019-02-23 - Avoiding wait() and why|Avoiding wait() and why]])
- 2019-02-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avoiding-wait-and-why/244015) Yield on Roblox events such as ChildAdded, AncestryChanged, ChildRemoved, CharacterAdded, or RunService.Heartbeat instead of polling. ([[2019-02-23 - Avoiding wait() and why|Avoiding wait() and why]])
- 2019-07-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-properly-save-player-data-in-data-stores-upon-server-close/313218) Don't use wait(n) inside BindToClose to stall for time; it buys no extra time. Do meaningful save work instead. ([[2019-07-20 - How to properly save player data in data stores upon server close|How to properly save player data in data stores up]])
- 2019-08-28 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-a-maid-class-on-roblox-to-manage-state/340061) Maid.isMaid checks ClassName == 'Maid' on a table, a simple duck-typing pattern for identifying custom classes. ([[2019-08-28 - How to use a Maid class on Roblox to manage state|How to use a Maid class on Roblox to manage state]])
- 2019-09-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/promises-and-why-you-should-use-them/350825) Use Promise.all to run independent async operations concurrently instead of sequentially when waiting is wasteful. ([[2019-09-16 - Promises and Why You Should Use Them|Promises and Why You Should Use Them]])
- 2019-09-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/promises-and-why-you-should-use-them/350825) Use promise:cancel() with an onCancel hook (e.g. cancelling tweens) to stop work when a menu closes, an ability is interrupted, or a cutscene is skipped. ([[2019-09-16 - Promises and Why You Should Use Them|Promises and Why You Should Use Them]])
- 2019-10-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838) Use lowercase for variables and PascalCase for classes, Instances, and Services; keep capitalization consistent. ([[2019-10-01 - Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblox|Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblo]])
- 2019-10-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838) Store services in variables with matching names, e.g. local ServerScriptService = game:GetService("ServerScriptService"), so usages are easy to find. ([[2019-10-01 - Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblox|Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblo]])
- 2019-10-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838) Prefer local variables over globals to limit scope and avoid accidental collisions. ([[2019-10-01 - Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblox|Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblo]])
- 2019-10-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838) Use FindFirstChild when an instance may not exist (returns nil); use WaitForChild when it will appear later. ([[2019-10-01 - Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblox|Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblo]])
- 2019-12-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930) Use numeric or ipairs loops for arrays to guarantee order; on Roblox ipairs is now as fast or faster than numeric for loops. ([[2019-12-07 - All About Tables (the first of two tutorials on the subject)|All About Tables (the first of two tutorials on th]])
- 2019-12-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930) Use pairs for dictionaries since order is not guaranteed; stick with one iterator consistently in a codebase. ([[2019-12-07 - All About Tables (the first of two tutorials on the subject)|All About Tables (the first of two tutorials on th]])
- 2019-12-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-tables-the-first-of-two-tutorials-on-the-subject/402930) Use table.pack and table.unpack to copy array parts; table.concat joins array elements with an optional delimiter. ([[2019-12-07 - All About Tables (the first of two tutorials on the subject)|All About Tables (the first of two tutorials on th]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-you-need-to-know-about-metatables-and-metamethods/503259) Use rawget/rawset/rawequal inside metamethods to avoid re-triggering __index/__newindex/__eq and causing infinite recursion or C-stack overflow. ([[2020-03-31 - All you need to know about Metatables and Metamethods|All you need to know about Metatables and Metameth]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-you-need-to-know-about-metatables-and-metamethods/503259) Metamethods only fire when a key is missing (__index) or new (__newindex); __newindex does not fire on updating existing keys. ([[2020-03-31 - All you need to know about Metatables and Metamethods|All you need to know about Metatables and Metameth]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-you-need-to-know-about-metatables-and-metamethods/503259) For operator overloading, metamethods must be set on both operands for __eq; order matters for __add/__concat, so check operand types inside the function. ([[2020-03-31 - All you need to know about Metatables and Metamethods|All you need to know about Metatables and Metameth]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-you-need-to-know-about-metatables-and-metamethods/503259) Roblox does not support __gc and forced garbage collection; do not depend on collectgarbage() to free memory. ([[2020-03-31 - All you need to know about Metatables and Metamethods|All you need to know about Metatables and Metameth]])
- 2020-04-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-save-parts-and-the-idea-of-serialization/524311) Use Color3:ToHSV() and Color3.fromHSV(unpack(t)) for compact Color3 serialization; CFrame:GetComponents() with CFrame.new(unpack(t)) is a shorter CFrame alternative. ([[2020-04-14 - How to save parts, and the idea of Serialization|How to save parts, and the idea of Serialization]])
- 2020-04-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/coroutines-when-and-how-to-use-them/541683) Wrap blocking while-loops (e.g. round loops with wait) in coroutine.wrap so code after them still runs; remember to call the wrapped function. ([[2020-04-25 - Coroutines - When and how to use them|Coroutines - When and how to use them]])
- 2020-04-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/coroutines-when-and-how-to-use-them/541683) Prefer coroutine.wrap over create/resume for convenience; it returns a callable function, though it does not return the success boolean. ([[2020-04-25 - Coroutines - When and how to use them|Coroutines - When and how to use them]])
- 2020-09-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/concepts-object-oriented-programming-in-roblox/761267) Use colon syntax (obj:Method()) for methods; it is sugar for obj.Method(obj), so self is passed automatically. ([[2020-09-08 - Concepts Object Oriented Programming in Roblox|Concepts: Object Oriented Programming in Roblox]])
- 2020-09-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/concepts-object-oriented-programming-in-roblox/761267) Prefix private fields and methods with an underscore (_secret) as a convention; Luau has no access modifiers. ([[2020-09-08 - Concepts Object Oriented Programming in Roblox|Concepts: Object Oriented Programming in Roblox]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Localize variables and library functions; the gain is small, so prioritise other optimisations first. Use table.clear() over manual clearing. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-10-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/setup-rojo-fast-the-easy-way-to-setup-rojo-with-git-support/838182) Install Roblox LSP, Selene (linter), Roblox Lua Autocompletes and Roblox API Explorer in VSCode to get Luau IntelliSense and diagnostics. ([[2020-10-26 - Setup Rojo Fast The easy way to setup Rojo with Git support|Setup Rojo Fast: The easy way to setup Rojo with G]])
- 2020-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-vector3cross-and-vector3dot/953984) Use Vector3:Dot() on unit vectors to test facing: result 1 = same direction, -1 = opposite, 0 = perpendicular. Useful for 'only attack if facing NPC' checks. ([[2020-12-31 - The Ultimate Guide to Vector3Cross() and Vector3Dot()|The Ultimate Guide to Vector3:Cross() and Vector3:]])
- 2020-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-vector3cross-and-vector3dot/953984) Clamp dot product to [-1, 1] before math.acos when computing angles, since floating point can push values slightly outside that range. ([[2020-12-31 - The Ultimate Guide to Vector3Cross() and Vector3Dot()|The Ultimate Guide to Vector3:Cross() and Vector3:]])
- 2020-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-vector3cross-and-vector3dot/953984) Vector3:Cross() order matters: LookVector:Cross(UpVector) gives RightVector, while the reverse gives LeftVector. Swap operands to flip the result. ([[2020-12-31 - The Ultimate Guide to Vector3Cross() and Vector3Dot()|The Ultimate Guide to Vector3:Cross() and Vector3:]])
- 2021-02-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-checking-for-beginners/1027242) Add types with 'local name: Type'; Luau then warns on any later assignment of a different type. ([[2021-02-05 - Type checking for beginners!|Type checking for beginners!]])
- 2021-02-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-checking-for-beginners/1027242) Use type assertions (value :: type) to silence warnings when you know a value's type, e.g. any values from tonumber input. ([[2021-02-05 - Type checking for beginners!|Type checking for beginners!]])
- 2021-02-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-checking-for-beginners/1027242) Avoid annotating variables assigned on the same line; type inference already handles it, except for table and complex types. ([[2021-02-05 - Type checking for beginners!|Type checking for beginners!]])
- 2021-02-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-checking-for-beginners/1027242) Use intersection (&) of small table types to build reusable types such as Vector2 or Vector3 shapes. ([[2021-02-05 - Type checking for beginners!|Type checking for beginners!]])
- 2021-05-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-how-to-actually-improve-performance-in-your-games/1221842) Use the task library (task.spawn, task.defer) in place of wait-based spawn(); use += and -= operators. ([[2021-05-12 - Improved How to Actually Improve Performance in Your Games|[Improved] How to Actually Improve Performance in ]])
- 2021-06-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/be-careful-when-using-assert-and-why/1289175) Use Luau type annotations (e.g. number parameters) to move argument type checking to static analysis instead of runtime checks. ([[2021-06-14 - Be careful when using assert() and Why|Be careful when using assert() and Why]])
- 2021-07-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/weighted-chance-system/1373953) Scale float chances by 10 (or a suitable multiplier) before calling math.random, since math.random only accepts integers. ([[2021-07-26 - Weighted Chance System|Weighted Chance System]])
- 2021-08-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcall-xpcall-and-ypcall-tutorial-how-do-they-work/1417471) Do not use ypcall(); it is deprecated because pcall() now yields too. ([[2021-08-16 - Pcall(), xpcall() and ypcall() Tutorial - How do they work|Pcall(), xpcall() and ypcall() Tutorial - How do t]])
- 2021-08-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcall-xpcall-and-ypcall-tutorial-how-do-they-work/1417471) Use xpcall() with a custom error handler function when you need to react to errors; the handler runs only on failure. ([[2021-08-16 - Pcall(), xpcall() and ypcall() Tutorial - How do they work|Pcall(), xpcall() and ypcall() Tutorial - How do t]])
- 2021-08-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pcall-xpcall-and-ypcall-tutorial-how-do-they-work/1417471) Use return inside the pcall'd function to pass data back out; otherwise the result value is nil. ([[2021-08-16 - Pcall(), xpcall() and ypcall() Tutorial - How do they work|Pcall(), xpcall() and ypcall() Tutorial - How do t]])
- 2022-02-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-entity-component-system/1664447) Use Matter.useDeltaTime() when moving entities per frame so speed is frame-rate independent (speed * deltaTime, not speed per frame). ([[2022-02-12 - All about Entity Component System|All about Entity Component System]])
- 2022-09-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-type-checking-with-oop/1997394) Annotate methods with dot syntax and an explicit self: Car.Boost(self: Car) so Luau types self and still allows colon-call syntax. ([[2022-09-27 - Guide to Type-Checking with OOP|Guide to Type-Checking with OOP]])
- 2022-09-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-type-checking-with-oop/1997394) For inheritance, define Truck type as typeof(setmetatable({} :: self, Truck)) & Car.Car to combine child fields with parent type. ([[2022-09-27 - Guide to Type-Checking with OOP|Guide to Type-Checking with OOP]])
- 2022-09-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-type-checking-with-oop/1997394) In subclass constructors, wrap the parent constructor result as setmetatable(Car.new() :: Truck, Truck) to avoid type errors. ([[2022-09-27 - Guide to Type-Checking with OOP|Guide to Type-Checking with OOP]])
- 2022-09-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-type-checking-with-oop/1997394) Set the child's metatable to the parent class (setmetatable(Truck, Car)) after defining the child type, not before, for the typechecker to accept it. ([[2022-09-27 - Guide to Type-Checking with OOP|Guide to Type-Checking with OOP]])
- 2023-04-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-custom-chat-tag/2302562) Type the callback parameter as TextChatMessage to make the OnIncomingMessage handler's expected input explicit. ([[2023-04-18 - How to Create a Custom Chat Tag|How to Create a Custom Chat Tag]])
- 2023-07-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-realistic-npc-eyesight-system/2456522) The tutorial suggests using the task library instead of coroutine.wrap for spawning loops. ([[2023-07-07 - How to Create a Realistic NPC Eyesight System|How to Create a Realistic NPC Eyesight System]])
- 2023-08-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginner-guide-to-metatables-what-are-they-and-every-metamethod-explained/2505946) Use __index for default values or inheritance-like behaviour; it must return a value when a key is missing. ([[2023-08-04 - Full Beginner Guide To Metatables - What are they and every metamethod|Full Beginner Guide To Metatables - What are they ]])
- 2023-08-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginner-guide-to-metatables-what-are-they-and-every-metamethod-explained/2505946) Use __newindex to block or validate unwanted new keys on a table, enforcing rules on assignments. ([[2023-08-04 - Full Beginner Guide To Metatables - What are they and every metamethod|Full Beginner Guide To Metatables - What are they ]])
- 2023-08-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginner-guide-to-metatables-what-are-they-and-every-metamethod-explained/2505946) Both operands of __eq, __lt and __le should share the same metatable, otherwise the comparison fails. ([[2023-08-04 - Full Beginner Guide To Metatables - What are they and every metamethod|Full Beginner Guide To Metatables - What are they ]])
- 2023-08-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginner-guide-to-metatables-what-are-they-and-every-metamethod-explained/2505946) Use __metatable to lock a metatable so getmetatable returns a fixed value instead of the real metatable. - 2023-08-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginner-guide-to-metatables-what-are-they-and-every-metamethod-explained/2505946) Use weak tables (__mode 'k', 'v' or 'kv') for caches so the garbage collector can reclaim unused entries. - 2023-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333) Cache services and required modules in local variables at the top of scripts for better performance. ([[2023-09-09 - All about optimization|All about optimization]])
- 2023-10-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datakeep-a-promise-based-auto-saving-datastore-module/2656200) Luau cannot type-check Promises, so typed promise APIs only get partial type safety. - 2023-10-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-write-great-code-and-speed-up-everything/2666661) Use Promise :andThen and :catch as chainable transforms; they return new promises resolving to the callback result, enabling clean sequencing.
