- 2023-11-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585) Name objects PascalCase, primitives and functions camelCase, constants UPPERCASE, use underscore for unused loop variables, and avoid 3-4 character or vague names like data or connection. ([[2023-11-02 - Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide|Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide]])
- 2023-11-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-luau-buffer-type-beta/2724894) Buffers are not auto-resizable; estimate an upper bound, compute exact size in a prior pass, or resize adaptively with buffer.create/buffer.copy. ([[2023-11-30 - Introducing Luau buffer type Beta|Introducing Luau buffer type [Beta]]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Put --!strict at the top of every script so variables get inferred types instead of falling back to any. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Avoid any unless there is no alternative; it silences the type system entirely. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Refine nullable values with if-checks, and/or inline, early return, or assert before indexing them to avoid warnings. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Use typeof() for top-level concrete types and IsA() to downcast Instances to subclasses like BasePart or Terrain. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Use unknown for untyped external input and narrow it with type() or typeof() before use. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Define reusable shapes with type aliases; use {T} for arrays and {[K]: V} for dictionaries keyed by any type, such as Player. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Use typecasts like {} :: {[Player]: number} to inline complex types compactly instead of multi-line annotations. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Write nullable function types as ((A) -> B)? because (A) -> B? means a function returning a nullable value. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Use (obj :: any) only for deliberate dynamic property access on concrete types, such as setting BodyColors properties by name. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-annotations-a-guide-to-writing-luau-code-that-is-actually-good%E2%84%A2/2843221) Annotate typeof(template) as a type alias to type cloned template objects; if Parent warns, cast the new parent to any. ([[2024-02-18 - Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code that is Actually Good™|Type Annotations! A guide to writing Luau code tha]])
- 2024-03-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/howto-physics-based-tweenservice/2873727) Use TweenService:GetValue(alpha, style, direction) as a pure easing function, with your own alpha accumulator, to get per-frame control instead of Tween:Play(). ([[2024-03-12 - HowTo Physics-Based TweenService|HowTo: Physics-Based TweenService]])
- 2024-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-use-robloxs-physics-character-controllers/3092097) Set Humanoid.EvaluateStateMachine = false on CharacterAdded to disable default humanoid physics before adding a ControllerManager. ([[2024-07-29 - How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Controllers|How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Con]])
- 2024-08-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-when-to-use-different-cases-in-luau-scripting/3113987) Use camelCase for local variables, function names, member values and file names; use LOUD_SNAKE_CASE for constants that never change, e.g. MAX_HEALTH. ([[2024-08-11 - Understanding When to Use Different Cases in Luau Scripting|Understanding When to Use Different Cases in Luau ]])
- 2024-08-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-when-to-use-different-cases-in-luau-scripting/3113987) Write acronyms in mixed case within names (makeHttpRequest, JsonTable), but keep abbreviations fully capitalised (aRGBValue, XYZCoordinate). ([[2024-08-11 - Understanding When to Use Different Cases in Luau Scripting|Understanding When to Use Different Cases in Luau ]])
- 2024-10-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543) Type profile tables with typeof(PlayerStore:StartSessionAsync()) to get autocompletion from ProfileStore's Luau types. ([[2024-10-11 - ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataStore Module)|ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataSto]])
- 2025-02-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/advanced-scripting-tutorial/3511460) Use guard clauses (if not x then return end) instead of nested ifs to flatten logic and make code readable. ([[2025-02-27 - Advanced Scripting Tutorial|Advanced Scripting Tutorial]])
- 2025-02-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/advanced-scripting-tutorial/3511460) Use descriptive PascalCase variable names and camelCase function names; avoid shortcut names like p, c, scr. ([[2025-02-27 - Advanced Scripting Tutorial|Advanced Scripting Tutorial]])
- 2025-02-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/advanced-scripting-tutorial/3511460) Remove unused variables and redundant checks (e.g. Health > 0 checks, extra elseif branches); Roblox core scripts already handle some of these. ([[2025-02-27 - Advanced Scripting Tutorial|Advanced Scripting Tutorial]])
- 2025-02-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/advanced-scripting-tutorial/3511460) Add type annotations to function parameters (e.g. Hit: BasePart) for autocomplete and clearer intent. ([[2025-02-27 - Advanced Scripting Tutorial|Advanced Scripting Tutorial]])
- 2025-02-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/advanced-scripting-tutorial/3511460) Use the same method consistently for getting services (GetService preferred over direct dot access for ReplicatedStorage and CollectionService). ([[2025-02-27 - Advanced Scripting Tutorial|Advanced Scripting Tutorial]])
- 2025-02-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/advanced-scripting-tutorial/3511460) Always remove metatables with setmetatable(Table, nil) when done to avoid memory leaks; a table can only have one metatable. ([[2025-02-27 - Advanced Scripting Tutorial|Advanced Scripting Tutorial]])
- 2025-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luaus-new-type-solver-how-to-utilize-type-functions/3637378) Use keyof<T> to derive a union of a table's keys instead of hand-writing key unions; use index<T, U> to get value types from key unions. ([[2025-05-04 - Luau's New Type Solver How to utilize type functions!|Luau's New Type Solver: How to utilize type functi]])
- 2025-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luaus-new-type-solver-how-to-utilize-type-functions/3637378) Use rawkeyof and rawget when you want to ignore metatables and only inspect a table's own properties. ([[2025-05-04 - Luau's New Type Solver How to utilize type functions!|Luau's New Type Solver: How to utilize type functi]])
- 2025-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luaus-new-type-solver-how-to-utilize-type-functions/3637378) Use the read and write keywords on table type properties to make them read-only or write-only; type errors are raised on violating access. ([[2025-05-04 - Luau's New Type Solver How to utilize type functions!|Luau's New Type Solver: How to utilize type functi]])
- 2025-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luaus-new-type-solver-how-to-utilize-type-functions/3637378) Custom type functions must return a type when called outside type functions, otherwise a type error is emitted. ([[2025-05-04 - Luau's New Type Solver How to utilize type functions!|Luau's New Type Solver: How to utilize type functi]])
- 2025-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luaus-new-type-solver-how-to-utilize-type-functions/3637378) Intersection types can join tables (XCoord & YCoord) or model overloaded functions; primitive intersections like string & number are impossible. ([[2025-05-04 - Luau's New Type Solver How to utilize type functions!|Luau's New Type Solver: How to utilize type functi]])
- 2025-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luaus-new-type-solver-how-to-utilize-type-functions/3637378) Luau cannot declare overloaded function parameters directly; write the function first, then cast it with :: to the overloaded intersection type. ([[2025-05-04 - Luau's New Type Solver How to utilize type functions!|Luau's New Type Solver: How to utilize type functi]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) For Actors-based parallel Luau, use roughly 32-64 Actors, avoid long computations in them, and note the client is currently capped at 3 parallel threads. CFrame setting is not parallel-safe. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2026-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258) Pre-allocate tables and avoid spamming print() or warn(), which costs performance. ([[2026-05-10 - The definitive tutorial to game optimization everything you need to kn|The definitive tutorial to game optimization: ever]])
- 2026-08-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143) Use has() child queries (e.g. Part:has(> Part#SomePart)) so the typed callback argument lets you access the child without WaitForChild. ([[2026-08-13 - Collections How to group instances with query patterns|Collections: How to group instances with query pat]])
- 2026-08-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143) Bind callbacks to class-specific events (OnTouched) so the argument is typed to that class instead of generic Instance. ([[2026-08-13 - Collections How to group instances with query patterns|Collections: How to group instances with query pat]])

## [[code-architecture-and-frameworks]]
- 2014-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-object-oriented-programming/8585) Define a class as a table with Class.__index = Class, a Class.new constructor, and methods on the same table; use setmetatable(obj, Class) so instances find methods. ([[2014-09-03 - All about Object Oriented Programming|All about Object Oriented Programming]])
- 2014-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-object-oriented-programming/8585) Put functions in the class table, not in each instance table; copying functions into every object is inefficient and messy. ([[2014-09-03 - All about Object Oriented Programming|All about Object Oriented Programming]])
- 2014-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-object-oriented-programming/8585) Split large scripts into ModuleScripts that end with 'return Class' so each class is small and easy to change. ([[2014-09-03 - All about Object Oriented Programming|All about Object Oriented Programming]])
- 2014-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-object-oriented-programming/8585) For inheritance, have the child constructor call Parent.new, then setmetatable(Child, Parent) so inherited methods resolve; child methods override parent methods of the same name. ([[2014-09-03 - All about Object Oriented Programming|All about Object Oriented Programming]])
- 2015-12-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/hey-experienced-programmers-nevermore-engine-now-can-be-installed-in-30-seconds/20808) Nevermore is a set of libraries plus a loader; libraries only run if you explicitly load them, so unused ones cost no processing power. ([[2015-12-24 - Hey experienced programmers, Nevermore Engine now can be installed in|Hey experienced programmers, Nevermore Engine now ]])
- 2015-12-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/hey-experienced-programmers-nevermore-engine-now-can-be-installed-in-30-seconds/20808) Build reusable building blocks (e.g. compass model plus a GUI-animation class) rather than finished blocks, so systems stay customisable. ([[2015-12-24 - Hey experienced programmers, Nevermore Engine now can be installed in|Hey experienced programmers, Nevermore Engine now ]])
- 2018-02-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082) If a custom Signal class uses a BindableEvent internally, ensure every connection made on it is disconnected before the owning object's lifetime ends, or give objects a Destroy method. ([[2018-02-08 - PSA Connections can memory leak Instances!|PSA: Connections can memory leak Instances!]])
- 2018-02-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/psa-connections-can-memory-leak-instances/90082) Use a cleanup pattern such as a Maid class to track and disconnect connections in bulk. ([[2018-02-08 - PSA Connections can memory leak Instances!|PSA: Connections can memory leak Instances!]])
- 2018-08-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780) Split API routes into separate router modules (e.g. api/users.js) mounted via app.use, and keep config in its own file. ([[2018-08-31 - Guide to Web Development|Guide to Web Development]])
- 2018-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780) Learn OOP and uni-directional data flow in Lua via the Kurdiez Scripting series (#3-#5) to structure larger code. ([[2018-09-09 - Game Development Resources MEGA THREAD|Game Development Resources [MEGA THREAD]]])
- 2018-11-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-furniture-placement-system/205509) Use OOP classes with metatables and separate constructors (new, fromSerialization) to keep reusable, modular systems. ([[2018-11-26 - Creating A Furniture Placement System|Creating A Furniture Placement System]])
- 2019-08-28 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-a-maid-class-on-roblox-to-manage-state/340061) Use a Maid class to collect events, connections and instances, then clean them all up in one call to avoid memory leaks and simplify teardown. ([[2019-08-28 - How to use a Maid class on Roblox to manage state|How to use a Maid class on Roblox to manage state]])
- 2019-09-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dungeon-generation-a-procedural-generation-guide/342413) Split procedural generation into OOP classes (Cell, Maze, Room, House, Dungeon) with a Settings module holding tunable constants. ([[2019-09-01 - Dungeon Generation A Procedural Generation Guide|Dungeon Generation: A Procedural Generation Guide]])
- 2019-09-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/promises-and-why-you-should-use-them/350825) Wrap yielding functions in Promises so callers know the call is async; calling a Promise-returning function never yields, making yield bugs and race conditions easier to avoid. ([[2019-09-16 - Promises and Why You Should Use Them|Promises and Why You Should Use Them]])
- 2019-09-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/promises-and-why-you-should-use-them/350825) Use finally for cleanup that must run whether a Promise resolves, rejects, or is cancelled. ([[2019-09-16 - Promises and Why You Should Use Them|Promises and Why You Should Use Them]])
- 2019-09-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/promises-and-why-you-should-use-them/350825) Centralise async logic in one Promise library so all async APIs behave consistently and compose; ad-hoc pcall/success-flag patterns diverge and cause usage mistakes. ([[2019-09-16 - Promises and Why You Should Use Them|Promises and Why You Should Use Them]])
- 2019-10-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scripting-basics-beginner-guide-to-lua-and-roblox/359838) Keep code neat: name variables clearly and indent, so you and others can read it later and spot improvements. ([[2019-10-01 - Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblox|Scripting Basics - Beginner guide to lua and Roblo]])
- 2019-11-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618) Use local variables for limited scope inside code blocks; global variables stay accessible throughout the script, which can cause clutter. ([[2019-11-24 - Lua Scripting Starter Guide|Lua Scripting Starter Guide]])
- 2019-11-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618) Use indentation and comments to organise code; they make code blocks readable for you and reviewers. ([[2019-11-24 - Lua Scripting Starter Guide|Lua Scripting Starter Guide]])
- 2020-02-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584) Use a class with .new and : methods (BattleCreator) holding per-battle state, so each battle is a self-contained object you can destroy cleanly. ([[2020-02-14 - Creating a battle system OOP style!|Creating a battle system: OOP style!]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-you-need-to-know-about-metatables-and-metamethods/503259) Use __index pointing to a backup table for default values or class-style lookups, sharing keys between a table and its fallback. ([[2020-03-31 - All you need to know about Metatables and Metamethods|All you need to know about Metatables and Metameth]])
- 2020-06-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/yucon-framework-optimization-organization-and-high-level-security/630895) Avoid general-purpose frameworks. They create unnecessary memory buildup and prevent proper typechecking and autocomplete on many things. ([[2020-06-17 - Yucon Framework Optimization, organization, and high-level security|Yucon Framework || Optimization, organization, and]])
- 2020-06-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/yucon-framework-optimization-organization-and-high-level-security/630895) Use smaller frameworks that each solve one problem: a UI framework (Fusion or Roact) for UI, a remote framework for remotes, and a load-behavior framework. ([[2020-06-17 - Yucon Framework Optimization, organization, and high-level security|Yucon Framework || Optimization, organization, and]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207) Store pseudo-accessory Models in a folder inside the character model for organisation, since they do not need to be parented directly to the character. ([[2020-08-27 - Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. UPDATED|Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. [UPDATED]])
- 2020-09-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/concepts-object-oriented-programming-in-roblox/761267) Build reusable objects with a ModuleScript constructor (Car.new) so each script doesn't redefine the constructor. ([[2020-09-08 - Concepts Object Oriented Programming in Roblox|Concepts: Object Oriented Programming in Roblox]])
- 2020-09-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/concepts-object-oriented-programming-in-roblox/761267) Avoid deep inheritance chains; they increase complexity and a broken parent prototype breaks all its children. Prefer composition. ([[2020-09-08 - Concepts Object Oriented Programming in Roblox|Concepts: Object Oriented Programming in Roblox]])
- 2020-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-scripting-roadmap-learning-resource-list/808430) Study Rojo and Git before building larger projects, and consider frameworks like Roact, Aero or Nevermore once comfortable with OOP and metatables. ([[2020-10-06 - Roblox Scripting Roadmap & Learning Resource List|Roblox Scripting Roadmap & Learning Resource List]])
- 2021-02-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-checking-for-beginners/1027242) Put --!strict at the top of every script you want type checked. ([[2021-02-05 - Type checking for beginners!|Type checking for beginners!]])
- 2021-02-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/type-checking-for-beginners/1027242) Define shared types in a ModuleScript using 'export type' and require them where needed to keep scripts organized. ([[2021-02-05 - Type checking for beginners!|Type checking for beginners!]])
- 2021-02-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/efficient-object-oriented-programming-tutorial/1061153) Use a base Rcade.class wrapper with __call and __init so classes are created via Class(...) and share one constructor pattern across the project. ([[2021-02-21 - Efficient Object Oriented Programming Tutorial|Efficient Object Oriented Programming Tutorial]])
- 2021-02-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/efficient-object-oriented-programming-tutorial/1061153) Implement inheritance with a __super field and an Rcade __index that falls back to the superclass; call super __init explicitly from the subclass __init. ([[2021-02-21 - Efficient Object Oriented Programming Tutorial|Efficient Object Oriented Programming Tutorial]])
- 2021-02-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/efficient-object-oriented-programming-tutorial/1061153) Copy metamethods (keys starting with __, excluding __super and __init) from the superclass onto subclasses so __tostring etc. are inherited; note this slightly increases memory. ([[2021-02-21 - Efficient Object Oriented Programming Tutorial|Efficient Object Oriented Programming Tutorial]])
- 2021-10-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-procedural-mountains-a-fractal-noise-tutorial/1494362) Split generation into modules (noise, colour, block generation) once you add more noise types or features. ([[2021-10-04 - Creating Procedural Mountains A Fractal Noise Tutorial|Creating Procedural Mountains: A Fractal Noise Tut]])
- 2021-12-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/you-need-to-use-the-knit-game-frameworka-template-to-help-get-started-using-knit/1592333) Use Knit's Service (server) and Controller (client) paradigm to split one-script games into modules that scale better, though one script is viable for performance. ([[2021-12-17 - You need to use the Knit Game Framework(A template to help get started|You need to use the Knit Game Framework(A template]])
- 2021-12-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/you-need-to-use-the-knit-game-frameworka-template-to-help-get-started-using-knit/1592333) Put Knit.AddServices and Knit.AddControllers in separate Runtime scripts, then call Knit.Start() on each side; this bootstrapping is configurable unlike Aero. ([[2021-12-17 - You need to use the Knit Game Framework(A template to help get started|You need to use the Knit Game Framework(A template]])
- 2021-12-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/you-need-to-use-the-knit-game-frameworka-template-to-help-get-started-using-knit/1592333) Use Component.Auto with CollectionService tags to bind classes to instances, with Init, Deinit and update hooks, and clean up connections via Trove. ([[2021-12-17 - You need to use the Knit Game Framework(A template to help get started|You need to use the Knit Game Framework(A template]])
- 2021-12-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-janitor-to-combat-memory-leaks/1601710) Give each class a _janitor member; add signals, connections and instances to it, and call Destroy in the class's Destroy method, setting it to nil afterward. ([[2021-12-25 - Using Janitor to combat memory leaks|Using Janitor to combat memory leaks]])
- 2021-12-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-janitor-to-combat-memory-leaks/1601710) Pass an index as the third argument to :Add so you can later prematurely clean one item with :Remove(index). ([[2021-12-25 - Using Janitor to combat memory leaks|Using Janitor to combat memory leaks]])
- 2021-12-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-janitor-to-combat-memory-leaks/1601710) Use :Cleanup to reuse a Janitor, and :Destroy when the object is finished, since Destroy makes it unusable. ([[2021-12-25 - Using Janitor to combat memory leaks|Using Janitor to combat memory leaks]])
- 2021-12-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-janitor-to-combat-memory-leaks/1601710) Use Janitor's returned object from :Add when constructing inline, e.g. local obj = janitor:Add(Obj.new(), "Destroy"). ([[2021-12-25 - Using Janitor to combat memory leaks|Using Janitor to combat memory leaks]])
- 2022-01-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-oop-object-oriented-programming/1639499) Put shared modules in ReplicatedStorage/Shared, server-only modules in ServerStorage, and keep a single client LocalScript and a single server Script as entry points. ([[2022-01-22 - Roblox OOP (Object Oriented Programming)|Roblox OOP (Object Oriented Programming)]])
- 2022-01-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-oop-object-oriented-programming/1639499) Use a module table with Module.__index = Module and a constructor that calls setmetatable(obj, Module) and returns obj; methods then work via colon syntax. ([[2022-01-22 - Roblox OOP (Object Oriented Programming)|Roblox OOP (Object Oriented Programming)]])
- 2022-01-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-oop-object-oriented-programming/1639499) Forgetting to return the constructed object from the constructor makes method calls fail; always return the new table. ([[2022-01-22 - Roblox OOP (Object Oriented Programming)|Roblox OOP (Object Oriented Programming)]])
- 2022-01-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-oop-object-oriented-programming/1639499) Use inheritance by requiring the base module and calling setmetatable(Child, Base) so child objects reuse base methods like Shoot while adding their own. ([[2022-01-22 - Roblox OOP (Object Oriented Programming)|Roblox OOP (Object Oriented Programming)]])
- 2022-02-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-entity-component-system/1664447) Prefer composition over deep inheritance: model behaviour as data components added to entities, so new behaviours (e.g. Fly, Float) combine without conflicting class hierarchies. ([[2022-02-12 - All about Entity Component System|All about Entity Component System]])
- 2022-02-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-entity-component-system/1664447) In ECS, components hold pure data with no behaviour; systems hold self-contained logic that query specific component sets. - 2019-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-you-should-secure-your-game-a-beginner-guide-for-secure-networking-and-developing-anticheats/351775) Limit remote traffic to about one basic remote per frame. - 2024-06-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930) Use RemoteEvents rather than RemoteFunctions for hit reporting so one attack can hit multiple targets, and for security reasons. - 2024-06-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930) Early attack signaling: send the hitbox event during a windup, include os.clock() timestamp, and have the client wait (DelayTime minus elapsed) before activating so hitbox appears in sync. - 2024-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332) Create predictable projectiles on the client (client-sided) to save resources; use the server for complex projectiles that follow cursors or rely on physics, then hand network ownership to the client. - 2024-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332) Client-origin creation (no server wait) suits FPS-style rapid fire, but sanity-check ammo and similar state on the server when the client calls a hit. ([[2024-06-24 - The Basics of Combat Games Projectiles|The Basics of Combat Games: Projectiles]])
- 2024-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332) Replicate client-made projectiles to other clients by sending the attacker's projectile direction back to the server, then relaying it to all clients for cosmetic copies. ([[2024-06-24 - The Basics of Combat Games Projectiles|The Basics of Combat Games: Projectiles]])
- 2024-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332) Let clients simulate stopping for rapid or unimportant projectiles; send a remote event only for important projectiles (e.g. explosions) so stop time and location stay accurate. ([[2024-06-24 - The Basics of Combat Games Projectiles|The Basics of Combat Games: Projectiles]])
- 2024-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-projectiles/3038332) Do not offset the server ghost projectile by one latency unit; it would end up ahead of the client's position. Keep it as-is, since it matches the client's position at hit time. ([[2024-06-24 - The Basics of Combat Games Projectiles|The Basics of Combat Games: Projectiles]])

- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-how-to-begin/4139185) Enable Server Authority via Workspace > Server Authority > AuthorityMode = Server; this disables certain other workspace properties automatically. - 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-how-to-begin/4139185) With Server Authority, clients no longer own instances; only their inputs are trusted. Keep replicated client data to a minimum. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority How to Begin|Server Authority: How to Begin?]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-how-to-begin/4139185) Put core game logic in RunService:BindToSimulation so it runs on both client and server, including during rollback. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority How to Begin|Server Authority: How to Begin?]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-how-to-begin/4139185) Place shared simulation code in a ModuleScript in ReplicatedStorage, required and run by both server and client scripts. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority How to Begin|Server Authority: How to Begin?]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321) Server only replicates changes. To force resync of an attribute holder, set an attribute each frame on the server (e.g. SetAttribute('Time', time())) in BindToSimulation. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engineers!|Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engin]])

- 2026-07-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993) Do not rely on RemoteEvents for anything that must stay synchronized with DataModel changes; they are not on the shared timeline. - 2026-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568) Summaries are generated on the network owner (client or server) and not replicated; fire your own RemoteEvent with only the needed data to inform other clients. ## [[anti-exploit-and-security]]
- 2015-11-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/stravants-bitbuffer-module-compact-storage-of-data-tutorial/20189) Compact encoding is obfuscation, not security; encrypt sensitive data and never store what players shouldn't decode. ([[2015-11-30 - Stravant's BitBuffer Module - Compact Storage Of Data Tutorial|Stravant's BitBuffer Module - Compact Storage Of D]])
- 2016-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-method-hooking-and-keeping-your-game-protected/31442) Assume the client can be hooked or spoofed. Never implement security fully on the client; any client-only solution will eventually be bypassed. ([[2016-12-09 - Understanding method hooking and keeping your game protected|Understanding method hooking and keeping your game]])
- 2016-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-method-hooking-and-keeping-your-game-protected/31442) Cache remote methods via a local reference (e.g. local FireServer = Instance.new("RemoteEvent").FireServer) to avoid calling hooked __index results. Only a partial mitigation; resets can undo it. ([[2016-12-09 - Understanding method hooking and keeping your game protected|Understanding method hooking and keeping your game]])
- 2016-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-method-hooking-and-keeping-your-game-protected/31442) Compare a cached method to the current one (e.g. RemoteEvent.FireServer) to detect detours. Avoid obvious responses since exploiters can adapt; it is easy to bypass. ([[2016-12-09 - Understanding method hooking and keeping your game protected|Understanding method hooking and keeping your game]])
- 2016-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/understanding-method-hooking-and-keeping-your-game-protected/31442) Treat any asset or code visible on the client as potentially exposed; exploiters can save instances to disk and read module source. ([[2016-12-09 - Understanding method hooking and keeping your game protected|Understanding method hooking and keeping your game]])
- 2017-10-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-scripting-bots-javascript-tutorial-funcaptcha-and-new-host-info/58201) Set a maximum promotable rank so bot-driven promotions can't change protected ranks like officers. ([[2017-10-25 - Guide to Scripting Bots Javascript Tutorial FunCaptcha and New Host In|Guide to Scripting Bots | Javascript Tutorial | Fu]])
- 2018-08-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780) Add express-rate-limit to your API; without it, attackers can brute-force your auth token. Tune max requests per window to your traffic. ([[2018-08-31 - Guide to Web Development|Guide to Web Development]])
- 2018-08-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780) Sanitize incoming request data with express-sanitizer and set security headers with helmet to block basic attacks. ([[2018-08-31 - Guide to Web Development|Guide to Web Development]])
- 2018-08-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780) Keep secrets like database URIs and API secrets in environment variables (process.env), not hardcoded in source. ([[2018-08-31 - Guide to Web Development|Guide to Web Development]])
- 2018-08-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780) Develop with security in mind and keep up with current attack methods (DoS, CSRF, XSS, MITM, brute force, social engineering). ([[2018-08-31 - Guide to Web Development|Guide to Web Development]])
- 2018-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780) Remove malicious scripts from free models using Ro-Defender before publishing; free models often contain virus-like scripts. ([[2018-09-09 - Game Development Resources MEGA THREAD|Game Development Resources [MEGA THREAD]]])
- 2018-11-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-furniture-placement-system/205509) Validate placements on the server (collision check and constraints) before cloning the model, since clients can send arbitrary CFrames. ([[2018-11-26 - Creating A Furniture Placement System|Creating A Furniture Placement System]])
- 2018-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-prevent-speed-hacking/210733) Detect speed hacks by position, not by WalkSpeed or Velocity: hackers can alter any property, but server-side position deltas catch all methods, including FPS-based hacks. ([[2018-12-12 - How to Prevent Speed Hacking|How to Prevent Speed Hacking]])
- 2018-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-prevent-speed-hacking/210733) Don't kick on suspected speed hacks; reset the player to their last valid CFrame to avoid rage from false positives on slow connections. ([[2018-12-12 - How to Prevent Speed Hacking|How to Prevent Speed Hacking]])
- 2018-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-prevent-speed-hacking/210733) Set the max distance above default WalkSpeed (e.g. 22 vs 16 with checktime 1s) to avoid flagging slow players who appear faster to the server. ([[2018-12-12 - How to Prevent Speed Hacking|How to Prevent Speed Hacking]])
- 2018-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-prevent-speed-hacking/210733) Keep maxspeed and checktime proportional; checks that run too fast (e.g. 0.1s) record positions too quickly and fail to work correctly. ([[2018-12-12 - How to Prevent Speed Hacking|How to Prevent Speed Hacking]])
- 2018-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-prevent-speed-hacking/210733) Compare only X and Z axes so falling does not trigger a false positive. ([[2018-12-12 - How to Prevent Speed Hacking|How to Prevent Speed Hacking]])
- 2018-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-prevent-speed-hacking/210733) Round the distance with math.floor before comparing to avoid inaccurate readings from long decimals. ([[2018-12-12 - How to Prevent Speed Hacking|How to Prevent Speed Hacking]])
- 2018-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-prevent-speed-hacking/210733) Disable the check for a few seconds around legitimate teleports, since position-based detection flags teleportation. ([[2018-12-12 - How to Prevent Speed Hacking|How to Prevent Speed Hacking]])
- 2019-04-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895) Put the permission check in a shared ModuleScript so client and server run the same validation, but always re-verify on the server. ([[2019-04-01 - Creating a CrateSpin System|Creating a Crate/Spin System]])
- 2019-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-state-machines-to-mitigate-teleport-exploits/297629) Model player progression as a finite state machine with explicit states, so invalid transitions between zones can be detected as exploits. ([[2019-06-24 - Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits|Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits]])
- 2019-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-state-machines-to-mitigate-teleport-exploits/297629) Detect teleporters with region checks or part collisions; a direct jump from zone A to non-adjacent zone C counts as an exploit. ([[2019-06-24 - Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits|Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits]])
- 2019-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-state-machines-to-mitigate-teleport-exploits/297629) Add transition conditions (minimum time to cross a zone, door opened, button pressed, key owned) to catch teleporters who skip zones in sequence. ([[2019-06-24 - Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits|Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits]])
- 2019-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-state-machines-to-mitigate-teleport-exploits/297629) Goal is to stop drastic speedups beyond intended pace, not all teleporting; slow short teleports are indistinguishable from skilled play on slow connections. ([[2019-06-24 - Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits|Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits]])
- 2019-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-state-machines-to-mitigate-teleport-exploits/297629) Be aware a time-based minimum can false-positive when players are flung, so account for that case. ([[2019-06-24 - Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits|Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits]])
- 2019-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-state-machines-to-mitigate-teleport-exploits/297629) For intentional teleports (events), add a temporary state that permits teleporting, with the exit transition being the teleport itself. ([[2019-06-24 - Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits|Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits]])
- 2019-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-state-machines-to-mitigate-teleport-exploits/297629) Use doors as state transitions instead of constant region checks on large or non-rectangular map sections. ([[2019-06-24 - Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits|Using State Machines To Mitigate Teleport Exploits]])
- 2019-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-you-should-secure-your-game-a-beginner-guide-for-secure-networking-and-developing-anticheats/351775) Never trust the client: exploiters can read and change any LocalScript/ModuleScript and all remote traffic. Put validation and authority on the server. ([[2019-09-18 - How you should secure your game - A beginner guide for secure networki|How you should secure your game - A beginner guide]])
- 2019-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-you-should-secure-your-game-a-beginner-guide-for-secure-networking-and-developing-anticheats/351775) Keep server-only logic in ServerScriptService/ServerStorage modules; their bytecode is never sent to the client and cannot be decompiled. ([[2019-09-18 - How you should secure your game - A beginner guide for secure networki|How you should secure your game - A beginner guide]])
- 2019-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-you-should-secure-your-game-a-beginner-guide-for-secure-networking-and-developing-anticheats/351775) Prefer passive anticheats that correct illegal state (reset position, clamp velocity) over kicking; they are more accurate and better for UX. ([[2019-09-18 - How you should secure your game - A beginner guide for secure networki|How you should secure your game - A beginner guide]])
- 2019-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-you-should-secure-your-game-a-beginner-guide-for-secure-networking-and-developing-anticheats/351775) Use a generous leeway (e.g. 2 studs) on speed/noclip checks; low leeway causes rubber-banding during lag. ([[2019-09-18 - How you should secure your game - A beginner guide for secure networki|How you should secure your game - A beginner guide]])
- 2019-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-you-should-secure-your-game-a-beginner-guide-for-secure-networking-and-developing-anticheats/351775) Avoid permanent bans as a main response: exploiters usually just make an alternate account. Soft penalties like disabling purchases or interactions work better. ([[2019-09-18 - How you should secure your game - A beginner guide for secure networki|How you should secure your game - A beginner guide]])
- 2019-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-you-should-secure-your-game-a-beginner-guide-for-secure-networking-and-developing-anticheats/351775) Use honeypot remotes (e.g. a fake AddMoney remote). Any firing of a remote that legitimate code never uses identifies an exploiter. ([[2019-09-18 - How you should secure your game - A beginner guide for secure networki|How you should secure your game - A beginner guide]])
- 2019-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-you-should-secure-your-game-a-beginner-guide-for-secure-networking-and-developing-anticheats/351775) Guard against false positives even at the cost of anticheat strength; never punish a player on an uncertain detection. ([[2019-09-18 - How you should secure your game - A beginner guide for secure networki|How you should secure your game - A beginner guide]])
- 2019-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-you-should-secure-your-game-a-beginner-guide-for-secure-networking-and-developing-anticheats/351775) Disguise soft punishments as glitches, e.g. give a cheater a fake 'Invalid Item', to make cheating annoying without outright banning. ([[2019-09-18 - How you should secure your game - A beginner guide for secure networki|How you should secure your game - A beginner guide]])
- 2019-11-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lua-scripting-starter-guide/394618) Exploits mostly succeed against weak scripts; write solid server-side logic and rely on FilteringEnabled, which is applied to all games by default. ([[2019-11-24 - Lua Scripting Starter Guide|Lua Scripting Starter Guide]])
- 2020-01-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replicating-instances-to-only-some-players/438374) Validate remote inputs on the server (typeof check for Instance) and only accept completion callbacks for objects the server actually sent to that player. ([[2020-01-20 - Replicating Instances to Only Some Players|Replicating Instances to Only Some Players]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318) Have the server manage damage, ammo and equipped state rather than trusting the client. ([[2020-03-31 - Writing an FPS framework (2020)|Writing an FPS framework (2020)]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-you-need-to-know-about-metatables-and-metamethods/503259) Never rely on client-side property checks like WalkSpeed > 16 to catch speed exploits; exploiters can spoof reads via getrawmetatable __index hooks. Validate on the server. ([[2020-03-31 - All you need to know about Metatables and Metamethods|All you need to know about Metatables and Metameth]])
- 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-remove-backdoors-from-your-game/511548) Check plugins before installing: fake or botted plugins are a common backdoor source. Look at creator username, join date, and creation date, not just sales counts. ([[2020-04-05 - How to remove backdoors from your game|How to remove backdoors from your game]])
- 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-remove-backdoors-from-your-game/511548) Treat free models as placeholders only. Check the creator (bot-like name or new account), and after inserting, delete any scripts inside the model (tools excepted). Replace with your own assets. ([[2020-04-05 - How to remove backdoors from your game|How to remove backdoors from your game]])
- 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-remove-backdoors-from-your-game/511548) Use Ctrl+Shift+F in Studio to search for backdoor strings: math., require, luraph, synapse, string., I, loadstring, getfenv, setfenv, IsStudio. Follow any found ModuleScript ID to its source. ([[2020-04-05 - How to remove backdoors from your game|How to remove backdoors from your game]])
- 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-remove-backdoors-from-your-game/511548) Look for a ':run'-style function call after a suspicious module, which typically activates the backdoor. ([[2020-04-05 - How to remove backdoors from your game|How to remove backdoors from your game]])
- 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-remove-backdoors-from-your-game/511548) Distinguish backdoors from server-side access remotes: both grant server-level effects but stem from different properties, so audit remotes separately. ([[2020-04-05 - How to remove backdoors from your game|How to remove backdoors from your game]])
- 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/clearing-your-game-of-malicious-scripts-plugins-and-backdoors/511830) Remove any plugin you don't recognize, and check that creator names match exactly since display names can be impersonated. ([[2020-04-05 - Clearing your game of malicious scripts, plugins, and backdoors|Clearing your game of malicious scripts, plugins, ]])
- 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/clearing-your-game-of-malicious-scripts-plugins-and-backdoors/511830) Verify plugin source code before installing unless the plugin was purchased with Robux; many free plugins can inject malicious code. ([[2020-04-05 - Clearing your game of malicious scripts, plugins, and backdoors|Clearing your game of malicious scripts, plugins, ]])
- 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/clearing-your-game-of-malicious-scripts-plugins-and-backdoors/511830) Scan for rogue scripts by printing GetFullName() of all LuaSourceContainer descendants across every service you can, including CSGDictionaryService. - 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/clearing-your-game-of-malicious-scripts-plugins-and-backdoors/511830) Enable 'Show Hidden Objects in Explorer' in Studio settings to find scripts hidden in services like CSGDictionaryService; anything besides MeshData there is suspicious. - 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/clearing-your-game-of-malicious-scripts-plugins-and-backdoors/511830) Search all scripts (Ctrl/Cmd+Shift+F) for MarketplaceService, string.reverse, string.byte, require, and getfenv to locate backdoor code. - 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/clearing-your-game-of-malicious-scripts-plugins-and-backdoors/511830) Limit free models and check inserted models for scripts before keeping them. - 2020-04-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/clearing-your-game-of-malicious-scripts-plugins-and-backdoors/511830) Only use scripts from trusted sources; the DevForum is safer because public posts are visible to everyone and bad actors get caught quickly. - 2020-05-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/perspective-object-dragging-like-superliminal/566200) Network ownership handed to clients can cause security issues in multiplayer; validate or find another ownership solution before shipping multiplayer. ([[2020-05-09 - Perspective object dragging like Superliminal|Perspective object dragging like Superliminal]])
- 2020-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-part-2-2020/581791) Don't trust client-sent hit results; the author's damage-by-client remote is a shortcut, and real games should verify hits server-side. - 2020-06-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-server-synced-daily-shops/632401) Keep daily shop rotation logic on the server (ServerStorage/ServerScriptService) so it is not replicated to clients. - 2020-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiters-and-how-they-spoof-stuff-in-your-own-game/695958) Never rely on client-side checks like Humanoid.WalkSpeed > 16 then kick; exploiters can hook the game metatable to spoof returned values. ([[2020-07-29 - Exploiters and how they spoof stuff in your own game|Exploiters and how they spoof stuff in your own ga]])
- 2020-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiters-and-how-they-spoof-stuff-in-your-own-game/695958) Do not call LocalPlayer:Kick() from the client; exploiters can hook __namecall and return early so the kick never happens. ([[2020-07-29 - Exploiters and how they spoof stuff in your own game|Exploiters and how they spoof stuff in your own ga]])
- 2020-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiters-and-how-they-spoof-stuff-in-your-own-game/695958) Put anti-cheat validation on the server; client-sided anticheat is easily bypassed within minutes by a decent cheater. - 2020-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiters-and-how-they-spoof-stuff-in-your-own-game/695958) Hiding a check deep in casual scripts only stops deletion, not spoofing, so it is not a sufficient defence. - 2020-07-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753) Remove noPlotActivate() unless placement is intentionally plot-free, to prevent exploiters from using it. ([[2020-07-31 - How to use Placement Service|How to use Placement Service]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912) Do all damage and combat-tag tracking on the server; the server-only approach cannot be spoofed by clients. ([[2020-08-27 - How to Catch Combat Logging|How to Catch Combat Logging]])
- 2020-09-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594) Rely mostly on server-side anti-exploit logic; client-side checks can be deleted by exploiters, but server scripts are not replicated and cannot be seen or removed by them. - 2020-09-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594) Audit every free model and plugin script for backdoors; search for 'require' and obfuscated code, since backdoors (SS) are the main server-side risk. - 2020-09-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594) Log client errors via ScriptContext.Error and send message plus script full name to a RemoteEvent; review logs manually before banning to avoid false positives. - 2020-09-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594) Use game:GetService("Name") instead of game.Name so that randomised service names cause errors you can catch when exploit scripts run. - 2020-09-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594) Implement server-side distance checks per interval (e.g. every 4s) and warn when travel exceeds expected walkspeed × time plus an error margin. - 2020-09-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594) Validate money and item remotes on the server: check award size, ensure it only affects the firing player, and rate-limit item giving per second. - 2020-09-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-how-exploits-work-how-to-best-prevent-them/767594) Expect that Roblox cannot fully stop exploits itself; developers must write game-specific checks because they understand their own game's valid player behaviour best. - 2020-09-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/account-security-psa/792056) Enable 2-step verification in account settings to protect your account, which matters for developers who control games and Robux. ([[2020-09-26 - Account Security PSA|Account Security PSA]])
- 2020-09-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/account-security-psa/792056) Use strong passwords mixing letters, numbers and symbols; never give personal information to anyone. ([[2020-09-26 - Account Security PSA|Account Security PSA]])
- 2020-09-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/account-security-psa/792056) Use the Report Abuse button on private messages asking you to visit an off-platform link. ([[2020-09-26 - Account Security PSA|Account Security PSA]])
- 2020-11-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/melee-hitboxes-what-is-the-best-solution-and-is-the-touched-event-really-that-bad/867477) Validate Touched hits server-side by rejecting any touched part farther than about 5 studs from the weapon or player. - 2020-12-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-ownership-for-beginners-how-to-handle-physics-on-the-client/947029) Client-owned physics trusts the client; validate important results server-side when handing ownership to players. - 2021-01-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/hexolus-server-sided-anticheat/983706) Anticheat works best for simple games that don't heavily customize physics, such as minigames; expect compatibility issues with custom physics. - 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) Never trust the client or rely on a fully client-side anti-exploit; exploiters can disable or spoof it. - 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) Don't instantly kick or ban on detection; anti-cheats produce false positives. - 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) Add a margin of error to each check to absorb lag. For max WalkSpeed 16, flag only above ~24-28. ([[2021-12-30 - A Guide to Making Proper Anti-Exploits|A Guide to Making Proper Anti-Exploits]])
- 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) Don't check PlatformStanding, Humanoid.Running or humanoid states for exploits; the client controls them and can stop them replicating. - 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) Detect speed/teleport by computing distance/time between server loop samples, ignoring the Y axis to avoid ladder and fall false positives. ([[2021-12-30 - A Guide to Making Proper Anti-Exploits|A Guide to Making Proper Anti-Exploits]])
- 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) Detect noclip by raycasting between last and current positions in both directions; flag only if inside a solid or passed through a large depth. - 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) For fly detection, check Humanoid.FloorMaterial ~= Air (reliable now) and flag only if airborne for long (e.g. 5s) and not falling. - 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) Validate every remote on the server: check purchases have enough currency and donation amounts are positive and the target is valid. - 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) Anti-cheat must be designed around game mechanics such as sprint or speed boosts; poorly tuned anti-cheats can ruin a game's playerbase. ([[2021-12-30 - A Guide to Making Proper Anti-Exploits|A Guide to Making Proper Anti-Exploits]])
- 2022-01-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginners-guide-on-scripting-animefighting-vfxvisual-effects-part-1/1610853) Use server-sided debounces (a table keyed by player name) instead of client-sided bool/time debounces, since exploiters can spam FireServer. ([[2022-01-14 - How exploits work and how to combat them|How exploits work and how to combat them]])
- 2022-01-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-exploits-work-and-how-to-combat-them/1627229) Check property changes like WalkSpeed on the server, since client-owned movement replicates regardless of the server-side property value. ([[2022-01-14 - How exploits work and how to combat them|How exploits work and how to combat them]])
- 2022-01-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-exploits-work-and-how-to-combat-them/1627229) Be careful with memory-spike detection: large or part-heavy games can trigger false-positive kicks. ([[2022-01-14 - How exploits work and how to combat them|How exploits work and how to combat them]])
- 2022-01-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-exploits-work-and-how-to-combat-them/1627229) Never expose loadstring-style remotes that run client-sent code; this is the backdoor pattern hidden in free models. ([[2022-01-14 - How exploits work and how to combat them|How exploits work and how to combat them]])
- 2022-01-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298) Don't rely on client-side security; exploiters can spoof any client method, so memory checks, Index overrides and CoreGui checks are bypassable within minutes. - 2022-01-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298) Avoid memory checking: it is bypassable in about 10 lines of code and can false-positive on low-end hardware. ([[2022-01-25 - Why Your Anti-Exploit Sucks|Why Your Anti-Exploit Sucks]])
- 2022-01-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298) Validate speed on the server using strictly horizontal velocity, and flag only consistent over-threshold intervals rather than single spikes. - 2022-01-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298) Punish gently when a machine flags someone: teleport the player back to pre-speed-change position instead of kicking or banning. ([[2022-01-25 - Why Your Anti-Exploit Sucks|Why Your Anti-Exploit Sucks]])
- 2022-01-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/why-your-anti-exploit-sucks/1643298) Don't kick players for legitimate states such as PlatformStanding (used for ragdolls); it blocks legit developers and is bypassed by not using that state. ([[2022-01-25 - Why Your Anti-Exploit Sucks|Why Your Anti-Exploit Sucks]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developers-are-not-equipped-to-deal-with-exploiters/1770356) Only player:Kick() is available to remove a player after join; there is no native Ban, so build your own ban list with DataStoreService or a third-party service. ([[2022-04-27 - Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiters|Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiter]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developers-are-not-equipped-to-deal-with-exploiters/1770356) Ban accounts only if you can; account creation is free, so account bans alone are insufficient against exploiters who create new accounts. ([[2022-04-27 - Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiters|Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiter]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developers-are-not-equipped-to-deal-with-exploiters/1770356) Log every moderation action and support ticket; the author tracked bans by month and by source (moderator vs anti-cheat) to spot trends. ([[2022-04-27 - Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiters|Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiter]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developers-are-not-equipped-to-deal-with-exploiters/1770356) Keep a watch list of suspected exploiters separate from confirmed bans; most flagged accounts are not yet observed exploiting, so require evidence before banning. ([[2022-04-27 - Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiters|Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiter]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/developers-are-not-equipped-to-deal-with-exploiters/1770356) Validate all client-sent actions on the server; the official Game Security doc page offers only brief guidance (disable loadstring, validate server-side, check models for scripts). ([[2022-04-27 - Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiters|Developers are not equipped to deal with exploiter]])
- 2023-02-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-proper-anti-exploits-the-ultimate-guide/2172882) Never trust remote arguments; validate on the server. Don't accept price from the client; look it up from a server-side table keyed by item name. ([[2023-02-03 - Creating Proper Anti-Exploits The Ultimate Guide|Creating Proper Anti-Exploits: The Ultimate Guide]])
- 2023-02-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-proper-anti-exploits-the-ultimate-guide/2172882) Check argument types (e.g. type(ItemName) == "string") and existence (Items[ItemName]) before use so exploiters can't error your script. ([[2023-02-03 - Creating Proper Anti-Exploits The Ultimate Guide|Creating Proper Anti-Exploits: The Ultimate Guide]])
- 2023-02-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-proper-anti-exploits-the-ultimate-guide/2172882) Verify balances on the server against server-side data (leaderstats value check) before deducting or granting items. ([[2023-02-03 - Creating Proper Anti-Exploits The Ultimate Guide|Creating Proper Anti-Exploits: The Ultimate Guide]])
- 2023-02-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-proper-anti-exploits-the-ultimate-guide/2172882) Use the client for convenience but don't rely on it solely for security; exploiters fully control their client. ([[2023-02-03 - Creating Proper Anti-Exploits The Ultimate Guide|Creating Proper Anti-Exploits: The Ultimate Guide]])
- 2023-02-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-proper-anti-exploits-the-ultimate-guide/2172882) Guard against false positives: laggy players get server-side teleported, so teleport checks need tolerance and proper measures. ([[2023-02-03 - Creating Proper Anti-Exploits The Ultimate Guide|Creating Proper Anti-Exploits: The Ultimate Guide]])
- 2023-02-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-proper-anti-exploits-the-ultimate-guide/2172882) For teleport detection, ignore the Y axis, sample position twice over ~1 second, and flag only if the horizontal distance exceeds WalkSpeed + 3 studs. ([[2023-02-03 - Creating Proper Anti-Exploits The Ultimate Guide|Creating Proper Anti-Exploits: The Ultimate Guide]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Never trust the client; store sensitive data and purchase/economy logic on server-side scripts only. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Validate every purchase/reward server-side (check balance before deducting and granting). Client-side checks are trivially bypassed. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Secure RemoteEvents/RemoteFunctions against RemoteSpy-style logging and firing; validate all arguments on the server. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Build a server-side anti-cheat rather than a client-side one, since client scripts can be deleted. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Treat honeypots as a basic supplement only: fake remotes with tempting names (e.g. GiveMoney) that ban on fire, with a fake 'game closed for update' message. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Avoid free models/plugins where possible; check for require(), getfenv(), obfuscated code and low like/dislike ratios before using them to avoid backdoors. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Obfuscate shipped code by renaming variables/functions and removing comments, but expect performance cost; test for lag after obfuscating. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Use HTTPS for any external API or website calls from game code. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Keep loadstring() disabled unless strictly needed; if enabled, secure it heavily. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-04-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/exploiting-and-how-to-prevent-it/2304937) Keep FilteringEnabled on (default); it filters server actions so clients cannot directly change server state. ([[2023-04-20 - Exploiting and how to prevent it|Exploiting and how to prevent it]])
- 2023-07-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-anti-cheats-arent-as-bad-as-you-think/2471974) Treat the client as a secondary defence; keep the server as the main security focus and validate everything there. - 2023-07-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-anti-cheats-arent-as-bad-as-you-think/2471974) Hide the client anti-cheat script by setting getfenv().script to nil after destroying it so it does not appear in Dex or getnilinstances(). ([[2023-07-16 - Client Anti Cheats Aren't as bad as you think!|Client Anti Cheats: Aren't as bad as you think!]])
- 2023-07-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-anti-cheats-arent-as-bad-as-you-think/2471974) Implement a handshake: client fires a RemoteEvent every few seconds; server kicks players whose last handshake is older than the timeout (example uses 30s). ([[2023-07-16 - Client Anti Cheats Aren't as bad as you think!|Client Anti Cheats: Aren't as bad as you think!]])
- 2023-07-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-anti-cheats-arent-as-bad-as-you-think/2471974) Use keys and encryption in handshakes so exploiters cannot simply replicate the FireServer loop; a plain FireServer is only a demo. - 2023-07-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-anti-cheats-arent-as-bad-as-you-think/2471974) Combine client anti-cheat with server anti-cheat; the combination is stronger than either alone. - 2023-07-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/client-anti-cheats-arent-as-bad-as-you-think/2471974) Consider tamper checks such as property spoofing and namecall tampering detection for further client protection. - 2023-08-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/securing-your-anticheat-common-bad-practices-and-how-powerful-are-exploiters-guide-on-handshakes/2519952) Avoid detections based on rawequal, rawget or rawset on the client unless inside a metatable context; exploiters can hook them to return spoofed results.
