- 2021-12-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/using-janitor-to-combat-memory-leaks/1601710) Use Janitor per player, per instance, or per script/tool to group everything that must be cleaned together. ([[2021-12-25 - Using Janitor to combat memory leaks|Using Janitor to combat memory leaks]])
- 2022-08-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732) Don't write your own DataStore module unless you have proper security; use a proven one like ProfileService or DataStore2. ([[2022-08-17 - ProfileService Tutorial!|ProfileService Tutorial!]])
- 2022-08-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732) Define all default fields in ProfileTemplate; updating the template won't add missing values to existing player profiles. ([[2022-08-17 - ProfileService Tutorial!|ProfileService Tutorial!]])
- 2022-08-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732) Call profile:Reconcile() after loading to fill in template fields missing from older saves. ([[2022-08-17 - ProfileService Tutorial!|ProfileService Tutorial!]])
- 2022-08-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732) Call profile:Release() in PlayerRemoving and kick players in ListenToRelease, since the profile may be loaded on another server. ([[2022-08-17 - ProfileService Tutorial!|ProfileService Tutorial!]])
- 2022-08-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732) Kick the player if LoadProfileAsync returns nil, which usually means another server is loading the same profile. ([[2022-08-17 - ProfileService Tutorial!|ProfileService Tutorial!]])
- 2022-08-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732) Call profile:AddUserId(player.UserId) for GDPR compliance. ([[2022-08-17 - ProfileService Tutorial!|ProfileService Tutorial!]])
- 2022-08-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732) Handle players who leave before their profile finishes loading by releasing the profile immediately. ([[2022-08-17 - ProfileService Tutorial!|ProfileService Tutorial!]])
- 2022-08-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732) When migrating from an old DataStore, load the old data for joining players into their profile, then delete the old record; remove the old code later. ([[2022-08-17 - ProfileService Tutorial!|ProfileService Tutorial!]])
- 2022-08-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profileservice-tutorial/1926732) If you already use DataStore2 and it works, there is no need to switch to ProfileService. ([[2022-08-17 - ProfileService Tutorial!|ProfileService Tutorial!]])
- 2023-07-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-best-and-easiest-way-to-save-and-load-data/2494650) Use ProfileService (ProfileStore) rather than raw DataStores to load and save player data safely. ([[2023-07-30 - The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data|The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data]])
- 2023-07-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-best-and-easiest-way-to-save-and-load-data/2494650) Keep a Template module defining default stats (e.g. Coins, Gems, Rebirths) so new players start with known values. ([[2023-07-30 - The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data|The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data]])
- 2023-07-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-best-and-easiest-way-to-save-and-load-data/2494650) Change the ProfileStore key to a production name before launch; changing it later makes existing data appear lost, but reverting recovers it. ([[2023-07-30 - The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data|The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data]])
- 2023-07-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-best-and-easiest-way-to-save-and-load-data/2494650) Key each profile by player UserId and kick players whose profile fails to load, rather than letting them play without saved data. ([[2023-07-30 - The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data|The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data]])
- 2023-07-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-best-and-easiest-way-to-save-and-load-data/2494650) Loop through players already in the server on startup so profiles load for players who joined before the script ran. ([[2023-07-30 - The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data|The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data]])
- 2023-07-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-best-and-easiest-way-to-save-and-load-data/2494650) Listen for PlayerRemoving and release the profile to prevent data leaks. ([[2023-07-30 - The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data|The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data]])
- 2023-07-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-best-and-easiest-way-to-save-and-load-data/2494650) Enable Studio Access to API Services in game settings before testing DataStore-based saving. ([[2023-07-30 - The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data|The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data]])
- 2023-10-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datakeep-a-promise-based-auto-saving-datastore-module/2656200) Use a promise-based DataStore wrapper so you know exactly when a profile load or save has finished, instead of awkward async waiting. ([[2023-10-22 - DataKeep - A promise based, auto-saving DataStore module|DataKeep - A promise based, auto-saving DataStore ]])
- 2023-10-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datakeep-a-promise-based-auto-saving-datastore-module/2656200) Use a DataStore module with session locking so a player's data isn't written by two servers at once. ([[2023-10-22 - DataKeep - A promise based, auto-saving DataStore module|DataKeep - A promise based, auto-saving DataStore ]])
- 2023-10-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/datakeep-a-promise-based-auto-saving-datastore-module/2656200) Spread save calls evenly over time rather than saving all players at once. ([[2023-10-22 - DataKeep - A promise based, auto-saving DataStore module|DataKeep - A promise based, auto-saving DataStore ]])
- 2023-10-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-write-great-code-and-speed-up-everything/2666661) Use Promises for loading player data; discard or cancel the promise if the player leaves so callbacks never run. ([[2023-10-29 - How to write great code and speed up everything|How to write great code and speed up everything]])
- 2023-11-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585) Load player data via ProfileService in a DataManager module, expose it through a dictionary for server scripts, and have clients request data via remotes rather than reading it directly. ([[2023-11-02 - Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide|Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide]])
- 2023-11-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585) Save purchase receipts (PurchaseId) in a PurchaseLog within player data to guard against duplicate processing. ([[2023-11-02 - Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide|Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide]])
- 2023-11-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983) Initialize the host DataStore key once (SetAsync with an empty table) before the queue system reads it, or GetAsync will return nil. ([[2023-11-19 - How to make a cross-server matchmaking system Full Tutorial|How to make a cross-server matchmaking system | Fu]])
- 2023-11-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-luau-buffer-type-beta/2724894) Do not store buffer.tostring output in DataStores; it often contains non-UTF8 bytes. Later updates added DataStore support. ([[2023-11-30 - Introducing Luau buffer type Beta|Introducing Luau buffer type [Beta]]])
- 2024-01-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lets-teach-an-ai-to-catch-exploiters/2783460) Record player positions once per second on the server and save them; exporting large data causes lag, so use a better export method on weak connections. ([[2024-01-07 - Let's teach an AI to catch exploiters!|Let's teach an AI to catch exploiters!]])
- 2024-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-ban-api-and-alt-account-detection/3039740) Keep your own ban database if you need cross-experience or bulk lists. The API only exposes per-experience data and per-user moderation history. ([[2024-06-25 - Introducing the Ban API and Alt Account Detection|Introducing the Ban API and Alt Account Detection]])
- 2024-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/documentservice-a-powerful-schematised-datastore-library/3146934) Use a schema-validated data library so unsavable data errors before publishing rather than failing silently in production. ([[2024-09-05 - DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStore library|DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStor]])
- 2024-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/documentservice-a-powerful-schematised-datastore-library/3146934) Use migrations to change data format over time and mark changes as not backwards compatible, instead of only adding new fields. ([[2024-09-05 - DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStore library|DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStor]])
- 2024-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/documentservice-a-powerful-schematised-datastore-library/3146934) Validate data both when saving and when loading to catch manual edits or corruption that would otherwise cause odd side effects. ([[2024-09-05 - DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStore library|DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStor]])
- 2024-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/documentservice-a-powerful-schematised-datastore-library/3146934) Implement GlobalUpdates by checking if the player is in the current server and using IsOpenAvailable; otherwise update via MessagingService from the server holding the data. ([[2024-09-05 - DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStore library|DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStor]])
- 2024-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/documentservice-a-powerful-schematised-datastore-library/3146934) Use a non-session-locked document for shared data such as groups or houses that need multi-server editing. ([[2024-09-05 - DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStore library|DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStor]])
- 2024-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/documentservice-a-powerful-schematised-datastore-library/3146934) When migrating an existing game, open documents on keys that already hold data; the library will reuse and reformat existing keys. ([[2024-09-05 - DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStore library|DocumentService - A powerful, schematised DataStor]])
- 2024-10-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543) Use one profile per player with a session lock so data is not accessed by multiple servers at once, preventing item dupes in trading games. ([[2024-10-11 - ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataStore Module)|ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataSto]])
- 2024-10-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543) Call profile:Reconcile() after StartSessionAsync and set defaults in the PROFILE_TEMPLATE so new keys are filled in and nil checks are unnecessary. ([[2024-10-11 - ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataStore Module)|ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataSto]])
- 2024-10-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543) Call profile:AddUserId(player.UserId) on load for GDPR compliance. ([[2024-10-11 - ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataStore Module)|ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataSto]])
- 2024-10-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543) Pass a Cancel function to StartSessionAsync that returns true when the player has left, so loads for departed players stop early. ([[2024-10-11 - ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataStore Module)|ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataSto]])
- 2024-10-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543) Call profile:EndSession() in PlayerRemoving and kick players on OnSessionEnd so stale sessions are not kept. ([[2024-10-11 - ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataStore Module)|ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataSto]])
- 2024-10-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543) Use ProfileStore:MessageAsync() for cross-server data writes such as player gifting instead of writing to DataStores directly. ([[2024-10-11 - ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataStore Module)|ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataSto]])
- 2024-10-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543) Do not use ProfileStore for leaderboards or global state; it is designed only for per-player data. ([[2024-10-11 - ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataStore Module)|ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataSto]])
- 2024-10-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/profilestore-save-your-player-data-easy-datastore-module/3190543) Test migration from ProfileService in Studio with API access before deploying live, since old ProfileService may fail to reload profiles after MessageAsync is used. ([[2024-10-11 - ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataStore Module)|ProfileStore - Save your player data easy (DataSto]])
- 2024-10-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replica-server-to-client-state-replication-module/3216980) Use ProfileStore (DataStore wrapper) for player data persistence; Replica is for replicating state, not saving it. ([[2024-10-16 - Replica - Server to client state replication (Module)|Replica - Server to client state replication (Modu]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157) Store synchronized game state (health, ammo) in Attributes on predicted Instances so it rolls back on misprediction; plain Luau variables are not rolled back. ([[2025-12-09 - Studio Beta Build Fair, Responsive Games with Server Authority|[Studio Beta] Build Fair, Responsive Games with Se]])
- 2026-05-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/protect-your-games-with-script-capabilities-sandboxing/4634642) Sandboxed scripts cannot require unsandboxed ModuleScripts; keep shared modules sandboxed or move them under a matching-capability container. ([[2026-05-13 - Protect Your Games with Script Capabilities Sandboxing|Protect Your Games with Script Capabilities Sandbo]])
- 2026-07-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993) Attributes are limited to 64 per instance with limited string size under Next Generation Replication; plan data accordingly. ([[2026-07-09 - Full Release Ship Fair And Competitive Games with Server Authority|[Full Release] Ship Fair And Competitive Games wit]])
- 2026-07-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905) Make migrations fail closed: if any migration step throws, do not stamp or save, so half-migrated profiles never persist. ([[2026-07-15 - Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated player data built o|Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated]])
- 2026-07-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905) Enable a wipe guard (WipeGuardPolicy = "Block") to hold saves when top-level keys vanish or payload collapses, persisting the last good snapshot. ([[2026-07-15 - Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated player data built o|Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated]])
- 2026-07-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905) Set SaveInterval no lower than 15 seconds; autosave intervals are capped at 300 seconds in v2.6.0. ([[2026-07-15 - Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated player data built o|Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated]])
- 2026-07-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905) Name your own ProfileStoreIndex so test builds never share live DataStore storage. ([[2026-07-15 - Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated player data built o|Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated]])
- 2026-07-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/scribe-v261-persistent-typed-auto-replicated-player-data-built-on-profilestore/4738905) Use AwaitBudget when migrating from several stores so per-player reads stay within DataStore request budget and don't throttle. ([[2026-07-15 - Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated player data built o|Scribe v2.6.1 - Persistent, typed, auto-replicated]])
- 2026-08-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collections-how-to-group-instances-with-query-patterns/4809143) Use Atomic Streaming Modes with collections so callbacks only run on streamed-in instances, avoiding WaitForChild. ([[2026-08-13 - Collections How to group instances with query patterns|Collections: How to group instances with query pat]])

## [[matchmaking-and-teleport]]
- 2019-03-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/cross-server-matchmaking-module/255142) Use a dedicated matchmaking host server that holds the queue; a re-host loses the queue, so notify players to rejoin. ([[2019-03-21 - Cross-Server Matchmaking Module|Cross-Server Matchmaking Module]])
- 2019-03-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/cross-server-matchmaking-module/255142) Avoid auto-queuing players; it sends many queue requests simultaneously. Manual queue requests are the recommended approach. ([[2019-03-21 - Cross-Server Matchmaking Module|Cross-Server Matchmaking Module]])
- 2019-03-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/cross-server-matchmaking-module/255142) Set matchmakingServerId to the PlaceId of the match place; matchmaking conditions are configurable. ([[2019-03-21 - Cross-Server Matchmaking Module|Cross-Server Matchmaking Module]])
- 2020-02-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584) Use GetPlayerFromCharacter instead of searching Players by name when you have a character and need its Player. ([[2020-02-14 - Creating a battle system OOP style!|Creating a battle system: OOP style!]])
- 2023-11-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983) Only one server should confirm queue matches; elect a host server per game mode via a DataStore key so 100 servers don't all try to teleport the same players. ([[2023-11-19 - How to make a cross-server matchmaking system Full Tutorial|How to make a cross-server matchmaking system | Fu]])
- 2023-11-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983) Use MemoryStoreService sorted maps for cross-server queues; read in Descending order so the oldest-queued players are matched first. ([[2023-11-19 - How to make a cross-server matchmaking system Full Tutorial|How to make a cross-server matchmaking system | Fu]])
- 2023-11-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983) Use MessagingService to tell other servers when host data changes and to send teleport instructions for players not in the host server. ([[2023-11-19 - How to make a cross-server matchmaking system Full Tutorial|How to make a cross-server matchmaking system | Fu]])
- 2023-11-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983) Use TeleportService:ReserveServer then TeleportToPrivateServer to move matched groups into a fresh server; check players are still in the game before teleporting. ([[2023-11-19 - How to make a cross-server matchmaking system Full Tutorial|How to make a cross-server matchmaking system | Fu]])
- 2023-11-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-cross-server-matchmaking-system-full-tutorial/2707983) Remove players from the MemoryStore queue on PlayerRemoving so departed players are not matched into a round. ([[2023-11-19 - How to make a cross-server matchmaking system Full Tutorial|How to make a cross-server matchmaking system | Fu]])
- 2025-11-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/custom-matchmaking-is-now-available-to-all-experiences-on-roblox/4051419) Adjust only a few matchmaking signal weights, a little at a time, to avoid making matchmaking too restrictive. ([[2025-11-06 - Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experiences on Roblox|Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experie]])
- 2025-11-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/custom-matchmaking-is-now-available-to-all-experiences-on-roblox/4051419) Prioritise a few signals. Weights are relative across all signals, so raising many weights dilutes the important ones. ([[2025-11-06 - Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experiences on Roblox|Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experie]])
- 2025-11-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/custom-matchmaking-is-now-available-to-all-experiences-on-roblox/4051419) For competitive games, store a skill rating in a DataStore, expose it as a custom signal, and weight it to match similar-skill players. ([[2025-11-06 - Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experiences on Roblox|Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experie]])
- 2025-11-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/custom-matchmaking-is-now-available-to-all-experiences-on-roblox/4051419) Raise the Latency signal weight if your game needs responsive server-to-client replication. ([[2025-11-06 - Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experiences on Roblox|Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experie]])
- 2025-11-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/custom-matchmaking-is-now-available-to-all-experiences-on-roblox/4051419) Raise the Language signal weight for co-op or social games where players need to chat and coordinate. ([[2025-11-06 - Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experiences on Roblox|Custom Matchmaking is now available to all experie]])
- 2026-03-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/voice-chat-is-now-supported-in-servers-of-up-to-100-users/4534031) If your experience relies heavily on voice, use the custom matchmaking Age signal. Only users in the same age group can chat, so this raises the odds of voice-compatible server mates. ([[2026-03-24 - Voice Chat is now supported in servers of up to 100 users!|Voice Chat is now supported in servers of up to 10]])

## [[physics-pathfinding-and-npc-ai]]
- 2018-09-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/modeling-a-projectiles-motion/176677) Humanoid.JumpPower acts as initial velocity: jump height h = JumpPower^2/(2*g), airtime = 2*JumpPower/g; JumpPower = sqrt(2*g*h) for a target height. ([[2018-09-07 - Modeling a projectile's motion|Modeling a projectile's motion]])
- 2018-11-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-furniture-placement-system/205509) Set a Touched connection before calling GetTouchingParts(), since parts need a touch interest for GetTouchingParts to return results. ([[2018-11-26 - Creating A Furniture Placement System|Creating A Furniture Placement System]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) CanCollide only matters for moving objects near players. For reachable details, set CanCollide false and use a hitbox; unreachable details matter little. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-03-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/2-joint-2-limb-inverse-kinematics/252399) Solve 2-joint IK with the law of cosines: c = distance shoulder-to-goal, then A and C angles from arm lengths a, b and c. ([[2019-03-15 - 2 Joint 2 Limb Inverse Kinematics|2 Joint 2 Limb Inverse Kinematics]])
- 2019-03-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/2-joint-2-limb-inverse-kinematics/252399) Clamp unreachable targets: if distance < |l1-l2|, push shoulder back and fold arm; if distance > l1+l2, push shoulder forward and fully extend. ([[2019-03-15 - 2 Joint 2 Limb Inverse Kinematics|2 Joint 2 Limb Inverse Kinematics]])
- 2019-03-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/2-joint-2-limb-inverse-kinematics/252399) Use a rolled base plane (axis from cross product of Vector3.new(0,0,-1) and goal direction) instead of CFrame.new(shoulder, goal) for natural shoulder/hip rotation. ([[2019-03-15 - 2 Joint 2 Limb Inverse Kinematics|2 Joint 2 Limb Inverse Kinematics]])
- 2019-11-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801) Consider TweenService instead of Humanoid:MoveTo() for NPC movement visuals, moving the cost to the client; the author notes this is tricky to implement. ([[2019-11-17 - How to Optimize Humanoids|How to Optimize Humanoids]])
- 2019-11-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801) Keep NPC AI as simple ('dumb') as possible; more advanced AI costs more resources, especially with many NPCs. ([[2019-11-17 - How to Optimize Humanoids|How to Optimize Humanoids]])
- 2019-12-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dragging-objects-with-the-mouse/403766) Use AlignPosition rather than setting Position each frame for smoother physically-driven dragging. ([[2019-12-08 - Dragging objects with the mouse|Dragging objects with the mouse]])
- 2020-04-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-rig-a-car/523855) Pick up a car model first, then export and assemble it in Studio before rigging; the series assumes a 3D-modeled car as the starting point. ([[2020-04-14 - How to Rig a Car|How to Rig a Car]])
- 2020-04-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-rig-a-car/523855) Set up attachments and constraints (e.g. hinges/springs on wheels) before writing any vehicle scripts, so physics behaviour is established first. ([[2020-04-14 - How to Rig a Car|How to Rig a Car]])
- 2020-05-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/perspective-object-dragging-like-superliminal/566200) Offset the part back by half its size along the ray to stop it clipping into nearby geometry. ([[2020-05-09 - Perspective object dragging like Superliminal|Perspective object dragging like Superliminal]])
- 2021-01-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pid-control-pt-1-proportional-control/968881) For a rolling ball, apply VectorForce = kP * (desiredVelocity - currentVelocity) each frame; example uses kP=300 and top speed 30. ([[2021-01-07 - PID control pt. 1 Proportional Control|PID control pt. 1: Proportional Control]])
- 2021-01-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/pid-control-pt-1-proportional-control/968881) P-only control overshoots and oscillates without dampening; add a D term (part 2) or rely on friction. Setting kP to 3000 on the ball example makes it yeet off. ([[2021-01-07 - PID control pt. 1 Proportional Control|PID control pt. 1: Proportional Control]])
- 2021-02-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/justice-the-awesome-npc-system/1026927) Tag walkable areas with Justice:Pathfindable so NPCs wander there automatically; use Justice:Deadzone labels in a PathfindingModifier to exclude areas. ([[2021-02-05 - Justice, the awesome NPC system|Justice, the awesome NPC system]])
- 2021-02-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/justice-the-awesome-npc-system/1026927) Limit NPC counts in live games; the author says running 500 NPCs causes visible issues, though 1000 ran locally with only a performance hit. ([[2021-02-05 - Justice, the awesome NPC system|Justice, the awesome NPC system]])
- 2021-03-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-a-mesh-deformation-ocean/1122587) Run wave formula calculations on the client; running them on the server is jittery and slows the server down. ([[2021-03-21 - Making A Mesh Deformation Ocean|Making A Mesh Deformation Ocean]])
- 2021-03-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/fps-using-viewmodels-the-improved-version-parts-2-out-of-3/1129877) Put ViewModel parts in their own collision group so they don't collide with the player's world or tools. ([[2021-03-25 - FPS using ViewModels (The improved version) Parts 2 out of 3|FPS using ViewModels (The improved version) // Par]])
- 2021-10-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nature2d-2d-physics-engine-for-ui-elements/1510935) Nature2D simulates RigidBodies from UI elements; use it for 2D physics since Roblox has no built-in 2D physics engine. ([[2021-10-16 - Nature2D - 2D Physics Engine for UI Elements|Nature2D - 2D Physics Engine for UI Elements]])
- 2021-10-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nature2d-2d-physics-engine-for-ui-elements/1510935) Use Constraints between Points to build ropes, rods, bridges, chains; points can be connected across bodies or individually. ([[2021-10-16 - Nature2D - 2D Physics Engine for UI Elements|Nature2D - 2D Physics Engine for UI Elements]])
- 2021-10-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nature2d-2d-physics-engine-for-ui-elements/1510935) RigidBodies currently cannot have different masses; plan designs around uniform mass. ([[2021-10-16 - Nature2D - 2D Physics Engine for UI Elements|Nature2D - 2D Physics Engine for UI Elements]])
- 2022-02-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/my-intro-to-raycasting-what-why-and-how/1681977) Use raycasts to stop projectiles or movement from passing through walls. ([[2022-02-26 - My Intro to Raycasting - WHAT, WHY, and HOW|My Intro to Raycasting - WHAT, WHY, and HOW]])
- 2022-04-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/benis-pathfinding-customizable-easy-to-use-auto-animating-r6-r15-support/1759994) Use requiresSight to make the NPC only chase targets it can see; this also reduces the effective maxDistance. ([[2022-04-18 - Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & Auto-Animating (R6 &|Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & A]])
- 2022-04-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/benis-pathfinding-customizable-easy-to-use-auto-animating-r6-r15-support/1759994) Set stopNearPlayer so the NPC halts within 3 studs of its target instead of pushing into them. ([[2022-04-18 - Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & Auto-Animating (R6 &|Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & A]])
- 2022-04-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/benis-pathfinding-customizable-easy-to-use-auto-animating-r6-r15-support/1759994) Enable moveIfNoPath only with caution: it moves the NPC 15 studs away when path.Status is not Success and may break movement if the target is unreachable. ([[2022-04-18 - Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & Auto-Animating (R6 &|Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & A]])
- 2022-04-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/benis-pathfinding-customizable-easy-to-use-auto-animating-r6-r15-support/1759994) Disable moveToWait for smoother movement, but expect broken pathfinding as a trade-off. ([[2022-04-18 - Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & Auto-Animating (R6 &|Benis' Pathfinding - Customizable, Easy to Use & A]])
- 2022-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simple-pathfinding-ai/1815347) Set the NPC's PrimaryPart network owner to nil (SetNetworkOwner(nil)) so the server controls NPC movement instead of a client. ([[2022-05-29 - Simple pathfinding ai|Simple pathfinding ai]])
- 2022-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simple-pathfinding-ai/1815347) Compute paths with PathfindingService:CreatePath() then path:ComputeAsync(start, destination); walk the result from path:GetWaypoints(). ([[2022-05-29 - Simple pathfinding ai|Simple pathfinding ai]])
- 2022-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simple-pathfinding-ai/1815347) When a waypoint's Action is Enum.PathWaypointAction.Jump, set humanoid.Jump = true before calling MoveTo on that waypoint. ([[2022-05-29 - Simple pathfinding ai|Simple pathfinding ai]])
- 2022-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simple-pathfinding-ai/1815347) Wait on humanoid.MoveToFinished between waypoints so each step completes before the next MoveTo. ([[2022-05-29 - Simple pathfinding ai|Simple pathfinding ai]])
- 2022-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simple-pathfinding-ai/1815347) Predicting target position by adding HumanoidRootPart.Velocity.Unit * 7 causes visible twitching; tune the lead distance (the author's 7 studs) or drop it. ([[2022-05-29 - Simple pathfinding ai|Simple pathfinding ai]])
- 2022-07-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779) Set AgentRadius above default 2 to keep a minimum separation of (value - 2) studs from obstacles. ([[2022-07-03 - How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0|How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0]])
- 2022-07-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779) Set Costs multipliers below 1 (e.g. Metal = 0.1) to make pathfinding prefer those materials; above 1 to avoid them. ([[2022-07-03 - How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0|How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0]])
- 2022-07-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779) Use a Costs value of math.huge on a PathfindingModifier label to exclude a region from paths entirely. ([[2022-07-03 - How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0|How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0]])
- 2022-07-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779) Use a PathfindingModifier with PassThrough enabled (non-collidable, transparent part) to mark traversable regions such as hinged doors without setting a cost. ([[2022-07-03 - How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0|How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0]])
- 2022-07-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779) Use PathfindingLink with attachments to make humanoids traverse gaps (e.g. jumps) that ComputeAsync cannot find on its own, and trigger custom events on those waypoints. ([[2022-07-03 - How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0|How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0]])
- 2022-07-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779) Setting WaypointSpacing to math.huge yields the fewest waypoints for the shortest path, but it breaks the MoveToFinished reached-within-8s check. ([[2022-07-03 - How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0|How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0]])
- 2022-10-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nextpath-next-gen-script-for-nextbots/2030360) Use a hybrid chase: SimplePath pathfinding when outside RunDistance (default 20 studs), then Humanoid:MoveTo() inside it for a clear, direct approach. ([[2022-10-22 - NextPath Next gen script for Nextbots|NextPath: Next gen script for Nextbots]])
- 2022-10-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nextpath-next-gen-script-for-nextbots/2030360) Use raycast visibility checks to pick targets so NPCs only chase players they could realistically see; disable the check (VisibilityCheck=false) to queue all players. ([[2022-10-22 - NextPath Next gen script for Nextbots|NextPath: Next gen script for Nextbots]])
- 2022-10-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nextpath-next-gen-script-for-nextbots/2030360) Tune Rate (default 0.1 s tick) and MaxDistance (default 500 studs) to control NPC thinking cost; the author did not performance-test the script. ([[2022-10-22 - NextPath Next gen script for Nextbots|NextPath: Next gen script for Nextbots]])
- 2022-10-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nextpath-next-gen-script-for-nextbots/2030360) Give NPCs a jump mode: if a target is VerticalJumpDistance (default 20 studs) above, wait MaxJumpTicks (default 20) before jumping. ([[2022-10-22 - NextPath Next gen script for Nextbots|NextPath: Next gen script for Nextbots]])
- 2023-03-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571) Give enemies behaviour patterns beyond wandering; avoid enemies that look like standard Roblox avatars. ([[2023-03-09 - Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences|Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences]])
- 2023-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333) Use hitboxes that only run when players enter or leave an area, rather than constant per-frame player detection. ([[2023-09-09 - All about optimization|All about optimization]])
- 2023-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333) Delete the Humanoid from NPCs that do not move; for many NPCs, move them with constraints or CFrame instead of MoveTo and use BillboardGui health bars. ([[2023-09-09 - All about optimization|All about optimization]])
- 2024-03-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/howto-physics-based-tweenservice/2873727) Set AssemblyLinearVelocity = (newPos - oldPos) / deltaTime and AssemblyAngularVelocity from the rotation delta on each part of the moving model. ([[2024-03-12 - HowTo Physics-Based TweenService|HowTo: Physics-Based TweenService]])
- 2024-03-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/mass-uncopylocked-35-free-games-and-projects/2880269) Constraints (e.g. train in Vehicle Testing) may not replicate from server to client; test vehicle constraints in multiplayer. ([[2024-03-18 - Mass Uncopylocked 35 free games and projects|Mass Uncopylocked | 35 free games and projects]])
- 2024-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-use-robloxs-physics-character-controllers/3092097) Set ControllerPartSensor.SensorUpdateType to Manual and feed it your own raycast each frame (SensedPart, HitNormal, HitFrame). ([[2024-07-29 - How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Controllers|How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Con]])
- 2024-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-use-robloxs-physics-character-controllers/3092097) Also write GroundSensor.HitFrame = CFrame.new(hit.Position); the docs omit it and characters can't climb slopes without it. ([[2024-07-29 - How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Controllers|How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Con]])
- 2024-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-use-robloxs-physics-character-controllers/3092097) Use a downward raycast of about 4.5 studs from HumanoidRootPart to decide Ground vs Air controller; switch to AirController when it misses. ([[2024-07-29 - How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Controllers|How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Con]])
- 2025-05-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-pathfinding-chase/3648450) Use distance-based update rates for pathfinding NPCs so distant chasers recompute paths less often, saving performance. ([[2025-05-13 - Smooth Pathfinding Chase|Smooth Pathfinding Chase]])
- 2025-05-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-pathfinding-chase/3648450) Use a data-oriented design so one system can drive many NPCs simultaneously instead of per-NPC heavy logic. ([[2025-05-13 - Smooth Pathfinding Chase|Smooth Pathfinding Chase]])
- 2025-05-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-pathfinding-chase/3648450) Handle jumps explicitly in async pathfinding movement; the module's jump handling is a known requirement for natural chase movement. ([[2025-05-13 - Smooth Pathfinding Chase|Smooth Pathfinding Chase]])
- 2025-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628) Use VectorForce with RelativeTo World and raycast-based springs instead of SpringConstraints for more control over suspension behaviour. ([[2025-09-03 - In Depth Scripted Car Physics|In Depth Scripted Car Physics]])
- 2025-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628) Set wheel parts Anchored=false, CanCollide=false, CanQuery=false, CanTouch=false so visual wheels don't bounce the car. ([[2025-09-03 - In Depth Scripted Car Physics|In Depth Scripted Car Physics]])
- 2025-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628) Set damping roughly 10x lower than stiffness as a starting point (example: STIFFNESS 10, DAMPING 1), then tune by testing. ([[2025-09-03 - In Depth Scripted Car Physics|In Depth Scripted Car Physics]])
- 2025-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628) Raycast down only to rest length plus wheel radius, not infinity, so the car isn't flung when driving off ramps. ([[2025-09-03 - In Depth Scripted Car Physics|In Depth Scripted Car Physics]])
- 2025-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628) Exclude the whole car model in RaycastParams and set RespectCanCollide=true so wheels don't snap to the car body or walls. ([[2025-09-03 - In Depth Scripted Car Physics|In Depth Scripted Car Physics]])
- 2025-09-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/in-depth-scripted-car-physics/3915628) Reset each VectorForce to zero when the wheel has no ground hit, otherwise the car keeps floating away. ([[2025-09-03 - In Depth Scripted Car Physics|In Depth Scripted Car Physics]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-how-to-begin/4139185) Use RunService:GetPredictionStatus to debug which vehicle/physics instances are predicted when some are and some aren't. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority How to Begin|Server Authority: How to Begin?]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321) Keep mechanisms simple (one assembly) under Server Authority; multi-assembly mechanisms like suspension cars will jitter more in this beta. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engineers!|Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engin]])
- 2026-01-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561) Set IsKinematic=False on joints to get force/torque-driven behavior; kinematic joints stay locked to animation, as with Motor6D. ([[2026-01-26 - Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Character Movement is No|Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Char]])
- 2026-01-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561) Disable or weaken AnimationConstraints and disable CharacterBreakJointsOnDeath to create immediate realistic ragdolls on death. ([[2026-01-26 - Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Character Movement is No|Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Char]])
- 2026-01-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561) R6 is not covered by the upgrade; add BallSocketConstraints and AnimationConstraints via script if needed. ([[2026-01-26 - Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Character Movement is No|Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Char]])
- 2026-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904) The controller is physics-based (ControllerManager, 240hz constraints) so ice-friction and momentum behave per floor material; account for this in level design. ([[2026-02-18 - Studio Beta AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-Based Character Control|[Studio Beta] AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-B]])
- 2026-06-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211) Test on your game now by setting Workspace.ImprovedAnimationConstraint = Enabled; Phase 1 feedback shapes Phase 2/3 timing. ([[2026-06-23 - Improved AnimationConstraint Stable, Mass-Independent Tracking for Phy|Improved AnimationConstraint: Stable, Mass-Indepen]])
- 2026-06-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211) Strength/Damping properties only apply when IsKinematic = false; kinematic constraints are unchanged. ([[2026-06-23 - Improved AnimationConstraint Stable, Mass-Independent Tracking for Phy|Improved AnimationConstraint: Stable, Mass-Indepen]])
- 2026-06-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211) Pin AngularStrength = 1 (60 Hz) and tune AngularDamping upward rather than raising AngularStrength, which can cause overcorrection/wobble. ([[2026-06-23 - Improved AnimationConstraint Stable, Mass-Independent Tracking for Phy|Improved AnimationConstraint: Stable, Mass-Indepen]])
- 2026-06-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211) When a held object's effective inertia changes, scale upstream AngularDamping by the square root of the new/original inertia ratio (e.g., inertia x4 means damping x2). ([[2026-06-23 - Improved AnimationConstraint Stable, Mass-Independent Tracking for Phy|Improved AnimationConstraint: Stable, Mass-Indepen]])
- 2026-06-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211) For a root joint with a BallSocketConstraint handling position, set LinearStrength = 0 and drive orientation with angular parameters only. ([[2026-06-23 - Improved AnimationConstraint Stable, Mass-Independent Tracking for Phy|Improved AnimationConstraint: Stable, Mass-Indepen]])
- 2026-06-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211) Keep MaxForce and MaxTorque as hard limits; they still cap the resulting force and torque. ([[2026-06-23 - Improved AnimationConstraint Stable, Mass-Independent Tracking for Phy|Improved AnimationConstraint: Stable, Mass-Indepen]])
- 2026-06-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improved-animationconstraint-stable-mass-independent-tracking-for-physically-simulated-joints/4700211) Use damping ratio 1 for critically damped, below 1 for ringing/oscillation, above 1 for slow approach without overshoot. ([[2026-06-23 - Improved AnimationConstraint Stable, Mass-Independent Tracking for Phy|Improved AnimationConstraint: Stable, Mass-Indepen]])

## [[combat-and-gameplay-systems]]
- 2019-04-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895) Generate the full batch of spin items (e.g. 45) and pick the winner at a fixed index (e.g. 5 from the end) so the spinner looks like it has more to come. ([[2019-04-01 - Creating a CrateSpin System|Creating a Crate/Spin System]])
- 2019-04-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895) Make sure rarity chances sum to exactly 1, since weighted selection relies on cumulative chance. ([[2019-04-01 - Creating a CrateSpin System|Creating a Crate/Spin System]])
- 2019-04-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895) Convert duplicate items into a cash reward per rarity instead of granting them again, and remove owned items from the pool if you want new-item-only spins. ([[2019-04-01 - Creating a CrateSpin System|Creating a Crate/Spin System]])
- 2019-12-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dragging-objects-with-the-mouse/403766) Use Drag Detectors instead of hand-rolled mouse dragging; they replicate automatically and give good control. ([[2019-12-08 - Dragging objects with the mouse|Dragging objects with the mouse]])
- 2020-02-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584) Define creature, move and NPC data in separate ModuleScripts (moves as {amount, type} pairs) so balance changes need no logic edits. ([[2020-02-14 - Creating a battle system OOP style!|Creating a battle system: OOP style!]])
- 2020-02-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584) Use a simple NPC AI: heal if the attack would kill the NPC's creature, otherwise attack. Easy to extend later. ([[2020-02-14 - Creating a battle system OOP style!|Creating a battle system: OOP style!]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318) Use a fire loop with wait(60/rpm) and a canFire cooldown; shove recoil springs on fire and reverse after ~0.15s. ([[2020-03-31 - Writing an FPS framework (2020)|Writing an FPS framework (2020)]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318) Use FastCast for projectiles; the author noted it as the projectile library of choice. ([[2020-03-31 - Writing an FPS framework (2020)|Writing an FPS framework (2020)]])
- 2020-07-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753) Set CanTouch to false on parts that should be ignored by collision detection. ([[2020-07-31 - How to use Placement Service|How to use Placement Service]])
- 2020-08-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-raycasting-gun/723716) When a raycast returns nil, use origin + direction as the hit position so misses still show a bullet. ([[2020-08-14 - How to make a raycasting gun|How to make a raycasting gun]])
- 2020-08-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-raycasting-gun/723716) Check the hit part's parent and grandparent for a Humanoid so hits on nested models still register damage. ([[2020-08-14 - How to make a raycasting gun|How to make a raycasting gun]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912) Only build an anti-combat-log if dying carries a punishment (e.g. inventory loss); otherwise leaving mid-fight has no benefit to exploit. ([[2020-08-27 - How to Catch Combat Logging|How to Catch Combat Logging]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912) Tag the victim with the attacker's Player object (ObjectValue 'creator') immediately after server-side damage, clearing old tags first so only one exists. ([[2020-08-27 - How to Catch Combat Logging|How to Catch Combat Logging]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912) Use Debris:AddItem on tag instances to auto-expire them; instances are easier to clean up than tables. ([[2020-08-27 - How to Catch Combat Logging|How to Catch Combat Logging]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912) Store combat-log tags as named children in a ServerStorage folder; reset the timer by destroying and recreating the tag on each hit. ([[2020-08-27 - How to Catch Combat Logging|How to Catch Combat Logging]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912) Choose a combat-log window (the example uses 15 seconds) and make it the minimum time a tagged player must stay after being hit. ([[2020-08-27 - How to Catch Combat Logging|How to Catch Combat Logging]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-catch-combat-logging/743912) Wrap the combat-log tagging in a module and call it wherever the server applies damage, right after humanoid:TakeDamage. ([[2020-08-27 - How to Catch Combat Logging|How to Catch Combat Logging]])
- 2020-11-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/melee-hitboxes-what-is-the-best-solution-and-is-the-touched-event-really-that-bad/867477) For melee hitboxes, connect Touched on the server and set the weapon's network owner to the client (parent to character or SetNetworkOwner) so hits register despite ping. ([[2020-11-12 - Melee Hitboxes - What Is The Best Solution, and is the Touched event r|Melee Hitboxes - What Is The Best Solution, and is]])
- 2021-03-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/fps-using-viewmodels-the-improved-version-parts-2-out-of-3/1129877) Wait one frame before changing a tool's RightGrip Part0 to the ViewModel arm, since Roblox resets the weld if changed immediately. ([[2021-03-25 - FPS using ViewModels (The improved version) Parts 2 out of 3|FPS using ViewModels (The improved version) // Par]])
- 2021-04-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/camera-animations-for-cutscenes-and-gameplay/1181691) For attack cutscenes, animate the camera as normal, then in code set Camera.CFrame = reference part CFrame * frame CFrame, using HumanoidRootPart as the reference 99% of the time. ([[2021-04-22 - Camera Animations for cutscenes and gameplay!|Camera Animations for cutscenes and gameplay!]])
- 2021-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507) Raycast toward the bullet's predicted future position each frame; on hit, destroy the bullet and disconnect its loop. Skip this step if using FastCast. ([[2021-05-04 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner’s guide PART 2|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner’s guide [PART]])
- 2021-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507) Track ammo in a variable, reject shots at 0, decrement per shot, and refill only when the reload animation ends. ([[2021-05-04 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner’s guide PART 2|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner’s guide [PART]])
- 2021-09-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/open-source-lock-on-combat-in-3d/1458506) Lock-on targeting removes aim-by-camera skill, letting combat depth come from guard positioning, openings and timing instead of shiftlock positioning. ([[2021-09-07 - OPEN SOURCE Lock-On Combat in 3D|[OPEN SOURCE] Lock-On Combat in 3D]])
- 2021-09-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/open-source-lock-on-combat-in-3d/1458506) Lock-on combat is inherently 1v1-oriented; teaming is very difficult or impossible to balance with this system, so plan modes around it. ([[2021-09-07 - OPEN SOURCE Lock-On Combat in 3D|[OPEN SOURCE] Lock-On Combat in 3D]])
- 2021-12-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-making-proper-anti-exploits/1606949) For client-side hit detection, validate on server: range check with lag margin, attack cooldown via os.clock, and facing angle unless the attack is AoE. - 2022-01-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginners-guide-on-scripting-animefighting-vfxvisual-effects-part-1/1610853) Prevent multi-hit damage with a per-player debounce value inside the humanoid, named with the player name plus attack key, removed after 1-4 seconds via Debris. - 2022-01-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-beginners-guide-on-scripting-animefighting-vfxvisual-effects-part-1/1610853) Filter hitbox Touched events with IsDescendantOf so the caster never damages themselves. - 2022-01-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-2-characters-and-play-it-in-game-like-a-takedown-animation/1625809) Disable movement (WalkSpeed = 0) during a scripted animation and restore it after the main animation's Stopped event fires. ([[2022-01-13 - How to animate 2 characters and play it in-game like a takedown animat|How to animate 2 characters and play it in-game li]])
- 2022-02-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tools-the-easiest-and-best-way-for-melees/1654589) Redo the Motor6D swap every time the tool is equipped; delete the old Motor6D first to avoid stale grips. ([[2022-02-03 - How to animate tools (the easiest and best way) (for melees)|How to animate tools (the easiest and best way) (f]])
- 2022-02-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/my-intro-to-raycasting-what-why-and-how/1681977) Use workspace:Raycast for hitscan weapons; the speaker says it gives extra accuracy and is the basis for FastCast-style bullet physics. - 2022-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simple-pathfinding-ai/1815347) Check target.Humanoid.Health > 0 before following a target so the NPC stops chasing dead characters. ([[2022-05-29 - Simple pathfinding ai|Simple pathfinding ai]])
- 2022-07-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-roblox-pathfinding-service-20/1857779) Handle Path.Blocked by recomputing the path only when the blocked waypoint index is ahead of the current waypoint. ([[2022-07-03 - How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0|How To Use Roblox Pathfinding Service 2.0]])
- 2022-10-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/nextpath-next-gen-script-for-nextbots/2030360) Make NPC damage configurable (Damage=1000 default, set 0 to disable) so the same NPC can be a non-lethal or lethal threat. ([[2022-10-22 - NextPath Next gen script for Nextbots|NextPath: Next gen script for Nextbots]])
- 2023-03-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571) Avoid instant-kill enemies unless the player has tools to prevent it; otherwise let them hurt the player so near-misses are felt. ([[2023-03-09 - Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences|Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences]])
- 2023-07-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-best-and-easiest-way-to-save-and-load-data/2494650) Build leaderstats from the loaded profile data in a dedicated function called right after the profile is stored in the Manager. ([[2023-07-30 - The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data|The Best and Easiest Way To Save and Load Data]])
- 2024-01-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/hitboxclass-v11b-a-powerful-oop-based-hitbox-module/2790395) Use a hitbox module that handles client-sided hit detection and networking so you don't write remote plumbing for every attack. ([[2024-01-13 - HitboxClass v1.1B A Powerful OOP-based Hitbox Module|HitboxClass | v1.1B | A Powerful OOP-based Hitbox ]])
- 2024-01-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/hitboxclass-v11b-a-powerful-oop-based-hitbox-module/2790395) Set a DebounceTime per hitbox (e.g. 1 second) to stop the same character being hit repeatedly by one attack. ([[2024-01-13 - HitboxClass v1.1B A Powerful OOP-based Hitbox Module|HitboxClass | v1.1B | A Powerful OOP-based Hitbox ]])
- 2024-01-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/hitboxclass-v11b-a-powerful-oop-based-hitbox-module/2790395) Keep hit targets in a single folder (e.g. Alive) and only search that folder; otherwise nothing gets detected. - 2024-01-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/hitboxclass-v11b-a-powerful-oop-based-hitbox-module/2790395) Check the connected return value from HitboxClass.new; it is always true for server hitboxes but may be false for client hitboxes. - 2024-03-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/emilios-ragdoll-module-easy-ahh-ragdolls-r15-and-r6/2865426) Call TimedRagdoll(char, seconds) from a server Script for touch-triggered knockdowns; pass the character model and a duration. - 2024-03-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/emilios-ragdoll-module-easy-ahh-ragdolls-r15-and-r6/2865426) Use NpcRagdoll for NPCs and put a debounce on it; the author notes NPC ragdoll fixes required this in V1.7. - 2024-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-health-and-damage/2994963) Store player stats (Health, MaxHealth, WalkSpeed, JumpPower) in a ModuleScript inside the character model and require() it from server scripts. ([[2024-05-29 - The Basics of Combat Games Health and Damage|The Basics of Combat Games: Health and Damage]])
- 2024-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-health-and-damage/2994963) Put damage logic in a TakeDamage function in the module that takes an AttackInfo dictionary, so new attack parameters don't require copy-pasted code. ([[2024-05-29 - The Basics of Combat Games Health and Damage|The Basics of Combat Games: Health and Damage]])
- 2024-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-health-and-damage/2994963) Use math.clamp on (Defense - Piercing) so armor never goes negative, and use 'or 0' so a missing Piercing value doesn't error. ([[2024-05-29 - The Basics of Combat Games Health and Damage|The Basics of Combat Games: Health and Damage]])
- 2024-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-health-and-damage/2994963) Use newproxy() as unique tokens to detect interrupted timed effects; compare the stored token to the local one after task.wait instead of using a counter. ([[2024-05-29 - The Basics of Combat Games Health and Damage|The Basics of Combat Games: Health and Damage]])
- 2024-05-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-health-and-damage/2994963) Define status effects in a dictionary (Info and State tables) and apply them through one generic function, rather than writing a separate function per status. ([[2024-05-29 - The Basics of Combat Games Health and Damage|The Basics of Combat Games: Health and Damage]])
- 2024-06-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930) Avoid .Touched() for melee hitboxes; it is inaccurate on moving parts and misses parts already inside the hitbox. Reserve it for anchored triggers like landmines. ([[2024-06-03 - The Basics of Combat Games Hitboxes|The Basics of Combat Games: Hitboxes]])
- 2024-06-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930) Magnitude hitboxes (distance to a radius) are cheap and usually precise enough; prefer them unless precision is required. ([[2024-06-03 - The Basics of Combat Games Hitboxes|The Basics of Combat Games: Hitboxes]])
- 2024-06-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930) For fast projectiles, raycast between last recorded and current position each interval; use GetPartsInBox for large boxy projectiles, sized to travel distance plus projectile size. ([[2024-06-03 - The Basics of Combat Games Hitboxes|The Basics of Combat Games: Hitboxes]])
- 2024-06-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-basics-of-combat-games-hitboxes/3002930) Keep a table of already-hit targets per attack and filter them out so a single swing does not damage the same character repeatedly. ([[2024-06-03 - The Basics of Combat Games Hitboxes|The Basics of Combat Games: Hitboxes]])
- 2024-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-use-robloxs-physics-character-controllers/3092097) Scale AirController.MoveMaxForce by velocity magnitude times BaseMoveSpeed to limit air directional influence, smoothing movement during knockback. ([[2024-07-29 - How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Controllers|How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Con]])
- 2024-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-use-robloxs-physics-character-controllers/3092097) Set AirController.MoveMaxForce to 0 to disable midair movement entirely, e.g. during a knockback state. ([[2024-07-29 - How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Controllers|How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Con]])
- 2024-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-actually-use-robloxs-physics-character-controllers/3092097) Apply equal and opposite impulse to the floor part at the sensed hit normal when jumping from ground. ([[2024-07-29 - How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Controllers|How to Actually Use Roblox's Physics Character Con]])
- 2024-09-13 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/state-machines-in-depth-tutorial/3157190) Only allow attacks in states where they make sense (e.g. Idle or Walk); block attacks in other states such as stunned or jumping. ([[2024-09-13 - State machines In-Depth Tutorial|State machines // In-Depth Tutorial]])
- 2025-05-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/math-for-roblox-developers-1-vectors/3659072) Use dot products to check whether a player faces an NPC or target; a result near 1 means aligned, 0 means perpendicular, negative means facing away. ([[2025-05-21 - Math for Roblox Developers 1 Vectors|Math for Roblox Developers #1: Vectors]])
- 2025-10-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-the-story-behind-99-nights-in-the-forest/4036940) Build rollback systems alongside client-predicted actions, since rollbacks are tricky; the team reports the approach working smoothly about 99% of the time. ([[2025-10-31 - Creator Spotlight The Story Behind 99 Nights in the Forest|Creator Spotlight: The Story Behind 99 Nights in t]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157) Note Attribute limit: max 64 attributes per Instance with limited string size; minimise instance payload sizes for efficient sync. ([[2025-12-09 - Studio Beta Build Fair, Responsive Games with Server Authority|[Studio Beta] Build Fair, Responsive Games with Se]])
- 2025-12-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/archived-rewind-server-authoritative-hit-validation-custom-character-replication/4160622) Validate all weapon hits on the server (Rewind.ValidateRay etc.) so exploiters cannot claim fake hits; compensate high-ping players by rewinding targets to their fire-time positions. ([[2025-12-18 - Archived Rewind - Server-Authoritative Hit Validation & Custom Charact|[Archived] Rewind - Server-Authoritative Hit Valid]])
- 2025-12-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/archived-rewind-server-authoritative-hit-validation-custom-character-replication/4160622) Pick the validation mode per weapon type: Ray for guns, Cone for melee, Sphere for explosions, Capsule for sweeps, Fan for shotguns. - 2025-12-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/archived-rewind-server-authoritative-hit-validation-custom-character-replication/4160622) Use per-weapon profiles (maxRange, rateLimit, isMelee, meleeAngleDeg, scaleMultiplier) rather than hardcoding limits; set isMelee=true to disable the duplicate shot check for melee. ([[2025-12-18 - Archived Rewind - Server-Authoritative Hit Validation & Custom Charact|[Archived] Rewind - Server-Authoritative Hit Valid]])
- 2026-04-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/new-spatial-query-filters/4563575) Whitelist-with-exceptions (include a folder, exclude specific children) avoids needing multiple raycasts per query. ([[2026-04-07 - New spatial query filters|New spatial query filters]])
- 2026-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568) Use contact Point and Normal for hit detection that needs to know which part of a blade connected, or for damage weighted by hit location. ([[2026-10-06 - Collision Summaries API Detailed Collision Data From The Engine|Collision Summaries API: Detailed Collision Data F]])

## [[building-and-level-design]]
- 2016-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/smooth-terrain-how-tos-and-tips-and-tricks/24017) Set Generate tool Biome Size smaller for many biomes close together, larger for sprawling maps; the default seed is the place ID. ([[2016-04-08 - Smooth Terrain How To's and Tips and Tricks|Smooth Terrain: How To's and Tips and Tricks]])
- 2017-03-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729) Make rig Motor6Ds direct children of the parent part (typically Part0); otherwise the plugin may fail to find the joint. ([[2017-03-30 - Blender rig exporteranimation importer|Blender rig exporter/animation importer]])
- 2018-11-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-furniture-placement-system/205509) Give each placeable model a transparent, non-collidable PrimaryPart as its bounding box so placement math uses consistent sizes. ([[2018-11-26 - Creating A Furniture Placement System|Creating A Furniture Placement System]])
- 2018-11-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-furniture-placement-system/205509) Snap placement to a grid by rounding constrained x/y to GridUnit multiples, offset by half-size remainder, to keep objects aligned. ([[2018-11-26 - Creating A Furniture Placement System|Creating A Furniture Placement System]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) Match detail to the game style and abstract away unnecessary detail, such as props seen from only 1 or 3 sides. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853) Decide genre, theme, player count, art style and map shape before opening Studio; FPS maps should be fluid with few dead ends, open-world maps need draw-in landmarks. ([[2019-05-08 - Ruski's Tutorial 1 - How to design a map layout|Ruski's Tutorial #1 - How to design a map layout]])
- 2019-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853) Sketch map layouts on paper first and collect a few dozen reference photos; it saves time even if drawings are not used exactly. ([[2019-05-08 - Ruski's Tutorial 1 - How to design a map layout|Ruski's Tutorial #1 - How to design a map layout]])
- 2019-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853) Keep players looking at something interesting; never leave empty space or bare walls as the main view. ([[2019-05-08 - Ruski's Tutorial 1 - How to design a map layout|Ruski's Tutorial #1 - How to design a map layout]])
- 2019-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853) Limit enterable buildings to about two or three entry points each to balance attackers and defenders; keep some dead ends as risk/reward spots. ([[2019-05-08 - Ruski's Tutorial 1 - How to design a map layout|Ruski's Tutorial #1 - How to design a map layout]])
- 2019-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853) Use curves and jogs rather than boxy shapes so the map feels fluid. ([[2019-05-08 - Ruski's Tutorial 1 - How to design a map layout|Ruski's Tutorial #1 - How to design a map layout]])
- 2019-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853) Mark off-limits rooftops and areas with clearly coloured boundary bricks instead of leaving players to wander into them. ([[2019-05-08 - Ruski's Tutorial 1 - How to design a map layout|Ruski's Tutorial #1 - How to design a map layout]])
- 2019-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853) Scale buildings to the camera: in first person, keep buildings 2-3 levels since players cannot zoom out to view the map. ([[2019-05-08 - Ruski's Tutorial 1 - How to design a map layout|Ruski's Tutorial #1 - How to design a map layout]])
- 2019-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ruskis-tutorial-1-how-to-design-a-map-layout/277853) Add cover in open areas and alleys, plus second floors, roofs, holes in walls and balconies so players have multiple routes. ([[2019-05-08 - Ruski's Tutorial 1 - How to design a map layout|Ruski's Tutorial #1 - How to design a map layout]])
- 2019-07-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introduction-to-tweening-models/315253) Choose a root that forms a bounding box over the model for accurate moves, or a invisible hinge pole for swinging doors matching the model's full height. ([[2019-07-24 - Introduction to Tweening Models|Introduction to Tweening Models]])
- 2019-09-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dungeon-generation-a-procedural-generation-guide/342413) Use Recursive Backtracker rather than Hunt and Kill for filling maze space; Hunt and Kill can leave blocked-off unreachable areas around rooms. ([[2019-09-01 - Dungeon Generation A Procedural Generation Guide|Dungeon Generation: A Procedural Generation Guide]])
- 2019-09-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dungeon-generation-a-procedural-generation-guide/342413) Require at least 2 rooms before placing doors; set a minimum spacing (ROOM_BORDER_LENIANCY) between rooms and cap brute-force attempts (MAX_ROOM_TRIES). ([[2019-09-01 - Dungeon Generation A Procedural Generation Guide|Dungeon Generation: A Procedural Generation Guide]])
- 2019-09-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dungeon-generation-a-procedural-generation-guide/342413) Connect rooms with a flood fill from each door to the nearest door of another room; close off doors that fail to find a path. ([[2019-09-01 - Dungeon Generation A Procedural Generation Guide|Dungeon Generation: A Procedural Generation Guide]])
- 2019-09-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dungeon-generation-a-procedural-generation-guide/342413) After pathing, remove non-path, non-room cells and rebuild walls only between them and adjacent path cells; reuse tables instead of creating new ones to save memory. ([[2019-09-01 - Dungeon Generation A Procedural Generation Guide|Dungeon Generation: A Procedural Generation Guide]])
- 2019-12-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/dragging-objects-with-the-mouse/403766) Clamp drag distance relative to the character's Head, not the camera, so zooming out doesn't send the part far away. ([[2019-12-08 - Dragging objects with the mouse|Dragging objects with the mouse]])
- 2019-12-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672) Use Gaea's Scale slider on the primitive to add more features for large maps or fewer for enclosed areas. ([[2019-12-11 - Large-Scale Roblox Terrain The ultimate guide|Large-Scale Roblox Terrain: The ultimate guide]])
- 2019-12-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672) Set sea_level in the conversion script (0 to 1 normalized) and fill water with Roblox's sea level method rather than the script's unfinished water fill. ([[2019-12-11 - Large-Scale Roblox Terrain The ultimate guide|Large-Scale Roblox Terrain: The ultimate guide]])
- 2020-01-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/replicating-instances-to-only-some-players/438374) For smooth terrain, use Terrain:ReadVoxels and WriteVoxels; the Instance replication technique does not apply to terrain. ([[2020-01-20 - Replicating Instances to Only Some Players|Replicating Instances to Only Some Players]])
- 2020-02-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/consume-everything-how-greedy-meshing-works/452717) Greedy meshing 1D: start at an unvisited non-empty block, extend while next block is non-empty and unvisited, then record the start and end positions as opposite corners of a cuboid. ([[2020-02-07 - Consume everything - how greedy meshing works|Consume everything - how greedy meshing works]])
- 2020-05-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/perspective-object-dragging-like-superliminal/566200) Use FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList from the camera to find the furthest unobstructed position, ignoring the character and the held object. ([[2020-05-09 - Perspective object dragging like Superliminal|Perspective object dragging like Superliminal]])
- 2020-07-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753) Make plot size a multiple of grid size; otherwise placement emits a warning in the output. ([[2020-07-31 - How to use Placement Service|How to use Placement Service]])
- 2020-07-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753) Set SmartDisplay to false unless grid size is under 5 studs, as the author recommends. ([[2020-07-31 - How to use Placement Service|How to use Placement Service]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Decide map layout (buildings, paths, landmarks) before making assets; use scaled placeholder blocks for houses, lanterns and trees to avoid resizing later. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Test in test mode often to view the map from player perspective and adjust scale, enclosure (mountains in distance) and tree/building heights. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Place tall buildings on downward slopes so they feel less imposing relative to the player. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Mark water locations (rivers, lakes, waterfalls, oceans) early in layout, before placing assets. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Use curved, varied slopes and subtle elevation changes rather than straight slopes for natural-looking terrain. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Keep cobblestone paths not too wide, and mix terrain materials (cobblestone, grass, leafy grass, rock) to avoid unnatural looks. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Build rivers/lakes by subtracting a deep curved gap with the square brush, then adding water with the add tool, and paint sides rock. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Build walls by painting the cobblestone path first, keep stone walls lower than the player, fill interior with grass, then erode excess terrain. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Use Terrain Grow tool with rock, with base size and strength turned down, to create natural rocks; dot them in empty areas. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Vary repeated props (flowers, etc.) by colour, size and angle; fill empty space with barrels, crates, rocks and plants at varied sizes. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Use bright accent colours (flags, flowers) against a muted palette for contrast; keep palette limited with unified shades. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Check and fix clipping issues at the end by testing from the player's perspective. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
