- 2021-02-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-2021-preventing-high-latency-reducing-lag/1046078) When generating many voxels or terrain parts, parent them to nil or a folder parented to nil first, then parent to Workspace once done. The author reports up to ~100x speedup. ([[2021-02-14 - Network Optimization (2021) - Preventing High Latency & Reducing Lag|Network Optimization (2021) - Preventing High Late]])
- 2021-07-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-add-weight-to-the-camera-camera-tutorial/1331996) Anchor the invisible Subject part and make it non-colliding/transparent so it doesn't interfere with the workspace; it must stay where you set it. ([[2021-07-06 - How to Add Weight to the Camera Camera Tutorial|How to Add "Weight" to the Camera [Camera Tutorial]])
- 2021-10-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-procedural-mountains-a-fractal-noise-tutorial/1494362) Take math.abs of noise before summing octaves and square the result to flatten mountain bases and separate peaks. ([[2021-10-04 - Creating Procedural Mountains A Fractal Noise Tutorial|Creating Procedural Mountains: A Fractal Noise Tut]])
- 2021-10-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-procedural-mountains-a-fractal-noise-tutorial/1494362) Blend colour layers by fading a noise map toward a constant 1 with linearStep between StartHeight and EndHeight so high peaks become snow rather than grass. ([[2021-10-04 - Creating Procedural Mountains A Fractal Noise Tutorial|Creating Procedural Mountains: A Fractal Noise Tut]])
- 2021-10-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-procedural-mountains-a-fractal-noise-tutorial/1494362) Use 1x1x1 blocks with gap-filling instead of tall prisms; tall prisms hide slope gaps but break mining mechanics. ([[2021-10-04 - Creating Procedural Mountains A Fractal Noise Tutorial|Creating Procedural Mountains: A Fractal Noise Tut]])
- 2021-12-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-of-how-to-improve-your-buildings-long/1606301) Keep a city's building part count low: an 800-part building cut to 68 parts via wall shapes, then cloned 100 times, yields 6,800 parts instead of 80,000. ([[2021-12-29 - Guide of how to improve your buildings! (Long)|Guide of how to improve your buildings! (Long)]])
- 2021-12-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-of-how-to-improve-your-buildings-long/1606301) Use unions (Solid Modeling) or meshes to reduce part counts for complex shapes, but check triangle counts in wireframe, since poorly topologized meshes can cost more than parts. ([[2021-12-29 - Guide of how to improve your buildings! (Long)|Guide of how to improve your buildings! (Long)]])
- 2021-12-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-of-how-to-improve-your-buildings-long/1606301) Use Part To Terrain to convert blocks into grass, water, or rivers quickly, and raise buildings above terrain to make small street scenes. ([[2021-12-29 - Guide of how to improve your buildings! (Long)|Guide of how to improve your buildings! (Long)]])
- 2022-02-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-cos-and-sin/1659301) To place parts in a ring, loop i=1..N and use angle i*(2*pi/N), then multiply cos/sin by the desired radius (cos/sin only give unit-circle values). ([[2022-02-07 - How to use Cos and Sin|How to use Cos and Sin]])
- 2022-02-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/basics-of-cframe-multiplication-and-weldsmotor6dsjoint-instances/1682079) Align a part to a surface normal with CFrame.lookAt(pos, pos+dir) only if you accept auto-generated right/up vectors; otherwise use a custom vector-alignment technique. ([[2022-02-26 - Basics of CFrame multiplication and WeldsMotor6DsJoint Instances|Basics of CFrame multiplication and Welds/Motor6Ds]])
- 2023-03-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571) Avoid excessive darkness where it doesn't belong; use lighting to guide players or deliberately mislead them. ([[2023-03-09 - Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences|Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences]])
- 2023-03-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571) Avoid DepthOfField and Blur post-processing, which the author finds detrimental. ([[2023-03-09 - Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences|Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648) Greybox the map with simple parts first to plan paths, spawns and endpoints before sculpting or detailing. ([[2024-05-10 - Creator Spotlight Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental Artist Behind Fantast|Creator Spotlight: Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental ]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648) In open worlds, use curves and multiple directions instead of straight paths to encourage exploration. ([[2024-05-10 - Creator Spotlight Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental Artist Behind Fantast|Creator Spotlight: Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental ]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648) When redesigning an existing game, keep the map layout unchanged to avoid confusing veteran players; upgrade only the visuals. ([[2024-05-10 - Creator Spotlight Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental Artist Behind Fantast|Creator Spotlight: Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental ]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648) Add lived-in details (fences, parks, gardens) to make an environment feel alive. ([[2024-05-10 - Creator Spotlight Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental Artist Behind Fantast|Creator Spotlight: Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental ]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648) Add small unique details, such as a different flower per floor, to reward players who explore. ([[2024-05-10 - Creator Spotlight Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental Artist Behind Fantast|Creator Spotlight: Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental ]])
- 2024-08-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400) Typical starting values: Scale 10, Frequency 0.4, Amplitude 10-15, ChunkSize 2, SmoothingFactor around 0.25 for best results. ([[2024-08-08 - Ultimate Perlin Noise and How To Make Procedural Terrain Guide 24,231|Ultimate Perlin Noise and How To Make Procedural T]])
- 2024-08-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400) Map height to color with a threshold dictionary (0-1 keys); the color applies to any height greater than the key, e.g. water, grass, rock, snow. ([[2024-08-08 - Ultimate Perlin Noise and How To Make Procedural Terrain Guide 24,231|Ultimate Perlin Noise and How To Make Procedural T]])
- 2024-08-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400) Flatten water by remapping heights above a threshold with inverse lerp, keeping the original height for colouring via a preservedBaseY variable. ([[2024-08-08 - Ultimate Perlin Noise and How To Make Procedural Terrain Guide 24,231|Ultimate Perlin Noise and How To Make Procedural T]])
- 2024-08-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ultimate-perlin-noise-and-how-to-make-procedural-terrain-guide-24231-characters-detailed/3109400) Set part Y size to y*2+1 so terrain columns are solid and holes disappear. ([[2024-08-08 - Ultimate Perlin Noise and How To Make Procedural Terrain Guide 24,231|Ultimate Perlin Noise and How To Make Procedural T]])
- 2025-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/i-made-a-planetaryspherical-gravity-system/3383865) Make the sun track the player: change the lighting's geographic latitude so the skybox sun aligns with the 3D sun, and scale brightness with distance for a sense of scale. ([[2025-01-15 - I made a PlanetarySpherical gravity system|I made a Planetary/Spherical gravity system]])
- 2025-05-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/math-for-roblox-developers-1-vectors/3659072) Use Vector:Lerp with a 0-1 alpha for smooth per-frame movement of cameras and objects; tween or interpolate every frame for smoothness. ([[2025-05-21 - Math for Roblox Developers 1 Vectors|Math for Roblox Developers #1: Vectors]])
- 2026-03-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374) Use SubstituteGeometry/ApplyMesh to update a part's shape while keeping its children intact, instead of replacing the part (avoids flicker). ([[2026-03-12 - Studio Beta Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragment and Sweep APIs|[Studio Beta] Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragm]])
- 2026-03-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/voice-chat-is-now-supported-in-servers-of-up-to-100-users/4534031) Before scaling past 50 users, check that the map is large enough for players to interact without crowding. ([[2026-03-24 - Voice Chat is now supported in servers of up to 100 users!|Voice Chat is now supported in servers of up to 10]])
- 2026-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258) Disable CanCollide, CanTouch, CanQuery and CastShadow on objects at placement time; doing it late is tedious and you'll miss items. ([[2026-05-10 - The definitive tutorial to game optimization everything you need to kn|The definitive tutorial to game optimization: ever]])

## [[3d-modeling-rigging-and-animation]]
- 2017-03-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729) Skinned meshes and bones are not supported; for skinned mesh animation use the built-in .fbx animation importer instead. ([[2017-03-30 - Blender rig exporteranimation importer|Blender rig exporter/animation importer]])
- 2017-03-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729) For high-quality animation, use frame step 1 at 30 fps. Frame step 2-5 often gives smoother results, depending on the animation. ([[2017-03-30 - Blender rig exporteranimation importer|Blender rig exporter/animation importer]])
- 2017-03-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729) Keep the generated __Rig armature when exporting to other tools; replacing it loses metadata. Map external armatures onto it by bone name instead. ([[2017-03-30 - Blender rig exporteranimation importer|Blender rig exporter/animation importer]])
- 2017-03-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729) Before mapping keyframes by bone name, set the scene frame range first; only frames inside the range are mapped. ([[2017-03-30 - Blender rig exporteranimation importer|Blender rig exporter/animation importer]])
- 2017-03-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729) Use the Create IK constraints tool in Pose mode by selecting end-of-chain bones (feet, hands); tune constraint parameters afterward. ([[2017-03-30 - Blender rig exporteranimation importer|Blender rig exporter/animation importer]])
- 2017-03-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/blender-rig-exporteranimation-importer/34729) Mixamo/FBX animations can be imported with the Import FBX button; the rig must contain one armature with animation data. ([[2017-03-30 - Blender rig exporteranimation importer|Blender rig exporter/animation importer]])
- 2018-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434) Use humanoid:LoadAnimation for hold and aim poses; animations replicate automatically and interpolate between each other, avoiding extra RemoteEvents for transitions. ([[2018-08-06 - The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter|The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter]])
- 2018-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-first-person-element-of-a-first-person-shooter/160434) Do not rely on shared animation IDs from others; Roblox blocks sharing of animations, so re-export them to your own account and update the IDs. ([[2018-08-06 - The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter|The First Person Element Of A First Person Shooter]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Add anticipation before every action (e.g. squash before a jump, wind-up before a punch) so players read what is about to happen. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Apply follow-through and overlapping action to secondary parts like capes, hair and limbs, but not the root, and avoid making it too flowy on a rig. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Use easing (Sine, Quad etc.) rather than linear keyframes; linear motion looks robotic. Use In/Out/InOut/OutIn directions to set speed through the motion. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Avoid twinning: keyframes in straight lines with no inbetweens. Vary timing and add secondary actions to break up uniform motion. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Move character through key poses first (pose-to-pose), then refine the transitions between them. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Exaggerate key poses for game feel; realistic motion often reads as weak in games. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Add arcs to limb and body movement; motion without arcs looks robotic. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Improve by repeating old animations, studying films and other games, copying references, polishing every animation, and trying varied styles. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Practice with cinematics and long scenes (fights, running, flying) as a strong skill-building exercise. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/jespones-guide-to-animations/225752) Use the Ease Visualizer (GreenSock) to understand easing curves before animating. ([[2019-01-15 - Jespone's guide to animations|Jespone's guide to animations]])
- 2019-01-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-a-toolobject-with-a-dummy-in-the-animation-editor/232317) Attach the tool to the rig with a Motor6D named HandleMotor6D: Part0 = RightHand (R15) or Right Arm (R6), Part1 = the Handle. ([[2019-01-27 - How to animate a toolobject with a Dummy in the Animation Editor|How to animate a tool/object with a Dummy in the A]])
- 2019-01-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-a-toolobject-with-a-dummy-in-the-animation-editor/232317) Once attached, the Handle appears under RightArm in the Animation Editor; moving or rotating the Handle moves the whole tool. ([[2019-01-27 - How to animate a toolobject with a Dummy in the Animation Editor|How to animate a tool/object with a Dummy in the A]])
- 2019-09-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484) Weld the tool to Torso rather than Right Arm if you want to animate the tool freely (reloads, knife tricks); arm-welded tools move with the arm. ([[2019-09-30 - How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)|How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)]])
- 2019-09-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tool-parts-guns-knifes-etc/359484) Make sure everything under the tool is unanchored, or parts will go missing when connected to the dummy. ([[2019-09-30 - How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)|How to animate Tool Parts (Guns, Knifes etc.)]])
- 2020-05-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/procedural-animation-what-it-is-and-how-to-do-it/564782) Procedural animation uses CFrames instead of animation tracks; it can replace or augment Roblox's built-in animation system. ([[2020-05-08 - Procedural Animation What it is and how to do it|Procedural Animation: What it is and how to do it]])
- 2020-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-part-2-2020/581791) Export looping animations for poses; use lower priority for idle; use Out easing for quick shooting animations. ([[2020-05-18 - Writing an FPS framework, part 2 (2020)|Writing an FPS framework, part 2 (2020)]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207) Use static Accessories (welds) for clothing and addons that never move; use Model plus Motor6D for weapons you animate with the character, like a gripped spear or spinning knife. ([[2020-08-27 - Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. UPDATED|Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. [UPDATED]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207) Set the body and handle attachment WorldOrientation to (0,0,0) and copy matching WorldPosition/WorldOrientation so the accessory does not get an unwanted rotation offset. ([[2020-08-27 - Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. UPDATED|Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. [UPDATED]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207) Add custom attachment points on rig body parts with the Default Move tool when Roblox's built-in attachments are not enough; keep their orientation at (0,0,0). ([[2020-08-27 - Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. UPDATED|Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. [UPDATED]])
- 2020-08-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/accessory-based-items-clothing-addons-updated/744207) Enable Constraint Details and Draw On Top in Studio to see attachments and constraints in 3D and select them easily. ([[2020-08-27 - Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. UPDATED|Accessory Based Items, Clothing & Addons. [UPDATED]])
- 2021-03-21 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-a-mesh-deformation-ocean/1122587) Use the avatar importer plugin with the Custom rig option to import an FBX mesh with bones already set up. ([[2021-03-21 - Making A Mesh Deformation Ocean|Making A Mesh Deformation Ocean]])
- 2021-05-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginners-guide/1198208) Build viewmodel bones with Motor6Ds: HumanoidRootPart to arms, CameraBone to HRP, and a 'Handle' Motor6D for welding the gun. Delete the viewmodel's Humanoid and use AnimationController instead. ([[2021-05-01 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner's guide|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner's guide]])
- 2021-05-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginners-guide/1198208) Zero the viewmodel HumanoidRootPart position and rotation before animating in Moon Animator, and set hold animations to loop. ([[2021-05-01 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner's guide|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner's guide]])
- 2022-02-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tools-the-easiest-and-best-way-for-melees/1654589) Convert the dummy's Right Grip weld to a Motor6D with Reclass; the Handle then appears as a body part under the right arm/hand in the animation editor. ([[2022-02-03 - How to animate tools (the easiest and best way) (for melees)|How to animate tools (the easiest and best way) (f]])
- 2022-02-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-animate-tools-the-easiest-and-best-way-for-melees/1654589) Moon Animator reportedly works best for animating tools, per the author. ([[2022-02-03 - How to animate tools (the easiest and best way) (for melees)|How to animate tools (the easiest and best way) (f]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creator-spotlight-meet-ahlvie-the-environmental-artist-behind-fantastical-worlds/2964648) Model stylized props in Blender and texture them in Substance Painter when needed. ([[2024-05-10 - Creator Spotlight Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental Artist Behind Fantast|Creator Spotlight: Meet Ahlvie, the Environmental ]])
- 2026-04-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788) Keep using ACE (Animation Clip Editor) for creating clips and fine-tuning keyframes/curves; use the Graph Editor only to organize clips into logic. ([[2026-04-02 - Studio Beta Introducing the Animation Graph System|[Studio Beta] Introducing the Animation Graph Syst]])
- 2026-04-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-introducing-the-animation-graph-system/4554788) Test graphs with Avatar Joint Upgrade (AJU) enabled now, since the platform-wide transition is in May 2026. ([[2026-04-02 - Studio Beta Introducing the Animation Graph System|[Studio Beta] Introducing the Animation Graph Syst]])
- 2026-04-30 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-the-us-18-devex-rate-earn-42-more-on-spend-from-18-us-players/4607091) Eligible avatar setups: R15 only, or always-custom human rig with 15+ joints distributed human-like, or always-custom non-human rig, or no visible player characters. ([[2026-04-30 - Introducing the US 18+ DevEx Rate Earn 42% More on Spend from 18+ US P|Introducing the US 18+ DevEx Rate: Earn 42% More o]])

## [[vfx-and-particles]]
- 2016-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-tutorial-for-all-resources-available/23919) Add atmosphere with ParticleEmitters, PointLights and Lighting properties; for a desert map use sandy fog, orange ambient, dark red ambience and a matching skybox. ([[2016-04-04 - Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)|Building Tutorial For All (Resources Available)]])
- 2018-09-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/modeling-a-projectiles-motion/176677) Draw projectile arc previews with a Beam using two Attachments; cubic Bezier points derive from x0, v0 and flight time, and CurveSize0/1 set handle lengths. ([[2018-09-07 - Modeling a projectile's motion|Modeling a projectile's motion]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Add leaf particles near trees for a falling-leaf effect as a low-cost final detail. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) For day/night: create multiple hand-tuned Atmosphere objects per time of day, then TweenService between them, tuning Haze, Glare, Color and Decay. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-08-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/map-design-and-scene-building-fantasy-medieval-and-nature-maps/709866) Animate Lighting ColorShift_Top during the day to make sunlight feel warmer. ([[2020-08-06 - Map Design and Scene Building Fantasy, Medieval and Nature maps|Map Design and Scene Building: Fantasy, Medieval a]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Scale :Emit() counts by the player's SavedQualityLevel, since :Emit() bypasses Roblox's automatic particle optimisation. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Avoid extremely high ParticleEmitter rates; they hurt lower-end devices notably. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-11-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/generating-equidistant-points-on-a-sphere/874144) Spherical point distributions work well for spell effects in RPGs; animate points slowly or attach a trail to one point and move it through the sequence. ([[2020-11-16 - Generating Equidistant Points on a Sphere|Generating Equidistant Points on a Sphere]])
- 2021-05-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginners-guide/1198208) Multiply bullet velocity by dt when moving projectiles per frame so speed stays consistent across frame-rate changes. ([[2021-05-01 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner's guide|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner's guide]])
- 2021-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507) Keep muzzle flash emitters disabled and call :Emit() on fire; enable LockedToPart so particles stick to the barrel. ([[2021-05-04 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner’s guide PART 2|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner’s guide [PART]])
- 2022-02-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-cos-and-sin/1659301) For smooth bobbing or floating motion, drive sin(alpha) with alpha += delta in Heartbeat; a full cycle takes 2*pi seconds at rate 1 per second. ([[2022-02-07 - How to use Cos and Sin|How to use Cos and Sin]])
- 2022-06-20 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/yuruzuus-open-source-vfx/1840021) Study ParticleEmitter-based VFX packs to learn how customizable particle effects are built and tuned. ([[2022-06-20 - Yuruzuu's Open Source VFX|Yuruzuu's Open Source VFX]])
- 2024-08-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-custom-skyboxes-using-blender-crafting-stunning-atmospheres/3134324) Pair Roblox Clouds with an Atmosphere object in Lighting; without it clouds render very faintly. ([[2024-08-25 - Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting stunning atmospheres|Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting ]])
- 2024-08-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-custom-skyboxes-using-blender-crafting-stunning-atmospheres/3134324) Increase Atmosphere Haze and Glare and lower ClockTime so the sun sits low for bloom-like sunset skies. ([[2024-08-25 - Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting stunning atmospheres|Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting ]])
- 2024-08-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-custom-skyboxes-using-blender-crafting-stunning-atmospheres/3134324) Raise EnvironmentalDiffuseScale and EnvironmentalSpecularScale in Lighting so skybox colours tint the environment. ([[2024-08-25 - Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting stunning atmospheres|Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting ]])
- 2024-08-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-custom-skyboxes-using-blender-crafting-stunning-atmospheres/3134324) Use Workspace.GlobalWind to control how fast Roblox Clouds scroll across the sky. ([[2024-08-25 - Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting stunning atmospheres|Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting ]])
- 2026-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/collision-summaries-api-detailed-collision-data-from-the-engine/4918568) Trigger impact sparks, audio and tire marks from summaries above a speed threshold (example uses >10) to avoid spawning effects on light contacts. ([[2026-10-06 - Collision Summaries API Detailed Collision Data From The Engine|Collision Summaries API: Detailed Collision Data F]])

## [[ui-ux-design]]
- 2015-09-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-slicecenter-robloxs-9-slice-gui-property/18313) For resizable GUI borders, set ImageLabel/ImageButton ScaleType to Slice so the image is sliced instead of stretched. ([[2015-09-07 - How to use SliceCenter (Roblox's 9-Slice GUI Property)|How to use SliceCenter (Roblox's 9-Slice GUI Prope]])
- 2015-09-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-slicecenter-robloxs-9-slice-gui-property/18313) SliceCenter is Rect2D (X0,Y0,X1,Y1); the two points define the center box. Everything outside it is border and follows 9-slice rules. ([[2015-09-07 - How to use SliceCenter (Roblox's 9-Slice GUI Property)|How to use SliceCenter (Roblox's 9-Slice GUI Prope]])
- 2017-08-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526) Use Offset only (no Scale) for elements that must not stretch, such as health bars, and keep a fixed Offset padding (e.g. 10) from screen edges. ([[2017-08-07 - A Guide to User Interface Design|A Guide to User Interface Design]])
- 2017-08-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526) Use Scale for position at center or edges (e.g. X Scale 0.5 for top center) and Offset for size, so UI doesn't stretch across different screens. ([[2017-08-07 - A Guide to User Interface Design|A Guide to User Interface Design]])
- 2017-08-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526) Keep UI grid-based with one universal padding value applied across all frames for consistent alignment. ([[2017-08-07 - A Guide to User Interface Design|A Guide to User Interface Design]])
- 2017-08-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526) Structure windows like websites: header for title/categories, body for items, footer for currency/resources; drop the footer when information is minimal. ([[2017-08-07 - A Guide to User Interface Design|A Guide to User Interface Design]])
- 2017-08-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526) Use concept art or reference games to decide layout, since one interface design does not fit all games. ([[2017-08-07 - A Guide to User Interface Design|A Guide to User Interface Design]])
- 2017-08-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526) Use color for meaning: red for danger/health, green for good/health/money, yellow for money/gold, black and white for foreground/background. ([[2017-08-07 - A Guide to User Interface Design|A Guide to User Interface Design]])
- 2017-08-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526) Use contrast in color weight (dark header and footer, lighter body) to create visual gravity and structure. ([[2017-08-07 - A Guide to User Interface Design|A Guide to User Interface Design]])
- 2017-08-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-guide-to-user-interface-design/47526) Place gradients and bright, pure colors on the most important information to act as a focal point; use gradients on selected category icons to show current state. ([[2017-08-07 - A Guide to User Interface Design|A Guide to User Interface Design]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Build UIs mainly in Scale rather than Offset; use Offset only when pixel-exact sizing is specifically needed. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Design for the device aspect ratio, not just Studio's screen size; Studio layouts often look different in-game on other devices. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Use SizeConstraint RelativeYY to keep aspect ratio; size relative to the dominant axis (e.g. width 2x height = {2,0},{1,0} for 2:1). ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Use RelativeXX for objects inside a vertical ScrollingFrame so they don't grow as CanvasSize increases; RelativeYY for horizontal ones. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Use AnchorPoint (e.g. 0,1 with Position Y scale 1) to pin elements to screen edges without extra position math. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Avoid TextScaled blindly; check visual consistency so buttons in the same group don't end up with different text sizes. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Use white or bright text by default; for dark colours like blue or red, lighten them or add a stroke or background. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Give text a TextStrokeColor3 that contrasts with the text colour; white text can use stroke transparency ~0.8 for subtle separation. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Keep the background behind text simple and uncluttered so letters remain readable. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Avoid text wrapping in buttons; resize the button, shrink the text, or disable TextWrapped for consistent appearance. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Do not display images larger than their original resolution; it causes pixelation and blur. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Use 9-slicing (ScaleType Slice, SliceCenter in pixels) so rounded or bordered images stretch without distortion. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Use spritesheets with ImageRectSize and ImageRectOffset to swap hover or animation states without uploading many images. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Add padding between element edges and parent frames; use UIPadding or manual sizing, and separate text from frame edges with a child label. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Maintain contrast between elements and keep UI styling consistent; restyle free models or bought GUIs to match your game. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Keep UI from covering the game view; large sidebars covering a third of the screen waste space. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2017-09-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-starter-guide/53461) Use ZIndex within a ScreenGui for layering; use ScreenGui DisplayOrder to layer separate ScreenGuis. ([[2017-09-23 - UI Design Starter Guide|UI Design Starter Guide]])
- 2018-09-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/modeling-a-projectiles-motion/176677) Aim-assist arc previews help players target throws; update the beam every RenderStepped frame from the mouse hit position. ([[2018-09-07 - Modeling a projectile's motion|Modeling a projectile's motion]])
- 2018-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780) Study the UI Design Starter Guide and A Guide to User Interface Design threads; use Gui-Designer to drag and scale GUI instances. ([[2018-09-09 - Game Development Resources MEGA THREAD|Game Development Resources [MEGA THREAD]]])
- 2019-03-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754) Give leaderboard rows gold/silver/bronze colours for places 1-3 and a header bar to make the board look appealing. ([[2019-03-23 - How to make a simple global leaderboard|How to make a simple global leaderboard]])
- 2019-03-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-simple-global-leaderboard/256754) If a part blocks scrolling on a Surface GUI leaderboard, move the Surface GUI part forward so the ScrollingFrame sits above it. ([[2019-03-23 - How to make a simple global leaderboard|How to make a simple global leaderboard]])
- 2019-04-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895) Set ClipsDescendants to true on the spinner's border frame so crates stay clipped inside the spinner window. ([[2019-04-01 - Creating a CrateSpin System|Creating a Crate/Spin System]])
- 2019-04-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895) Add a debounce and loading/error text on the spin button to prevent double-spinning and to show failed requests. ([[2019-04-01 - Creating a CrateSpin System|Creating a Crate/Spin System]])
- 2019-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785) Judge every UI concept against three principles: usable (responds to any interaction), navigable (no maze to find store or settings), and desirable (clean, no excess, fits game style). ([[2019-05-19 - How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfaces|How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfa]])
- 2019-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785) Build UI mockups in a vector tool like Adobe XD rather than directly in Studio, since Studio's built-in UI design tools are limited. ([[2019-05-19 - How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfaces|How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfa]])
- 2019-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-plan-design-and-build-quality-user-interfaces/281785) Keep UI elements consistent in size and style across the whole interface by designing each element carefully against one shared spec. ([[2019-05-19 - How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfaces|How to Plan, Design and Build Quality User Interfa]])
- 2019-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258) Keep on-screen UI limited to information relevant to gameplay; avoid persistent promo text like 'buy our gamepasses' that obstructs the view. ([[2019-10-12 - How to create good User Interface for your games|How to create good User Interface for your games]])
- 2019-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258) Pick a UI style that fits the game's theme (e.g. dark, metal or concrete textures for a medieval RPG) rather than bland generic shapes. ([[2019-10-12 - How to create good User Interface for your games|How to create good User Interface for your games]])
- 2019-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-good-user-interface-for-your-games/367258) Use a dark inset panel behind currency numbers so the text contrasts well against the textured frame. ([[2019-10-12 - How to create good User Interface for your games|How to create good User Interface for your games]])
- 2019-11-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792) Size ScrollingFrames with Offset rather than Scale, as the author recommends. ([[2019-11-22 - Quick Tutorial How to make scrolling frames properly|Quick Tutorial: How to make scrolling frames prope]])
- 2019-11-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792) Use UI constraints (UIListLayout/UIGridLayout) to lay out scrolling content; the author says to use them if you aren't already. ([[2019-11-22 - Quick Tutorial How to make scrolling frames properly|Quick Tutorial: How to make scrolling frames prope]])
- 2019-11-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792) Set ScrollingFrame.CanvasSize to the layout's AbsoluteContentSize X and Y so content neither overscrolls nor underscrolls. ([[2019-11-22 - Quick Tutorial How to make scrolling frames properly|Quick Tutorial: How to make scrolling frames prope]])
- 2019-11-22 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/quick-tutorial-how-to-make-scrolling-frames-properly/392792) For static content like a store GUI, set CanvasSize once manually or via script. ([[2019-11-22 - Quick Tutorial How to make scrolling frames properly|Quick Tutorial: How to make scrolling frames prope]])
- 2019-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-circularradial-hp-bar-like-kingdom-hearts/420996) Build a radial bar from corner pieces: use one 500x500 TopLeft piece rotated via UIGradient for three quarters, mapping health to 1/3 segments. ([[2019-12-31 - Creating A CircularRadial HP Bar Like Kingdom Hearts|Creating A Circular/Radial HP Bar Like Kingdom Hea]])
- 2019-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-circularradial-hp-bar-like-kingdom-hearts/420996) Use NumberSequence keypoints 0.001 apart; equal times can be ordered randomly, so offset the fill edge to avoid errors. ([[2019-12-31 - Creating A CircularRadial HP Bar Like Kingdom Hearts|Creating A Circular/Radial HP Bar Like Kingdom Hea]])
- 2019-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-circularradial-hp-bar-like-kingdom-hearts/420996) Reposition keypoints when rotating a UIGradient, since rotation skews the edges of the frame; scale keypoints to match the rotation angle. ([[2019-12-31 - Creating A CircularRadial HP Bar Like Kingdom Hearts|Creating A Circular/Radial HP Bar Like Kingdom Hea]])
- 2020-02-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-battle-system-oop-style/457584) Tween UI frames on and off screen using TweenService with predefined On/Off UDim2 positions for battle transitions. ([[2020-02-14 - Creating a battle system OOP style!|Creating a battle system: OOP style!]])
- 2020-03-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-2020/503318) Add a center-screen dot during testing; aim offset can look correct yet be visually misaligned. ([[2020-03-31 - Writing an FPS framework (2020)|Writing an FPS framework (2020)]])
- 2020-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/writing-an-fps-framework-part-2-2020/581791) Don't store animations inside the script output path only; put animation references in a settings module so missing animations don't throw errors. ([[2020-05-18 - Writing an FPS framework, part 2 (2020)|Writing an FPS framework, part 2 (2020)]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use UICorner for rounded corners; a scale of 0.5+ on a square makes a circle. Heavy use can hurt performance since it is a shader applied over the rendered object. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use UIListLayout and UIGridLayout instead of scripting positions; for grid cells, parent a UIAspectRatioConstraint inside the UIGridLayout to keep aspect ratio. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) For simple menus, manually toggle Visible/Enabled rather than UIPageLayout, which often needs cut/paste to refresh after visibility or LayoutOrder changes. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use UIScale on every element that must resize with screen size; then only update each UIScale's Scale value instead of recalculating sizes individually (e.g. 0.7 for smaller GUIs). ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use UISizeConstraint MinSize to stop elements shrinking to unreadable sizes on small screens. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Anchor GUIs to screen edges (e.g. X position {1,0} with AnchorPoint) so elements never float unintentionally in the middle of the screen. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use the top 36px inset: set ScreenGui.IgnoreGuiInset to true or compensate with offsets; account for it in code via GuiService:GetGuiInset(). ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use numeric rbxassetid:// IDs (Copy ID in Explorer), not rbxgameasset:// URLs, which break across games and when assets are renamed. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Add sound to UI interactions, including error sounds (e.g. insufficient funds) to communicate state to the player. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use GUI InputBegan/InputEnded/MouseButton1Up events for reliable hover and click handling; MouseEnter/MouseLeave often fail to fire. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Process a button press only if the mouse is released while still over the button, the standard real-world convention for a good experience. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Size a ScrollingFrame's CanvasSize by binding to layout:GetPropertyChangedSignal('AbsoluteContentSize') so scrolling always works correctly. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use camera ViewportSize for full screen size (no inset); use TextService:GetTextSize to measure text. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use TweenService to animate any GUI property, storing the Tween object for reused open/close animations and calling Play(). ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) For ViewportFrames, set the camera CFrame to look at the model from outside it, lower FieldOfView below 70 for better 2D viewing, and reassign CurrentCamera via script at runtime. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-objects-tips/613948) Use WorldModel instead of Model in a ViewportFrame to enable physics and animation on characters. ([[2020-06-06 - UI Design Objects & Tips|UI Design Objects & Tips]])
- 2020-06-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943) Add a settings menu to toggle each camera effect (bobbing, blur, sprint) on or off, since players have different preferences. ([[2020-06-27 - Creating a Simple, Realistic Camera (With View Bobbing, Dynamic Blur,|Creating a Simple, Realistic Camera (With View Bob]])
- 2020-07-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-use-placement-service/698753) On mobile, the module does not handle input; bind your own UI buttons to raise, lower, rotate, terminate and requestPlacement, detected via getPlatform() returning "Mobile". ([[2020-07-31 - How to use Placement Service|How to use Placement Service]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Set GUI Visible to false instead of tweening UI off-screen; hidden GUIs stop rendering and input. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Reduce tweens on GUI; dynamic GUI costs more draw time than static GUI, visible in MicroProfiler. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2021-02-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-advanced-health-bar/1042705) Enable ResetOnSpawn on the health ScreenGui so it is recreated with each character spawn. ([[2021-02-12 - How To Make Advanced Health Bar|How To Make Advanced Health Bar]])
- 2021-02-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-advanced-health-bar/1042705) Drive bar width with UDim2.fromScale(health ratio, 1) and clamp the ratio to 0-1 to avoid overflow. ([[2021-02-12 - How To Make Advanced Health Bar|How To Make Advanced Health Bar]])
- 2021-02-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-advanced-health-bar/1042705) Connect to Humanoid:GetPropertyChangedSignal for Health and MaxHealth so the bar updates on damage, healing and max changes. ([[2021-02-12 - How To Make Advanced Health Bar|How To Make Advanced Health Bar]])
- 2021-02-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-advanced-health-bar/1042705) Use TweenService to animate bar size changes for smooth feedback instead of snapping to new values. ([[2021-02-12 - How To Make Advanced Health Bar|How To Make Advanced Health Bar]])
- 2021-03-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/fps-using-viewmodels-the-improved-version-parts-2-out-of-3/1129877) Set Character.Archivable = true before cloning, then reset to false; Character:Clone() fails on non-archivable characters. ([[2021-03-25 - FPS using ViewModels (The improved version) Parts 2 out of 3|FPS using ViewModels (The improved version) // Par]])
- 2021-05-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginners-guide/1198208) Position the viewmodel to the camera CFrame every frame inside RenderStepped so the arms track the view without lag. ([[2021-05-01 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner's guide|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner's guide]])
- 2021-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507) Use a spring for bobble driven by player velocity and sway driven by mouse delta, applied to the viewmodel and HumanoidRootPart. ([[2021-05-04 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner’s guide PART 2|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner’s guide [PART]])
- 2021-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507) Tween an AimAlpha NumberValue (not IntegerValue) to 1 or 0 and lerp the sight toward the viewmodel camera for smooth aiming. ([[2021-05-04 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner’s guide PART 2|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner’s guide [PART]])
- 2021-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507) Scopes block the default mouse raycast; use a custom GetMouse raycast from the camera with RaycastParams to aim through scoped guns. ([[2021-05-04 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner’s guide PART 2|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner’s guide [PART]])
- 2021-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916) Sketch the UI roughly first (MS Paint is fine); avoid overdetailing so the sketch stays a quick layout plan. ([[2021-05-18 - The Ultimate UI Design Guide|The Ultimate UI Design Guide]])
- 2021-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916) Build a layout skeleton in Figma before details so buttons and text align without overlapping elements. ([[2021-05-18 - The Ultimate UI Design Guide|The Ultimate UI Design Guide]])
- 2021-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916) Don't always use dark themes; use light themes for cartoony or playful UI. ([[2021-05-18 - The Ultimate UI Design Guide|The Ultimate UI Design Guide]])
- 2021-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916) Experiment with rounded corners on frames and buttons depending on the UI style. ([[2021-05-18 - The Ultimate UI Design Guide|The Ultimate UI Design Guide]])
- 2021-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916) Use a palette tool like Coolors and lock colors you like while regenerating to build a scheme. ([[2021-05-18 - The Ultimate UI Design Guide|The Ultimate UI Design Guide]])
- 2021-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916) Set Anchor Point to 0.5,0.5 on background frames and position elements via the Properties tab rather than the UI editor, which is more precise. ([[2021-05-18 - The Ultimate UI Design Guide|The Ultimate UI Design Guide]])
- 2021-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916) Roblox Studio cannot upload font files; pick a similar built-in font (e.g. Gotham for Montserrat). ([[2021-05-18 - The Ultimate UI Design Guide|The Ultimate UI Design Guide]])
- 2021-05-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-ui-design-guide/1236916) Use an AutoScale plugin on UI elements so the interface scales across screen sizes. ([[2021-05-18 - The Ultimate UI Design Guide|The Ultimate UI Design Guide]])
- 2021-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701) Blur only works on 3D-space elements (decals, parts, SurfaceGuis); ScreenGui frames cannot be blurred directly. ([[2021-05-19 - Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)|Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)]])
- 2021-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701) Use a subframe named 'Blur' with scale offsets around -3 and Visible off, and place the LocalScript inside it to reduce edge bleeding. ([[2021-05-19 - Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)|Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)]])
- 2021-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701) Tune DepthOfField settings during playtesting to minimise bleeding around frame edges. ([[2021-05-19 - Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)|Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)]])
- 2021-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701) Very rounded frames (circles, non-square ImageFrames) do not work well with this effect; UICorner with offset around 5 to 7 still looks acceptable. ([[2021-05-19 - Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)|Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)]])
- 2021-05-19 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/gaussian-blur-filter-for-ui-tutorial/1238701) Tweening, scaling and positioning of blurred frames work without breaking the effect. ([[2021-05-19 - Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)|Gaussian blur filter for UI (tutorial)]])
- 2021-07-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-custom-loadinghealth-bars/1323459) Build a loading bar as nested frames: a rounded base, a clipping frame set to clip descendants, and a Top frame sized {1,0,1,0} inside it. ([[2021-07-01 - The Ultimate Guide to Custom LoadingHealth Bars|The Ultimate Guide to Custom Loading/Health Bars]])
- 2021-07-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-custom-loadinghealth-bars/1323459) Scale the bar by setting Clipping width to the ratio and Top width to 1/ratio so the inner fill stays correctly sized. ([[2021-07-01 - The Ultimate Guide to Custom LoadingHealth Bars|The Ultimate Guide to Custom Loading/Health Bars]])
- 2021-07-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-ultimate-guide-to-custom-loadinghealth-bars/1323459) Darken the ImageBase color when building image-based bars so the difference between the two images is visible. ([[2021-07-01 - The Ultimate Guide to Custom LoadingHealth Bars|The Ultimate Guide to Custom Loading/Health Bars]])
- 2021-07-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-add-weight-to-the-camera-camera-tutorial/1331996) Use different weights for horizontal and vertical camera motion (e.g. WEIGHT_XZ=20, WEIGHT_Y=5) since screens are wider than tall. ([[2021-07-06 - How to Add Weight to the Camera Camera Tutorial|How to Add "Weight" to the Camera [Camera Tutorial]])
- 2021-07-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-add-weight-to-the-camera-camera-tutorial/1331996) Disable camera weight in first person by switching CameraSubject back to the Humanoid when the camera is within 1 stud of the head; weight otherwise blocks the screen. ([[2021-07-06 - How to Add Weight to the Camera Camera Tutorial|How to Add "Weight" to the Camera [Camera Tutorial]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Build contrast/saturation sliders with normal in the centre, lowest on the far left and highest on the far right, so lower contrast is available as well as higher. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Provide brightness sliders; dark scenes hide content for low-vision players while bright ones cause eyestrain or seizures. Don't rely on a single default light source. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Use Adobe's Color Accessibility tool to pick UI palettes safe for every colour-blindness type, so colourblind players don't need a special mode. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Offer a colour-lock style option that limits team/ink colours to maximally contrasting pairs, as Splatoon does, for colourblind players to tell allies from enemies. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Add toggles to disable camera shake, view bobbing, particles (minimal/decreased/all) and animation; reduce overstimulation, motion sickness and seizure risk, and lag. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Add a health/epilepsy warning before play if you cannot remove strobing or camera-shake effects, and avoid strobing lights where possible. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Provide size adjustment for cursor, text, chat box and GUI elements (scale, width, height) so low-vision and dyslexic players can read and track them. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Support customisable keybinds; ergonomic and RSI players often need non-default layouts, and Roblox does not yet provide this out of the box. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Offer toggle alternatives to held buttons (toggle sprint, crouch, fly, emotes) and avoid requiring holding multiple keys at once or repeated presses. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-08-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simplest-way-to-make-your-gui-fit-to-all-devices/1413727) Set a GUI element's AnchorPoint to 0.5,0.5 before rescaling so it stays centred; drag it back to its original position afterward. ([[2021-08-14 - Simplest Way To Make Your GUI Fit To All Devices|Simplest Way To Make Your GUI Fit To All Devices]])
- 2021-08-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simplest-way-to-make-your-gui-fit-to-all-devices/1413727) Use a scaling plugin to rescale both Position and Size of selected UI so it fits different device resolutions. ([[2021-08-14 - Simplest Way To Make Your GUI Fit To All Devices|Simplest Way To Make Your GUI Fit To All Devices]])
- 2021-08-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/simplest-way-to-make-your-gui-fit-to-all-devices/1413727) Add UIAspectRatioConstraint to keep a frame's aspect ratio unchanged across devices; example 2:1 frame became 131x65 instead of distorted 131x90 on iPhone 4S. ([[2021-08-14 - Simplest Way To Make Your GUI Fit To All Devices|Simplest Way To Make Your GUI Fit To All Devices]])
- 2021-08-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491) Don't stretch text inputs to fill the full screen width; forms don't need to be 100% wide. ([[2021-08-15 - 5 Tricks to improve your UIs|5 Tricks to improve your UIs]])
- 2021-08-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491) Use visible labels instead of placeholders; placeholders vanish while typing and hurt accessibility, though they're fine in some cases. ([[2021-08-15 - 5 Tricks to improve your UIs|5 Tricks to improve your UIs]])
- 2021-08-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491) Reduce the number of required form fields so users can finish forms correctly on the first try. ([[2021-08-15 - 5 Tricks to improve your UIs|5 Tricks to improve your UIs]])
- 2021-08-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491) Pre-fill form data when known, such as the player's username, so users don't type it manually. ([[2021-08-15 - 5 Tricks to improve your UIs|5 Tricks to improve your UIs]])
- 2021-08-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491) Limit the characters an input accepts to prevent errors before submission. ([[2021-08-15 - 5 Tricks to improve your UIs|5 Tricks to improve your UIs]])
- 2021-08-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491) Remove unnecessary borders; UIs look cleaner without them. Solo developers without a designer tend to overuse borders. ([[2021-08-15 - 5 Tricks to improve your UIs|5 Tricks to improve your UIs]])
- 2021-08-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/5-tricks-to-improve-your-uis/1416491) Increase clickable area: bigger targets are easier to hit. For small controls, add an invisible clickable area around them. ([[2021-08-15 - 5 Tricks to improve your UIs|5 Tricks to improve your UIs]])
- 2021-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805) Hide unimportant text and secondary info behind buttons, links, or menus so the main UI stays uncluttered and easier to navigate. ([[2021-09-05 - UI Design and the Fundamentals|UI Design and the Fundamentals]])
- 2021-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805) Keep fonts, colors, spacing, and icon styles consistent throughout the interface; consistent UI is received better and appears more polished. ([[2021-09-05 - UI Design and the Fundamentals|UI Design and the Fundamentals]])
- 2021-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805) Structure element hierarchy so key information is prominent and minor items are de-emphasized, preventing players from missing information. ([[2021-09-05 - UI Design and the Fundamentals|UI Design and the Fundamentals]])
- 2021-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805) Use text weight and size to establish type hierarchy, ordering text by importance so the most important reads first. ([[2021-09-05 - UI Design and the Fundamentals|UI Design and the Fundamentals]])
- 2021-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805) Build contrast using value and saturation differences rather than just complementary hues; complementary colors alone can look poor together. ([[2021-09-05 - UI Design and the Fundamentals|UI Design and the Fundamentals]])
- 2021-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805) Ensure sufficient contrast for accessibility, since color vision deficiency affects many players. ([[2021-09-05 - UI Design and the Fundamentals|UI Design and the Fundamentals]])
- 2021-09-05 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/ui-design-and-the-fundamentals/1455805) Avoid pure white text on black backgrounds and pure black text on white backgrounds to reduce eye strain; use softer tones like dark gray. ([[2021-09-05 - UI Design and the Fundamentals|UI Design and the Fundamentals]])
- 2021-12-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917) Lock the camera to first person only at the end of development; test in third person during building for convenience. ([[2021-12-10 - How to Make a Terrifying Horror Game|How to Make a Terrifying Horror Game]])
- 2021-12-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-terrifying-horror-game/1583917) Add flavor text to items and make them more interactable, but keep monologue short so it does not become annoying. ([[2021-12-10 - How to Make a Terrifying Horror Game|How to Make a Terrifying Horror Game]])
- 2021-12-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-of-how-to-improve-your-buildings-long/1606301) Lighting filters (Sun rays, Color Correction, Bloom, Depth of Field) add mood; Color Correction is widely used in tycoons and simulators to make colors more vivid. ([[2021-12-29 - Guide of how to improve your buildings! (Long)|Guide of how to improve your buildings! (Long)]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083) Use UICorner on frames and images (radius around 0,15) to avoid harsh corners; the author says rounded edges make the UI easier on the eyes. ([[2022-04-27 - How to make a clean, appealing UI|How to make a clean, appealing UI]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083) Add a white UIStroke (thickness 3, ApplyStrokeMode Contextual) to the main frame to define its edges against the game background. ([[2022-04-27 - How to make a clean, appealing UI|How to make a clean, appealing UI]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083) Use Interface Tools plugin to build UI quickly, and keep layouts simple and minimalistic for a clean look. ([[2022-04-27 - How to make a clean, appealing UI|How to make a clean, appealing UI]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083) Place the title bar label at the top-middle, hanging slightly over the frame edge, so the UI's purpose is clear at a glance. ([[2022-04-27 - How to make a clean, appealing UI|How to make a clean, appealing UI]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083) Build a close button as a small red TextLabel at the top-right with a UICorner (radius 0,8) and a white border stroke. ([[2022-04-27 - How to make a clean, appealing UI|How to make a clean, appealing UI]])
- 2022-04-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-a-clean-appealing-ui/1770083) Tint a background image with a color (29,29,209) and transparency 0.7 to add subtle color without clutter. ([[2022-04-27 - How to make a clean, appealing UI|How to make a clean, appealing UI]])
- 2023-03-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571) Add quality-of-life options: progress saving, subtitles, objective/puzzle hints, skippable cutscenes, brightness sliders, toggleable crouch/sprint. ([[2023-03-09 - Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences|Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences]])
- 2023-04-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-create-a-custom-chat-tag/2302562) Chat tags can be customised by editing the colored PrefixText string in the TextChatService.OnIncomingMessage callback; the script is meant to be modified. ([[2023-04-18 - How to Create a Custom Chat Tag|How to Create a Custom Chat Tag]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Keep one constant color scheme across the UI; pick palettes with a color tool such as Adobe Color rather than ad hoc choices. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Make primary actions visually distinct from secondary ones: give the primary button a background color and remove the background from Cancel, so users don't misclick. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Place key navigation (e.g. Log In / Sign Up) in consistent, predictable locations so users never have to search for them. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Avoid over-expressive display fonts for primary text; use a clean font family (author recommends Gotham and its weights) throughout. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Check text contrast with a contrast checker (e.g. Coolors contrast checker); avoid low-contrast pairs like yellow on white. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Use flat, one-color vector icons (e.g. Google Material icon set, Font Awesome) instead of large multicolor icons. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Use gradients sparingly: as subtle shading, on highlighted text, or matched to a stroke on the primary button, never on all text. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Reduce clutter by using collapsible menus and dividers; for black backgrounds use slightly lighter dividers, for white backgrounds slightly darker ones. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Add depth with shadows: UIStroke with a transparency NumberSequence from 0 to 1 linearly, thickness ~2, black; or duplicated offset frames behind with lower ZIndex; or a drop-shadow texture placed behind the frame. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Use a subtle stroke on containers, slightly darker than the fill and partly transparent, to separate elements. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-make-extremely-clean-ui-while-keeping-good-ux/2438099) Use blur behind top bars or panels rather than a solid background for a cleaner look; a stronger blur often looks better. ([[2023-06-25 - How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good UX|How to make extremely Clean UI while keeping good ]])
- 2023-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/all-about-optimization/2586333) Offer graphics presets (High/Medium/Low/Very low) that remove shadows, fog, clouds and effects for weaker devices. ([[2023-09-09 - All about optimization|All about optimization]])
- 2023-11-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-lua-programming-formalities-guide/2672585) Route all UI open/close through a single visibility manager keyed by screen position so overlapping frames close automatically; distinguish temporary frames from default HUD frames. ([[2023-11-02 - Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide|Roblox Lua Programming Formalities Guide]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/viewportframe-masking/2964839) For image masks on a 0-alpha mesh, set SurfaceAppearance AlphaMode to Transparency; it supports only 2 or 5 transparency levels, which causes banding. ([[2024-05-10 - ViewportFrame Masking|ViewportFrame Masking]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543) Use built-in Roblox UI tools for simple static UIs; adopt React (react-lua) when UI complexity grows and needs structure. ([[2024-05-10 - How To React + Roblox|How To: React + Roblox]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543) Always pass an explicit dependency array to useEffect; omitting it reruns the effect every render and can cause infinite loops. ([[2024-05-10 - How To React + Roblox|How To: React + Roblox]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543) Clean up connections (e.g. RunService.Heartbeat, UserInputService signals) by returning a cleanup function from useEffect that calls Disconnect. ([[2024-05-10 - How To React + Roblox|How To: React + Roblox]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543) Style Roblox GUI by adding child objects like UIListLayout, UICorner, UIPadding and UIStroke inside the element's children table. ([[2024-05-10 - How To React + Roblox|How To: React + Roblox]])
- 2024-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-react-roblox/2964543) Treat useRef access to underlying GUIObjects as a last resort; it breaks unidirectional data flow and makes code harder to reason about. ([[2024-05-10 - How To React + Roblox|How To: React + Roblox]])
- 2025-03-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/text-custom-fonts-advanced-control/3521684) Use Text+ scaling instead of TextScaled when you need text to fit identically across devices; the library handles size differences more precisely. ([[2025-03-02 - Text+ Custom fonts & advanced control|Text+ | Custom fonts & advanced control]])
- 2026-01-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/completing-the-dynamic-head-migration/4301387) Consolidate separate Classic Heads and Classic Faces UI categories into a single unified Heads category. ([[2026-01-27 - Completing the Dynamic Head Migration|Completing the Dynamic Head Migration]])
- 2026-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904) Shift Lock and Sprint both bind to Left Shift today, causing conflicts; test input defaults carefully and give feedback on cross-platform keybinds. ([[2026-02-18 - Studio Beta AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-Based Character Control|[Studio Beta] AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-B]])
- 2026-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267) Keep custom UI out of roughly 60px from each screen side; default touch movement and jump buttons are moving into those zones. ([[2026-04-08 - Full Release The Future of Character Movement Character Controller Lib|[Full Release] The Future of Character Movement: C]])
- 2026-04-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-the-future-of-character-movement-character-controller-library/4565267) Check the new touch button placement in Studio Emulator or on a physical device for overlaps with custom UI after the April 30 update. ([[2026-04-08 - Full Release The Future of Character Movement Character Controller Lib|[Full Release] The Future of Character Movement: C]])
- 2026-05-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263) Use per-corner UICorner radii (TopLeftRadius etc.) for speech bubbles, tab headers and one-sided callouts instead of image assets or 9-slice workarounds. ([[2026-05-14 - Full Release New UI Capabilities Shadows & Individual Corners!|[Full Release] New UI Capabilities: Shadows & Indi]])
- 2026-05-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263) Use UIShadow for depth on buttons and glows; it does not affect layout or trigger recalculations, and multiple UIShadows can be stacked on one element. ([[2026-05-14 - Full Release New UI Capabilities Shadows & Individual Corners!|[Full Release] New UI Capabilities: Shadows & Indi]])
- 2026-05-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263) For TextLabel/TextBox/TextButton, UIShadow applies to the background rectangle, not the text; native text shadows are not available.
