([[2026-05-14 - Full Release New UI Capabilities Shadows & Individual Corners!|[Full Release] New UI Capabilities: Shadows & Indi]])
- 2026-05-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263) Avoid setting both CornerRadius and individual corner radii when styling a UICorner; it produces unexpected behaviour. Pick one approach. ([[2026-05-14 - Full Release New UI Capabilities Shadows & Individual Corners!|[Full Release] New UI Capabilities: Shadows & Indi]])
- 2026-05-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263) Setting CornerRadius overwrites all per-corner properties; set CornerRadius first, then override individual corners in Properties. ([[2026-05-14 - Full Release New UI Capabilities Shadows & Individual Corners!|[Full Release] New UI Capabilities: Shadows & Indi]])
- 2026-05-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263) Shadows may look jagged on elements with large corner radius; a known issue under investigation. ([[2026-05-14 - Full Release New UI Capabilities Shadows & Individual Corners!|[Full Release] New UI Capabilities: Shadows & Indi]])
- 2026-05-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263) Use low BlurRadius for hard edges and high values (up to 1000) for soft diffuse glows; scale values are a percentage of the parent's shortest dimension. ([[2026-05-14 - Full Release New UI Capabilities Shadows & Individual Corners!|[Full Release] New UI Capabilities: Shadows & Indi]])
- 2026-06-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/sling-a-modern-and-reactive-backpack-ui/4686535) Use Sling to replace the default backpack and group inventory into tab categories (Weapons, Potions, Resources) with declarative predicate functions. ([[2026-06-17 - Sling - A modern and reactive backpack UI|Sling - A modern and reactive backpack UI]])
- 2026-06-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/sling-a-modern-and-reactive-backpack-ui/4686535) Customize theme properties or switch between built-in default and light themes rather than building a backpack UI from scratch. ([[2026-06-17 - Sling - A modern and reactive backpack UI|Sling - A modern and reactive backpack UI]])
- 2026-06-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/sling-a-modern-and-reactive-backpack-ui/4686535) Programmatically set which items sit in the hotbar and track when players rearrange hotbar items. ([[2026-06-17 - Sling - A modern and reactive backpack UI|Sling - A modern and reactive backpack UI]])
- 2026-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-shop-a-personalized-in-game-storefront/4701505) Call MarketplaceService:OpenShop(player) from custom buttons, NPCs or trigger zones to open Shop while keeping your own UI design; requires PromptExternalPurchase capability. ([[2026-06-24 - Introducing Shop A Personalized In-Game Storefront|Introducing Shop: A Personalized In-Game Storefron]])
- 2026-06-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-shop-a-personalized-in-game-storefront/4701505) To hide the Shop button in the hamburger menu, call StarterGui:SetCoreGuiEnabled(Enum.CoreGuiType.ExperienceShop, false) from a client script. ([[2026-06-24 - Introducing Shop A Personalized In-Game Storefront|Introducing Shop: A Personalized In-Game Storefron]])

## [[sound-and-music]]
- 2019-04-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895) Play a tick sound effect as each crate passes the centre line to add feedback during the spin. ([[2019-04-01 - Creating a CrateSpin System|Creating a Crate/Spin System]])
- 2021-05-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/designing-an-fps-framework-beginner%E2%80%99s-guide-part-2/1203507) Clone gun sounds and parent them to workspace on fire so the sound has a world origin, and set PlayOnRemove true so clones clean up. ([[2021-05-04 - Designing an FPS Framework Beginner’s guide PART 2|Designing an FPS Framework: Beginner’s guide [PART]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Offer individual volume sliders per sound category (ambience, music, weapons, footsteps, enemies) rather than only a master volume. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Add mute buttons for specific sounds, since players with noise sensitivity or trauma need to fully silence triggers, not just lower them. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Make sounds distinct per object (e.g. straw vs glass drinks) so players can identify items by audio, which also helps blind players. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2021-07-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/making-accessible-games-for-the-disability-aware-era-a-not-fully-comprehensive-guide-by-an-actual-disabled-developer/1375919) Consider directional audio captions that indicate what sound is playing and whether it comes from left or right, since captions are mostly prewritten in games. ([[2021-07-27 - Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware Era - A Not-Fully-Com|Making Accessible Games for the Disability-Aware E]])
- 2022-03-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697) Replace flagged audio with audio uploaded by your own user or group, or with Roblox licensed music and SFX catalog tracks. ([[2022-03-09 - Action Needed Upcoming Changes to Asset Privacy for Audio|[Action Needed] Upcoming Changes to Asset Privacy ]])
- 2022-03-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/action-needed-upcoming-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1701697) For radios or boomboxes, switch from ID-based playback to letting users pick from the Roblox licensed music catalog to avoid breakage. ([[2022-03-09 - Action Needed Upcoming Changes to Asset Privacy for Audio|[Action Needed] Upcoming Changes to Asset Privacy ]])
- 2022-03-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/update-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1715717) Replace privately-restricted music with public or Pro Sound Effects library assets; search '[name] (SFX)' to find them. ([[2022-03-16 - Update Changes to Asset Privacy for Audio|[Update] Changes to Asset Privacy for Audio]])
- 2022-03-16 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/update-changes-to-asset-privacy-for-audio/1715717) Plan audio uploads around monthly limits: 10 uploads per month by default, 100 with ID verification. ([[2022-03-16 - Update Changes to Asset Privacy for Audio|[Update] Changes to Asset Privacy for Audio]])
- 2023-03-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571) Invest in subtle ambient sound (creaks, humming, pipes, breathing, heartbeat); Roblox games are often lacking here. ([[2023-03-09 - Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences|Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences]])
- 2023-03-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-making-better-horror-experiences/2212571) Custom soundtracks help identity, but Roblox APM music or free music is an acceptable substitute. ([[2023-03-09 - Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences|Guide to Making Better Horror Experiences]])
- 2024-08-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-custom-skyboxes-using-blender-crafting-stunning-atmospheres/3134324) Clouds must be inserted directly under Workspace.Terrain; they do not work elsewhere in the hierarchy. ([[2024-08-25 - Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting stunning atmospheres|Creating custom skyboxes using Blender & crafting ]])

## [[optimization-and-streaming]]
- 2018-09-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-development-resources-mega-thread/177780) Read 'The Basics of Basic Optimization' and the efficiency thread; compress PNGs with Save for Roblox before uploading to reduce size. ([[2018-09-09 - Game Development Resources MEGA THREAD|Game Development Resources [MEGA THREAD]]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) Use the Performance/Memory stats widget and Developer Console to measure memory in Studio and in-game before deciding what to optimise. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) For unions in detailed areas, set CollisionFidelity to Hull or Box, but only for parts the player will not touch; otherwise optimise or swap to parts/meshes. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) Monitor Terrain Physics and Voxels in the Performance widget; hollowing terrain reduces memory, but the hollow tool alone can add excess detail (about 3MB voxels and 5MB physics in the author's test). ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-02-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/building-optimisation-tips-and-tricks/235059) Never put decals or textures on fully transparent objects; this causes mobile performance problems per Zeuxcg. ([[2019-02-02 - Building Optimisation Tips and tricks!|Building Optimisation | Tips and tricks!]])
- 2019-02-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avoiding-wait-and-why/244015) wait() can overshoot by seconds and grows worse the more it is called, causing laggy gameplay; avoid it for performance. ([[2019-02-23 - Avoiding wait() and why|Avoiding wait() and why]])
- 2019-04-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895) Pre-create crate UI clones once when the player joins and toggle their contents per spin rather than regenerating them each spin. ([[2019-04-01 - Creating a CrateSpin System|Creating a Crate/Spin System]])
- 2019-04-01 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-cratespin-system/260895) Do not update each crate's position every frame in a loop; animate only the holder frame with TweenService and an easing style like Quart. ([[2019-04-01 - Creating a CrateSpin System|Creating a Crate/Spin System]])
- 2019-10-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/garbage-collection-and-memory-leaks-in-roblox-what-you-should-know/374954) Use collectgarbage("count") to read Luau memory in KB (multiply by 1024 for bytes); forced collection is not available on Roblox. ([[2019-10-24 - Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - What you should know|Garbage Collection and Memory Leaks in Roblox - Wh]])
- 2019-11-17 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/how-to-optimize-humanoids/389801) Disable humanoid states you don't need (e.g. climbing, swimming) and enable them on the fly only when required. ([[2019-11-17 - How to Optimize Humanoids|How to Optimize Humanoids]])
- 2019-12-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/large-scale-roblox-terrain-the-ultimate-guide/405672) Expect heavy RAM use: 16 GB recommended, 8 GB minimum; Gaea uses under 10% of a GTX 1080 Ti for slower tasks. ([[2019-12-11 - Large-Scale Roblox Terrain The ultimate guide|Large-Scale Roblox Terrain: The ultimate guide]])
- 2020-02-07 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/consume-everything-how-greedy-meshing-works/452717) Greedy-mesh voxel builds by merging adjacent blocks into larger cuboids; this cuts part count heavily while the visuals stay the same, reducing lag from large part counts. ([[2020-02-07 - Consume everything - how greedy meshing works|Consume everything - how greedy meshing works]])
- 2020-06-27 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-a-simple-realistic-camera-with-view-bobbing-dynamic-blur-sprinting/646943) Use RenderStepped with a modulus counter (e.g. every 5th frame) for camera checks, and avoid math.sqrt by comparing dot products of vectors. ([[2020-06-27 - Creating a Simple, Realistic Camera (With View Bobbing, Dynamic Blur,|Creating a Simple, Realistic Camera (With View Bob]])
- 2020-10-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/roblox-scripting-roadmap-learning-resource-list/808430) Read the Improving Performance and Fighting Against Lag docs and use the MicroProfiler and Script Analysis Tool to find bottlenecks. ([[2020-10-06 - Roblox Scripting Roadmap & Learning Resource List|Roblox Scripting Roadmap & Learning Resource List]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Reduce instance count; avoid converting whole data tables into Folders and Value objects. Use RemoteFunctions or compact strings instead. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Consider splitting heavy content across multiple places with TeleportService to reduce instances, maps, meshes and running scripts. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Lighting choice matters for low-end users; ShadowMap and Future are the most expensive options. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Check memory by joining a similar game and pressing F9; use it as a benchmark for your own target. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Offer a low-graphics option: locally switch materials to SmoothPlastic, disable CastShadow, and use cheaper lighting. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-considerations-improvements-and-optimizations-when-making-a-game/817499) Avoid unnecessary unique assets (images, meshes, textures, animations, sounds); keep sound music loaded only when needed. ([[2020-10-12 - Performance considerations, improvements, and optimizations when makin|Performance considerations, improvements, and opti]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lighting-tutorial-how-to-use-lighting/817013) Excessive lighting settings and effects can hurt performance on low-end devices; use them in moderation. ([[2020-10-12 - Lighting Tutorial - How to use Lighting|Lighting Tutorial - How to use Lighting]])
- 2020-10-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lighting-tutorial-how-to-use-lighting/817013) Keep post-processing effects inside the Lighting service; they only work there and must have Enabled set to true. ([[2020-10-12 - Lighting Tutorial - How to use Lighting|Lighting Tutorial - How to use Lighting]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Enable Streaming Enabled and code for its limitations; the author says this is the path to top performance for low-end users. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Disable ShadowCast on parts except key buildings that need shadows to reduce shadows the client renders. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Reduce mesh count and polycount; Roblox does not compress meshes, so a 1 MB mesh costs 1 MB in memory. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Compress audio to OGG; it has the lowest memory cost of supported formats. Limit how much audio plays at once. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Shrink textures (e.g. 1024x1024 to 512x512) and use JPEG instead of PNG; many uncompressed textures can exceed 200 MB. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Set Mesh RenderFidelity to Performance on anything not key to the experience, such as distant islands. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Set Mesh CollisionFidelity to Box for complex meshes like crates to cut physics collision work. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Disable CanCollide on parts that are touched but do not need collision, so they are not physics-checked. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Avoid unnecessary lighting effects such as blur; they cost performance and players on quality 10 see them. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])
- 2021-01-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/improving-game-performance-benchmarking-microprofiler-developer-stats-and-developer-console/1002074) Use the Developer Console memory view to find which client-side parts of the place use the most memory, such as overly many textures. ([[2021-01-23 - Improving Game Performance Benchmarking, Microprofiler, Developer Stat|Improving Game Performance: Benchmarking, Micropro]])

Processing one big packet costs far less CPU, memory and network overhead than many small ones. - 2021-02-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/network-optimization-2021-preventing-high-latency-reducing-lag/1046078) Coalesce work into larger chunks, but not so large that it causes stutter. - 2021-03-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419) Set CanCollide off and Collision Fidelity to Box on plants, grass, vines, mushrooms, and tree leaves so players don't need collision calculations for tiny details. - 2021-03-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419) Use Box collision fidelity for small or out-of-reach items; Default or Hull only for large items players walk on or bump into, like rocks, walls, roofs, stairs, and crates. - 2021-03-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419) Hull collision fidelity is the most resource-heavy; Box is quickest; Default is a mix of both. ([[2021-03-06 - Lag reducing tips and Organzation tips!|Lag reducing tips and Organzation tips!]])
- 2021-03-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419) Disable Cast Shadow on items that don't need it, such as custom grass, unseen items, and items in pitch black when using FIB3 dynamic lighting. - 2021-03-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419) Spread out light sources and avoid stacking many lights close together; each shadow costs render resources, especially from detailed meshes. - 2021-03-06 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/lag-reducing-tips-and-organzation-tips/1087419) Delete hidden or unseen parts: assets under terrain, behind walls, outside map view, duplicated copies, and stray parts out of reach. - 2021-06-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/meshpart-usage-performance-optimizations/1319217) Automatic degrades triangles with distance (e.g. 3,000 tris stay full under 250 studs, halved at 250-500 studs). - 2021-06-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/meshpart-usage-performance-optimizations/1319217) Choose CollisionFidelity by cost: Box is cheapest, then Hull, then Default, with PreciseConvexDecomposition most costly and rarely justified. - 2025-01-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345) Set MeshParts to Performance render fidelity and Unions to Automatic via command-bar scripts run separately. ([[2025-01-08 - The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize Your Game Using Just|The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize ]])
- 2025-01-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345) Hollow out terrain with Subtract and remove hidden terrain that players never see; keep voxel count under 100 million. ([[2025-01-08 - The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize Your Game Using Just|The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize ]])
- 2025-01-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345) Keep parts (including MeshParts) around 75k so mobile devices can manage them. ([[2025-01-08 - The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize Your Game Using Just|The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize ]])
- 2025-01-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345) Avoid flat terrain surfaces where possible; the author reports FPS gains of 15 to up to 48 in team tests. ([[2025-01-08 - The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize Your Game Using Just|The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize ]])
- 2025-01-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345) Avoid bulk regular water; use a toolbox infinite water mesh for ocean scenes. ([[2025-01-08 - The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize Your Game Using Just|The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize ]])
- 2025-01-08 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-hitchhikers-guide-to-optimization-optimize-your-game-using-just-one-post-instead-of-many/3358345) Keep CanTouch and CanQuery off unless needed; they cause lag when many build up. CanQuery only appears when CanCollide is off. ([[2025-01-08 - The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize Your Game Using Just|The Hitchhiker's Guide To Optimization - Optimize ]])
- 2025-03-02 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/text-custom-fonts-advanced-control/3521684) Text+ is marketed as ultra-optimized and lag-free; evaluate it against TextScaled if text rendering causes performance issues in heavy UI. ([[2025-03-02 - Text+ Custom fonts & advanced control|Text+ | Custom fonts & advanced control]])
- 2025-04-04 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/netray-high-performance-roblox-networking-library/3592849) Batch multiple small remote events into fewer network sends to reduce network load and improve responsiveness. ([[2025-04-04 - NetRay - High-Performance Roblox Networking Library|NetRay - High-Performance Roblox Networking Librar]])
- 2025-08-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566) Write whole pixels with buffer.writeu32 packing RGBA into one u32; it is about 4x faster than writing four u8 values separately. ([[2025-08-03 - A complete guide to EditableImages|A complete guide to EditableImages]])
- 2025-08-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566) Draw rectangles and textures by copying whole rows with buffer.copy instead of setting pixels one at a time; this significantly reduces draw operations. ([[2025-08-03 - A complete guide to EditableImages|A complete guide to EditableImages]])
- 2025-08-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/a-complete-guide-to-editableimages/3858566) Free texture buffers by clearing references (lazy load, then free) so the garbage collector can reclaim memory, important on memory-constrained devices. ([[2025-08-03 - A complete guide to EditableImages|A complete guide to EditableImages]])
- 2025-09-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/luau-bytecode-explained-how-to-read-debug-and-optimize-like-a-hacker/3941941) Watch register pressure: Luau has roughly 200 usable registers per function, and too many locals or temporaries in one function can cause an internal compiler error. ([[2025-09-18 - Luau Bytecode EXPLAINED - How to Read, Debug, and Optimize Like a Hack|Luau Bytecode EXPLAINED - How to Read, Debug, and ]])
- 2025-11-03 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/launching-even-more-server-cpu/4044040) If you notice negative server performance after the CPU change, report it in the post comments; Roblox recommends consulting its performance optimization best practices docs. ([[2025-11-03 - Launching even more Server CPU!|Launching even more Server CPU!]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157) Keep Instance Streaming enabled; Server Authority relies on the client simulating only locally loaded content and streaming also reduces join time and memory. ([[2025-12-09 - Studio Beta Build Fair, Responsive Games with Server Authority|[Studio Beta] Build Fair, Responsive Games with Se]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-build-fair-responsive-games-with-server-authority/4139157) Expect higher server costs and extra client work for prediction and resimulation; budget for it, as optimization is ongoing. ([[2025-12-09 - Studio Beta Build Fair, Responsive Games with Server Authority|[Studio Beta] Build Fair, Responsive Games with Se]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-how-to-begin/4139185) Use SetPredictionMode On only for critical instances; overuse hurts low-end devices. Automatic (default) is suitable for most BaseParts. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority How to Begin|Server Authority: How to Begin?]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-how-to-begin/4139185) Use SetPredictionMode Off for instances that don't need client prediction; they are then server-owned with no client prediction. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority How to Begin|Server Authority: How to Begin?]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321) Enable StreamingEnabled: NextGenerationReplication needs it for scalability and RunService:setPredictionMode depends on its atomic grouping. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engineers!|Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engin]])
- 2025-12-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/server-authority-studio-beta-insights-from-engineers/4139321) Server performance with 20+ player games is still weak in this beta; expect significant improvement before launch. ([[2025-12-09 - Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engineers!|Server Authority Studio Beta - Insights from Engin]])
- 2025-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855) Stay judicious with texture memory on unsupported instances such as ParticleEmitter, since texture streaming does not yet manage them. ([[2025-12-12 - Introducing Texture Streaming|Introducing Texture Streaming]])
- 2025-12-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-texture-streaming/4144855) No creator action is needed to enable texture streaming; it rolls out automatically per platform. ([[2025-12-12 - Introducing Texture Streaming|Introducing Texture Streaming]])
- 2025-12-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/archived-rewind-server-authoritative-hit-validation-custom-character-replication/4160622) Tune snapshotRate and maxRewindTime to your game's needs; the ring buffer keeps memory fixed. Stated overhead is under 0.5ms per validation on a mid-range server. ([[2025-12-18 - Archived Rewind - Server-Authoritative Hit Validation & Custom Charact|[Archived] Rewind - Server-Authoritative Hit Valid]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) Keep total replication under Roblox's ~50 KB/s throttle; above it, humanoids replicate slowly, laggily or teleport. Budget per-player unit counts accordingly. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) Use arrays instead of dictionaries for sent data; string keys add extra bytes per packet with no benefit. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) Compress CFrames: send position as Vector3int16 (~6 bytes) instead of CFrame (~20 bytes), or Vector2int16 (X,Z) if the client derives height from a heightmap. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) Send only the Y rotation of NPCs and pack it into a 2-byte integer with the buffer library (buffer.writei16) instead of an 8-byte float. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) Use workspace:BulkMoveTo with Enum.BulkMoveMode.FireCFrameChanged to move many NPC parts in one batch instead of looping over CFrame sets. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) Throttle rendering when NPC counts reach 1000+: render 500 per frame alternating (~50% workload cut) or 250 per frame (~75% cut), accepting slight choppiness, as a last resort. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2025-12-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/performance-tips-network-bandwidth-client-framerate-etc-for-custom-npc-replication/4202236) Try @native on server compression code, but verify with the ScriptProfiler since performance gains are not guaranteed. ([[2025-12-31 - Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framerate, etc.) for custo|Performance tips (network bandwidth, client framer]])
- 2026-01-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/avatar-joint-upgrade-for-physically-simulated-character-movement-is-now-live/4298561) The base joint upgrade has little to no performance impact, per the FAQ. ([[2026-01-26 - Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Character Movement is No|Avatar Joint Upgrade for Physically-Simulated Char]])
- 2026-02-18 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-avatarabilities-library-for-a-luau-based-character-controller/4403904) Controller work runs only when state changes, giving near zero-cost idle; avoid custom server-side NPC movement solutions where possible. ([[2026-02-18 - Studio Beta AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-Based Character Control|[Studio Beta] AvatarAbilities Library for a Luau-B]])
- 2026-03-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374) Keep watertight meshes under 20k triangles; simplification above that likely makes the mesh non-solid. ([[2026-03-12 - Studio Beta Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragment and Sweep APIs|[Studio Beta] Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragm]])
- 2026-03-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374) Be judicious with fragment counts: each fragment becomes a simulated physics instance. Use Debris service to clean up unneeded pieces. ([[2026-03-12 - Studio Beta Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragment and Sweep APIs|[Studio Beta] Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragm]])
- 2026-03-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374) Filter out very close CFrames from mouse/touch input before SweepPartAsync, since many CFrames create very large triangle counts. ([[2026-03-12 - Studio Beta Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragment and Sweep APIs|[Studio Beta] Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragm]])
- 2026-03-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374) Be aware CSG-created MeshParts keep a copy of mesh data in memory, giving a higher footprint than standard assets. ([[2026-03-12 - Studio Beta Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragment and Sweep APIs|[Studio Beta] Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragm]])
- 2026-03-12 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/studio-beta-solid-modeling-on-meshes-new-fragment-and-sweep-apis/4515374) The optional MeshPreloader (no-op CSG) cuts first-operation latency but costs memory; enable only if latency matters. ([[2026-03-12 - Studio Beta Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragment and Sweep APIs|[Studio Beta] Solid Modeling on Meshes & new Fragm]])
- 2026-03-24 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/voice-chat-is-now-supported-in-servers-of-up-to-100-users/4534031) Before scaling past 50 users, estimate the added cost of extra avatars, player-placed tools and structures. Confirm the experience has headroom for 50 more avatars. ([[2026-03-24 - Voice Chat is now supported in servers of up to 100 users!|Voice Chat is now supported in servers of up to 10]])
- 2026-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258) Test on low-end devices (2014-2020 laptops/phones), not just a powerful dev PC, since performance problems lose players and revenue. ([[2026-05-10 - The definitive tutorial to game optimization everything you need to kn|The definitive tutorial to game optimization: ever]])
- 2026-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258) Use MicroProfiler (Ctrl+Alt+F6 / Cmd+Opt+F6) and freeze with Ctrl+P to find long frames; on mobile, browse to the IP:1338 it shows. ([[2026-05-10 - The definitive tutorial to game optimization everything you need to kn|The definitive tutorial to game optimization: ever]])
- 2026-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258) Target frame budgets: 16.67ms for 60 FPS, 33.33ms for 30 FPS; watch for spikes, not just average FPS. ([[2026-05-10 - The definitive tutorial to game optimization everything you need to kn|The definitive tutorial to game optimization: ever]])
- 2026-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258) Keep developer console (F9) memory under a reasonable limit; exceeding player RAM causes lag. Total Data KB/s around 80 KB/s is a suggested limit. ([[2026-05-10 - The definitive tutorial to game optimization everything you need to kn|The definitive tutorial to game optimization: ever]])
- 2026-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258) Only anchor-off where physics is needed; keep Anchored on for static objects since physics is expensive. ([[2026-05-10 - The definitive tutorial to game optimization everything you need to kn|The definitive tutorial to game optimization: ever]])
- 2026-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/the-definitive-tutorial-to-game-optimization-everything-you-need-to-know/4629258) Disable AcousticSimulationEnabled in SoundService unless the game needs realistic audio, since it hurts performance. ([[2026-05-10 - The definitive tutorial to game optimization everything you need to kn|The definitive tutorial to game optimization: ever]])
- 2026-05-14 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-new-ui-capabilities-shadows-individual-corners/4636263) Keep under about 100 UIShadows on-screen at once for best performance; UIShadow is consistently faster than 9-sliced ImageLabels. ([[2026-05-14 - Full Release New UI Capabilities Shadows & Individual Corners!|[Full Release] New UI Capabilities: Shadows & Indi]])
- 2026-06-25 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/early-access-program-unlocking-massive-terrain-with-up-to-64-custom-materials/4703282) Terrain now stores on the asset platform rather than the place file, so large terrain no longer pushes places toward the 100 MB publish limit. ([[2026-06-25 - Early Access Program Unlocking Massive Terrain With Up to 64 Custom Ma|[Early Access Program] Unlocking Massive Terrain W]])
- 2026-06-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/automated-performance-profiling-with-microprofiler-and-assistant/4704417) Open MicroProfiler with Ctrl+F6 (Cmd+F6 on Mac), and pause the capture (Ctrl+P) when you spot a frame time spike before asking for analysis. ([[2026-06-26 - Automated Performance Profiling with MicroProfiler and Assistant|Automated Performance Profiling with MicroProfiler]])
- 2026-06-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/automated-performance-profiling-with-microprofiler-and-assistant/4704417) Ask Assistant whether a spike is physics-bound or script-bound, and which category has the highest exclusive time, to narrow down bottlenecks quickly. ([[2026-06-26 - Automated Performance Profiling with MicroProfiler and Assistant|Automated Performance Profiling with MicroProfiler]])
- 2026-07-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993) Expect higher server utilization; impact is small unless you were already near the server CPU limit from heavy server-side physics. Extended Services for Compute can add per-player CPU. ([[2026-07-09 - Full Release Ship Fair And Competitive Games with Server Authority|[Full Release] Ship Fair And Competitive Games wit]])
- 2026-07-09 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/full-release-ship-fair-and-competitive-games-with-server-authority/4727993) Use the Instance Streaming Conversion Skill (experimental, via MCP) to convert a non-streaming game to streaming, which Server Authority requires. ([[2026-07-09 - Full Release Ship Fair And Competitive Games with Server Authority|[Full Release] Ship Fair And Competitive Games wit]])
- 2026-07-23 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/reference-instances-directly-with-attributes/4753441) Use handle:Get() for a non-yielding check and handle:Wait(timeout) when the target may stream in; Get() returns nil once a target streams out. ([[2026-07-23 - Reference Instances Directly with Attributes|Reference Instances Directly with Attributes]])
- 2026-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-predictive-streaming-reduced-pausing-and-smoother-gameplay/4764052) Enable Predictive Streaming by setting Workspace.PredictiveStreamingMode to Enabled; it requires no code changes but only applies if Instance Streaming is enabled. ([[2026-07-29 - Introducing Predictive Streaming Reduced Pausing and Smoother Gameplay|Introducing Predictive Streaming: Reduced Pausing ]])
- 2026-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-predictive-streaming-reduced-pausing-and-smoother-gameplay/4764052) Use manual prefetches or multiple replication foci for respawn/CFrame cases needing precise control; otherwise let predictive streaming handle them automatically. ([[2026-07-29 - Introducing Predictive Streaming Reduced Pausing and Smoother Gameplay|Introducing Predictive Streaming: Reduced Pausing ]])
- 2026-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-predictive-streaming-reduced-pausing-and-smoother-gameplay/4764052) Predictive streaming is additive and won't add a focus where you already created a prefetch or MRF, so manual setups can coexist safely. ([[2026-07-29 - Introducing Predictive Streaming Reduced Pausing and Smoother Gameplay|Introducing Predictive Streaming: Reduced Pausing ]])
- 2026-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-predictive-streaming-reduced-pausing-and-smoother-gameplay/4764052) Predictive streaming skips predictions on resource-constrained client devices, so test streaming behavior on low-end hardware rather than assuming it always runs. ([[2026-07-29 - Introducing Predictive Streaming Reduced Pausing and Smoother Gameplay|Introducing Predictive Streaming: Reduced Pausing ]])
- 2026-07-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/introducing-predictive-streaming-reduced-pausing-and-smoother-gameplay/4764052) Predictive streaming is opt-in and not planned to become required; adopt it only if it improves your game's streaming performance. ([[2026-07-29 - Introducing Predictive Streaming Reduced Pausing and Smoother Gameplay|Introducing Predictive Streaming: Reduced Pausing ]])
- 2026-09-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/terrain-updates-object-scattering-path-splines-projected-terrain-decals-and-more/4865617) Expect virtual texturing to cache generated terrain material tiles so they aren't recomputed every frame, improving performance on any device. ([[2026-09-11 - Terrain Updates Object Scattering, Path Splines, Projected Terrain Dec|Terrain Updates: Object Scattering, Path Splines, ]])
- 2026-09-11 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/rdc26-what-we-announced/4865880) Minimum draw distance rises to 500 studs (from 200) even on low-end Android at quality level 1 (late 2026). ([[2026-09-11 - RDC26 What We Announced|RDC26: What We Announced]])
- 2026-09-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/frustum-streaming-stream-what-your-players-see/4904553) Enable frustum streaming only where needed (scopes, racing, free camera); it is opt-in and increases client memory and possibly CPU usage. ([[2026-09-29 - Frustum Streaming Stream What Your Players See|Frustum Streaming: Stream What Your Players See]])
- 2026-09-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/frustum-streaming-stream-what-your-players-see/4904553) Use Automatic mode for most games; the engine enables frustum streaming based on FOV, high directional velocity, or far camera movement. ([[2026-09-29 - Frustum Streaming Stream What Your Players See|Frustum Streaming: Stream What Your Players See]])
- 2026-09-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/frustum-streaming-stream-what-your-players-see/4904553) Use manual Enabled/Disabled via server script for granular control, e.g. force-enable at start of scope equip animation so targets stream in before aiming. ([[2026-09-29 - Frustum Streaming Stream What Your Players See|Frustum Streaming: Stream What Your Players See]])
- 2026-09-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/frustum-streaming-stream-what-your-players-see/4904553) Frustum streaming is capped by device render distance; if render distance is below your streaming target radius, frustum streaming won't help. ([[2026-09-29 - Frustum Streaming Stream What Your Players See|Frustum Streaming: Stream What Your Players See]])
- 2026-09-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/frustum-streaming-stream-what-your-players-see/4904553) Keep client scripts compatible with streamed games; instances in view stream in, and with opportunistic behaviour they stream out shortly after looking away. ([[2026-09-29 - Frustum Streaming Stream What Your Players See|Frustum Streaming: Stream What Your Players See]])
- 2026-09-29 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/frustum-streaming-stream-what-your-players-see/4904553) Frustum streaming is not needed for non-streaming games, since all instances are already present on the device. ([[2026-09-29 - Frustum Streaming Stream What Your Players See|Frustum Streaming: Stream What Your Players See]])

## [[version-control-and-tooling]]
- 2018-08-31 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/guide-to-web-development/173780) Commit to Git with a message before risky changes so you can roll back in an emergency; use nodemon to auto-restart on save. ([[2018-08-31 - Guide to Web Development|Guide to Web Development]])
- 2020-05-10 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/game-design-theory-project-management-with-devops-analytics-and-pipelines-for-successful-games-and-productive-teams/568057) Track production with a project board (ClickUp, Trello or Jira) using pre-filled checklist cards per asset, with Completed and Live columns. ([[2020-05-10 - Game Design Theory Project Management with DevOps, Analytics and Pipel|Game Design Theory: Project Management with DevOps]])
- 2020-10-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/setup-rojo-fast-the-easy-way-to-setup-rojo-with-git-support/838182) Install GitHub Desktop, create a repo per game, and set VSCode as its default editor to commit and push changes easily. ([[2020-10-26 - Setup Rojo Fast The easy way to setup Rojo with Git support|Setup Rojo Fast: The easy way to setup Rojo with G]])
- 2020-10-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/setup-rojo-fast-the-easy-way-to-setup-rojo-with-git-support/838182) Install the Rojo VSCode extension, then use 'rojo welcome screen' > 'MANAGE IT FOR ME' so the extension manages the Studio plugin automatically. ([[2020-10-26 - Setup Rojo Fast The easy way to setup Rojo with Git support|Setup Rojo Fast: The easy way to setup Rojo with G]])
- 2020-10-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/setup-rojo-fast-the-easy-way-to-setup-rojo-with-git-support/838182) Start Rojo in VSCode before pressing Connect in Studio; if the plugin fails, check the Studio output. ([[2020-10-26 - Setup Rojo Fast The easy way to setup Rojo with Git support|Setup Rojo Fast: The easy way to setup Rojo with G]])
- 2020-10-26 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/setup-rojo-fast-the-easy-way-to-setup-rojo-with-git-support/838182) In default.project.json, map $path to a src folder under a dedicated child folder (e.g. GameFiles); mapping directly into a service deletes any non-Rojo instances there. ([[2020-10-26 - Setup Rojo Fast The easy way to setup Rojo with Git support|Setup Rojo Fast: The easy way to setup Rojo with G]])

- 2021-05-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-simple-vr-hands-from-scratch/1227966) Set Camera.CameraType to Scriptable and reset camera CFrame to its position to avoid a wrongly angled VR view. - 2021-05-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-simple-vr-hands-from-scratch/1227966) Disable VR controller models (SetCore VREnableControllerModels false) and laser pointer (VRLaserPointerMode 0) before replacing them with custom hand models. - 2021-05-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-simple-vr-hands-from-scratch/1227966) Update hand positions in UserInputService.UserCFrameChanged, which fires before render, to avoid visible lag between controller and hand models; multiply move by camera.CFrame. - 2021-05-15 [page](https://devforum.roblox.com/t/creating-simple-vr-hands-from-scratch/1227966) Anchor the HumanoidRootPart to stop the default character from moving or being visible while in VR. ([[2021-05-15 - Creating simple VR Hands from Scratch|Creating simple VR Hands from Scratch]])
